It should understand with an ever-growing power the best methods of developing, strengthening, and directing the child's faculties of body and mind, so that each generation, reaching maturity, would start clear of the last, and show a finer, fuller growth, both physically and mentally, than the preceding. That humanity does slowly improve is not here denied; but, granting our gradual improvement, is it all that we could make? And is the gain due to a commensurate improvement in motherhood?

===Chinese===
母亲的职责，首先是生养与她同等好或比她更好的孩子；把她身后先人的体格与品格，经她的经管而愈见完好地传下去；以她作为母亲的巨大力量，筑造并改良人类这个种族；造出更好的人。这一点做完之后，母亲的职责——人类母亲的职责——便在于这样教育她的孩子，以完成那生育与哺乳只是开了个头的工作。她把这孩子在身内怀九个月，在臂弯里抱两年，而只要她活着，便把他放在心头与头脑里。对幼者的教育，是人类生殖中一个极其重大的因素。正当的母职应当能够完美地履行这一伟大功能。它应当以不断增长的力量，通晓发展、强化并引导孩子身心官能的最好方法，使每一代人达到成熟时，都能不含混地从上一代那里起步，并在身体与心智两方面，都显出比前一代更精纯、更充分的生长。人类确实在缓慢改进，这一点在此并不否认；但即便承认我们这渐进的改进，它是我们所能做到的全部吗？而这增益，是由于母职方面与之相称的改进吗？

===Original===
To both we must say no. When we see how some families improve, while others deteriorate, and how uncertain and irregular is such improvement as appears, we know that we could make better progress if all children had the same rich endowment and wise care that some receive. And, when we see how much of our improvement is due to gains made in hygienic knowledge, in public provision for education and sanitary regulation, none of which has been accomplished by mothers, we are forced to see that whatever advance the race has made is not exclusively attributable to motherhood. The human mother does less for her young, both absolutely and proportionately, than any kind of mother on earth. She does not obtain food for them, nor covering, nor shelter, nor protection, nor defence. She does not educate them beyond the personal habits required in the family circle and in her limited range of social life. The necessary knowledge of the world, so indispensable to every human being, she cannot give, because she does not possess it. All this provision and education are given by other hands and brains than hers. Neither does the amount of physical care and labor bestowed on the child by its mother warrant her claims to superiority in motherhood: this is but a part of our idealism of the subject.

===Chinese===
对这两个问题，我们都必须答一个「不」。当我们看到一些家庭在改进，另一些却在退化，而所见的那点改进又是何等无常、何等无规律，我们便知道：倘若所有的孩子都能得到某些孩子所得到的那种丰厚的给养与明智的照料，我们本可以取得更大的进步。而当我们看到，我们的改进有多少得自卫生学的知识、教育与卫生法规的公共供给——这些没有一样是由母亲们完成的——我们便不得不承认：种族所取得的任何进步，绝非仅仅归功于母职。人类的母亲为她的幼者所做的，无论就绝对量还是就比例而言，都少于世上任何一类母亲。她不为他们获取食物，不提供衣着、居所、保护与防卫。她对他们的教育，也超不出家庭圈子里和她有限的社会生活范围内所要求的那些个人习惯。那为每个人所不可或缺的、关于世界的必要知识，她给不了，因为她自己并不具备。这一切给养与教育，都由她之外的手与头脑给出。母亲倾注在孩子身上的身体照料与劳作之量，同样不足以为她在母职上的优越主张作担保：这不过是我们对这一题目的理想化的一部分罢了。

===Original===
The poor man's wife has far too much of other work to do to spend all her time in waiting on her children. The rich man's wife could do it, but does not, partly because she hires someone to do it for her, and partly because she, too, has other duties to occupy her time. Only in isolated cases do we find a mother deputing all other service to others, and concentrating her energies on feeding, clothing, washing, dressing, and, as far as may be, educating her own child. When such cases are found, it remains to be shown that the child so reared is proportionately benefited by this unremittent devotion of its mother. On the contrary, the best service and education a child can receive involve the accumulated knowledge and exchanged activities of thousands upon thousands besides his mother—the fathers of the race.

===Chinese===
穷人的妻子有太多别的活计要做，不可能把全部时间都花在伺候孩子上。富人的妻子做得到，却不做：一部分因为她雇了别人代做，一部分因为她同样有别的职责占住她的时间。只有在孤例中，我们才见到一位母亲把其他一切服务都托付给别人，而把自己的精力集中于亲自喂养、穿戴、洗濯、装扮自己的孩子，并在可能的范围内亲自教育他。即便找到这样的例子，也还须证明：这样养大的孩子，确乎从母亲这须臾不离的奉献中得到了相称的益处。恰恰相反，一个孩子所能得到的最好的服务与教育，涉及他母亲之外千千万万人——种族的父亲们——积累的知识与交换的活动。

===Original===
There does not appear, in the care and education of the child as given by the mother, any special superiority in human maternity. Measuring woman first in direct comparison of her reproductive processes with those of other animals, she does not fulfil this function so easily or so well as they. Measuring her educative processes by interpersonal comparison, the few admittedly able mothers with the many painfully unable ones, she seems more lacking, if possible, than in the other branch. The gain in human education thus far has not been acquired or distributed through the mother, but through men and single women; and there is nothing in the achievements of human motherhood to prove that it is for the advantage of the race to have women give all their time to it. Giving all their time to it does not improve it either in quantity or quality. The woman who works is usually a better reproducer than the woman who does not. And the woman who does not work is not proportionately a better educator.

===Chinese===
在母亲所给出的照料与教育之中，看不出人类的生育有任何特别的优越之处。先以她的生殖过程同其他动物直接比较，她履行这一功能不如它们那样轻易、那样好。再以人际比较来衡量她的教育过程——寥寥几位公认有才干的母亲，对上许许多多痛苦地无能为力的母亲——她在这一支上，倘使可能，似乎比在另一支上更显匮乏。人类教育迄今的增益，既不是经由母亲获得，也不是经由母亲分配的，而是经由男人们与单身女人们；而人类母职的种种成就里，没有任何东西能证明：让女人们把全部时间花在它上面，于种族有利。把全部时间都花在它上面，并不能使它在数量或质量上有所改进。做工的女人通常比不做工的女人是更好的生殖者。而不做工的女人，也并不因此就是相称的更好的教育者。

===Original===
An extraterrestrial sociologist, studying human life and hearing for the first time of our so-called "maternal sacrifice" as a means of benefiting the species, might be touched and impressed by the idea. "How beautiful!" he would say. "How exquisitely pathetic and tender! One-half of humanity surrendering all other human interests and activities to concentrate its time, strength, and devotion upon the functions of maternity! To bear and rear the majestic race to which they can never fully belong! To live vicariously forever, through their sons, the daughters being only another vicarious link! What a supreme and magnificent martyrdom!" And he would direct his researches toward discovering what system was used to develop and perfect this sublime consecration of half the race to the perpetuation of the other half. He would view with intense and pathetic interest the endless procession of girls, born human as their brothers were, but marked down at once as "female—abortive type—only use to produce males." He would expect to see this "sex sacrificed to reproductive necessities," yet gifted with human consciousness and intelligence, rise grandly to the occasion, and strive to fit itself in every way for its high office. He would expect to find society commiserating the sacrifice, and honoring above all the glorious creature whose life was to be sunk utterly in the lives of others, and using every force properly to rear and fully to fit these functionaries for their noble office. Alas for the extraterrestrial sociologist and his natural expectations! After exhaustive study, finding nothing of these things, he would return to Mars or Saturn or wherever he came from, marvelling within himself at the vastness of the human paradox.

===Chinese===
假若有一位来自地球之外的社会学家，研究着人类的生活，初次听到我们所谓的「母性牺牲」（"maternal sacrifice"）这样一种造福物种的手段，他或许会为这观念所感动、所折服。「多么美啊！」他会说。「多么精微地哀婉而温柔啊！人类的一半，舍弃其余一切人类的兴趣与活动，以便把她的时间、力量与爱全部倾注于生育的功能之上！生养那她们永远不能完全归属的庄严种族！永远替代性地活着——经由她们的儿子而活，女儿们不过是又一重替活的环节！何等至高而壮丽的殉道！」于是他会把自己的研究引向这样一个问题：究竟用的是什么体制，来发展并完善这半个种族对另半个种族之延续的崇高献身。他会怀着强烈而动人的兴趣，注视那川流不息的女孩的行列：她们同她们的兄弟一样生而为人，却被立刻标定为「女性——败育的类型——唯一用途是生产男性」。他会期待看到，这个「为生殖之必然而牺牲的性别」，虽被赋予人类的意识与智能，却庄严地临事而起，力求在一切方面使自己配得上它那崇高的职守。他会期待发现社会哀怜这牺牲，把至高的荣耀给予那生命注定要完全沉没于他人生命之中的辉煌造物，并运用一切力量去妥善地养育这些执事者、充分地装备她们以胜任其高贵的职守。可惜啊，这位地球之外的社会学家和他那自然的期待！在穷尽性的研究之后，发现这一切全无踪影，他只好返回火星（Mars）或土星（Saturn）或他来自的什么地方，心中惊叹着人类这个悖论的浩大。

===Original===
If the position of woman is to be justified by the doctrine of maternal sacrifice, surely society, or the individual, or both, would make some preparation for it. No such preparation is made. Society recognizes no such function. Premiums have been sometimes paid for large numbers of children, but they were paid to the fathers of them. The elaborate social machinery which constitutes our universal marriage market has no department to assist or advance motherhood. On the contrary, it is directly inimical to it, so that in our society life motherhood means direct loss, and is avoided by the social devotee. And the individual? Surely here right provision will be made. Young women, glorying in their prospective duties, their sacred and inalienable office, their great sex-martyrdom to race-advantage, will be found solemnly preparing for this work. What do we find? We find our young women reared in an attitude which is absolutely unconscious of and often injurious to their coming motherhood—an irresponsible, indifferent, ignorant class of beings, so far as motherhood is concerned. They are fitted to attract the other sex for economic uses or, at most, for mutual gratification, but not for motherhood. They are reared in unbroken ignorance of their supposed principal duties, knowing nothing of these duties till they enter upon them.

===Chinese===
倘若女人的位置要靠「母性牺牲」的学说来辩护，那么社会、或者个人、或者两者，总该为它做些准备吧。这样的准备并没有做。社会不承认有这样的功能。为多生孩子，有时也发过奖金，但那是发给孩子们的父亲的。那构成我们普遍婚姻市场的精巧社会机器，没有一个部门是用来扶助或促进母职的。恰恰相反，它直接与母职为敌，以致在我们的社交生活中，母职意味着直接的损失，从而被那虔诚的社交信徒所回避。那么个人呢？总该在这里见到正当的安排了吧。人们总该看到年轻女子们，怀着对自己未来职责——那神圣而不可剥夺的职守、那为种族利益而行的伟大性别殉道——的光荣感，郑重地为这工作做着准备吧。我们看到了什么呢？我们看到，我们的年轻女子们被养大在一种对她们将临的母职全然无觉、甚至常常有害的姿态之中——就母职而言，她们是一群不负责任、漠不关心、无知无识的生灵。她们被调教得能以经济之用去吸引异性，或者至多以相互取悦为能事，却不是为着母职。她们对自己那些所谓的主要职责，在被养育的全过程中一无所知，直到踏上这些职责，方才知道它们的存在。

===Original===
This is as though all men were to be soldiers with the fate of nations in their hands; and no man told or taught a word of war or military service until he entered the battlefield!

===Chinese===
这就好比所有男人都要当兵，国家的命运就握在他们手中；却没有任何一个男人，在被送上战场之前，听人讲过或学过一个字的战争或军务！

===Original===
The education of young women has no department of maternity. It is considered indelicate to give this consecrated functionary any previous knowledge of her sacred duties. This most important and wonderful of human functions is left from age to age in the hands of absolutely untaught women. It is tacitly supposed to be fulfilled by the mysterious working of what we call "the divine instinct of maternity." Maternal instinct is a very respectable and useful instinct common to most animals. It is "divine" and "holy" only as all the laws of nature are divine and holy; and it is such only when it works to the right fulfilment of its use. If the race-preservative processes are to be held more sacred than the self-preservative processes, we must admit all the functions and faculties of reproduction to the same degree of reverence—the passion of the male for the female as well as the passion of the mother for her young. And if, still further, we are to honor the race-preservative processes most in their highest and latest development, which is the only comparison to be made on a natural basis, we should place the great, disinterested, social function of education far above the second-selfishness of individual maternal functions. Maternal instinct, merely as an instinct, is unworthy of our superstitious reverence. It should be measured only as a means to an end, and valued in proportion to its efficacy.

===Chinese===
年轻女子的教育中，没有母性这一科目。给这位身负圣职的执事者一点关于其神圣职责的预备知识，会被认为有失体统。这一人类功能中最重要、最奇妙的一种，就这样一代又一代地被留在绝对未经教诲的女人们手里。人们心照不宣地以为，它自会凭着我们所称的「生育的神圣本能」的神秘作用而得以完成。母性本能是一种颇可敬、也颇有益的本能，为大多数动物所共有。它之所以「神圣」与「圣洁」，只在一切自然法则都是神圣与圣洁的那个意义上；而且只有当它致力于其用途的正当履行时，才是如此。倘若种族保存的诸过程要被奉为比自我保存的诸过程更神圣，那我们就必须把生殖的一切功能与官能，都纳入同等程度的崇敬——雄性对雌性的激情，一如母亲对幼者的激情。而倘若更进一步，我们要在诸种族保存过程的最高、最新的发展上致以最高的敬意——这是唯一能在自然基础上作的比较——那我们就应当把教育这一伟大的、无私的社会功能，置于个体母性功能那种「第二等的自私」之上远而又远的地方。母性本能，单作为一种本能而言，当不起我们迷信的崇敬。它只应作为达乎目的的手段来衡量，并依其效力而估价。

===Original===
Among animals, which have but a low degree of intelligence, instinct is at its height, and works well. Among savages, still incapable of much intellectual development, instinct holds large place. The mother beast can and does take all the care of her young by instinct; the mother savage, nearly all, supplemented by the tribal traditions, the educative influences of association, and some direct instruction. As humanity advances, growing more complex and varied, and as human intelligence advances to keep pace with new functions and new needs, instinct decreases in value. The human creature prospers and progresses not by virtue of his animal instinct, but by the wisdom and force of a cultivated intelligence and will, with which to guide his action and to control and modify the very instincts which used to govern him.

===Chinese===
在智能尚低的动物中间，本能正处在其巅峰，运转良好。在仍不能有多大智识发展的野蛮人中间，本能也占有广大的地盘。母兽能够、也确实凭本能独自照料她幼崽的一切；野蛮人的母亲几乎也是样样凭本能，只是辅以部族的传统、群居生活的教化影响，以及些许直接的教导。随着人类的进展，生活愈趋繁复多样，随着人类智能的进展赶上新的功能与新的需要，本能的价值便日趋下降。人这种造物之所以兴旺、之所以进步，并非凭借他的动物本能，而是凭借受了培育的智能与意志的智慧与力量——凭它们来引导他的行动，来控制并修正那从前支配着他的种种本能。

===Original===
The human female, denied the enlarged activities which have developed intelligence in man, denied the education of the will which only comes by freedom and power, has maintained the rudimentary forces of instinct to the present day. With her extreme modification to sex, this faculty of instinct runs mainly along sex-lines, and finds fullest vent in the processes of maternity, where it has held unbroken sway. So the children of humanity are born into the arms of an endless succession of untrained mothers, who bring to the care and teaching of their children neither education for that wonderful work nor experience therein: they bring merely the intense accumulated force of a brute instinct—the blind devoted passion of the mother for the child. Maternal love is an enormous force, but force needs direction. Simply to love the child does not serve him unless specific acts of service express this love. What these acts of service are and how they are performed make or mar his life forever.

===Chinese===
人类雌性既被剥夺了那些在男人身上发展出智能的阔大活动，又被剥夺了那种唯有经由自由与力量方能获得的意志之教育，便把本能的那些初级力量一直保持到今天。由于她在性上的极端改易，这种本能的官能便主要循着性的路线运行，并在生育的诸过程中找到最充分的宣泄——在那里，它掌着不曾间断的统治。于是，人类的孩子们便降生在一队无穷无尽、未经训练的母亲的怀抱里：她们对于照料与教导孩子这件奇妙的工作，既没有受过为此的教育，也没有其中的经验；她们带有的，不过是一种兽性本能的强烈积聚之力——母亲对孩子的盲目而炽热的激情。母爱是一股巨大的力量，但力量需要方向。单单爱这孩子，并不就能服务于他，除非有具体的服务行为来表达这爱。而这些服务行为是什么、做得怎样，则成全或毁掉他的一生，永远如此。

===Original===
Observe the futility of unaided maternal love and instinct in the simple act of feeding the child. Belonging to order mammalia, the human mother has an instinctive desire to suckle her young. (Some ultra-civilized have lost even that.) But this instinct has not taught her such habits of life as insure her ability to fulfil this natural function. Failing in the natural method, of what further use is instinct in the nourishment of the child? Can maternal instinct discriminate between Marrow's Food and Bridge's Food, Hayrick's Food and Pestle's Food, Pennywhistle's Sterilized Milk, and all the other infants' foods which are prepared and put upon the market by—men! These are not prepared by instinct, maternal or paternal, but by chemical analysis and physiological study; and their effect is observed and the diet varied by physicians, who do not do their work by instinct, either.

===Chinese===
且看那孤立无援的母爱与本能在喂养孩子这一简单举动上的无能。人类母亲既属哺乳纲，便有哺乳幼崽的本能欲望。（有些超文明的人连这个也丧失了。）但这本能并没有教会她那些足以保证她履行这一自然功能的生活习惯。既然天然的方法已经失效，本能在孩子的营养上还有什么进一步的用处呢？母性本能能分辨「马罗食品」（Marrow's Food）与「布里奇食品」（Bridge's Food）、「海里克食品」（Hayrick's Food）与「佩斯尔食品」（Pestle's Food）、「彭尼惠斯尔消毒奶」（Pennywhistle's Sterilized Milk），以及一切别的婴儿食品吗——而那些食品，全是男人们配制并投放到市场上去的！它们不是由本能配制的——无论母性的还是父性的——而是由化学分析与生理学研究配制的；它们的功效由医生们来观察，饮食由医生们来调整——医生们同样不是凭本能做他们的工作的。

===Original===
If the bottle-baby survive the loss of mother's milk, when he comes to the table, does maternal instinct suffice then to administer a proper diet for young children? Let the doctor and the undertaker answer. The wide and varied field of masculine activity in the interests of little children, from the peculiar human phenomenon of masculine assistance in parturition (there is one animal, the obstetric frog, where it also appears) to the manufacture of articles for feeding, clothing, protecting, amusing, and educating the baby, goes to show the utter inadequacy of maternal instinct in the human female. Another thing it shows also—the criminal failure of that human female to supply by intelligent effort what instinct can no longer accomplish. For a reasoning, conscious being deliberately to undertake the responsibility of maintaining human life without making due preparation for the task is more than carelessness.

===Chinese===
倘若那奶瓶娃娃熬过了失去母乳这一劫，等到他上餐桌的时候，母性本能总够用了吧——总能为幼儿安排一份正当的饮食了吧？且让医生与殡葬人来回答。男性为幼小儿童的利益而活动的广阔而多样的领域——从男性协助分娩这一人类特有的现象（有一种动物，即助产蛙（obstetric frog），也有此现象），到喂养、穿戴、保护、逗乐、教育婴儿的种种物品的制造——都表明母性本能在人类雌性身上是何等彻底地不敷使用。它还表明另一件事——人类雌性的一种犯罪的失职：本能已不能再完成的事，她不肯以智能的努力去补足。一个有理性、有自觉的生灵，明知故犯地承担起维持人类生命的责任，却不为这任务作应有的准备，这就远不止是疏忽了。

===Original===
Before a man enters a trade, art, or profession, he studies it. He qualifies himself for the duties he is to undertake. He would be held a presuming impostor if he engaged in work he was not fitted to do, and his failure would mark him instantly with ridicule and reproach. In the more important professions, especially in those dealing with what we call "matters of life and death," the shipmaster or pilot, doctor or druggist, is required not only to study his business, but to pass an examination under those who have already become past masters, and obtain a certificate or a diploma or some credential to show that he is fit to be entrusted with the direct responsibility for human life.

===Chinese===
一个人在进入一门行当、艺术或职业之前，先要研习它。他要使自己具备资格，去承担将要担负的职责。倘若他从事一项自己并不胜任的工作，他会被看成一个僭越的骗子，而他的失败立刻就会给他打上讥嘲与责难的印记。在那些更重要的职业里，尤其是在那些处理我们所谓「生死攸关之事」的职业里，船长或引航员、医生或药剂师，不仅被要求研习自己的业务，还被要求在那些已成老师傅的人面前通过考试，取得一份证书或文凭或某种凭据，以表明他配得上把与人命攸关的直接责任托付给他。

===Original===
Women enter a position which gives into their hands direct responsibility for the life or death of the whole human race with neither study nor experience, with no shadow of preparation or guarantee of capability. So far as they give it a thought, they fondly imagine that this mysterious "maternal instinct" will see them through. Instruction, if needed, they will pick up when the time comes: experience they will acquire as the children appear. "I guess I know how to bring up children!" cried the resentful old lady who was being advised: "I've buried seven!" The record of untrained instinct as a maternal faculty in the human race is to be read on the rows and rows of little gravestones which crowd our cemeteries. The experience gained by practising on the child is frequently buried with it.

===Chinese===
女人们进入的那个位置，把全人类的生死之直接责任交到她们手里，而她们既无研习又无经验，没有一丝一毫的准备或能力的保证。就她们肯想一想这事而言，她们天真地以为，这神秘的「母性本能」自会帮她们渡过难关。指导嘛，若有需要，到时候现学就是；经验嘛，孩子们一个个出世，自然就有了。「我想我还知道怎么带孩子吧！」那位受人劝告的老太太愤愤地喊道：「我都埋了七个啦！」未受训练的本能作为人类种族中一项母性官能的记录，就写在我们墓园里一排又一排拥挤的小墓碑上。靠在孩子身上练出来的经验，常常随孩子一同埋进土里。

===Original===
No, the maternal sacrifice theory will not bear examination. As a sex specialized to reproduction, giving up all personal activity, all honest independence, all useful and progressive economic service for her glorious consecration to the uses of maternity, the human female has little to show in the way of results which can justify her position. Neither the enormous percentage of children lost by death nor the low average health of those who survive, neither physical nor mental progress, give any proof of race advantage from the maternal sacrifice.

===Chinese===
不，「母性牺牲」论经不起检验。作为一个专门化于生殖的性别，为了她对生育之用途那光荣的献身，交出了全部个人活动、全部诚实的独立、全部有益而进步的经济服务——人类雌性拿不出多少成果，来为她的位置辩护。无论是孩子死亡的高得骇人的比例，还是存活者平均健康水平的低下；无论是身体上的还是心智上的进步——都不能给出任何证据，证明母性牺牲给种族带来了好处。

## Preface / 序言

===Original===
This book is written to offer a simple and natural explanation of one of the most common and most perplexing problems of human life﻿—a problem which presents itself to almost every individual for practical solution, and which demands the most serious attention of the moralist, the physician, and the sociologist﻿—

===Chinese===
本书旨在就人类生活中最常见、最令人困惑的问题之一，提出一个简单而自然的解释——这个问题几乎向每一个个人呈现，要求其加以实际的解决，又要求道德家、医生与社会学家予以最严肃的关注——

===Original===
To show how some of the worst evils under which we suffer, evils long supposed to be inherent and ineradicable in our natures, are but the result of certain arbitrary conditions of our own adoption, and how, by removing those conditions, we may remove the evils resultant﻿—

===Chinese===
要表明：我们所承受的一些最深重的祸害——那些长久以来被认定与生俱来、根植于我们本性而无法根除的祸害——不过是我们自行采纳的某些武断条件所致的结果；而只要除去那些条件，我们便可能除去随之而生的祸害——

===Original===
To point out how far we have already gone in the path of improvement, and how irresistibly the social forces of today are compelling us further, even without our knowledge and against our violent opposition﻿—an advance which may be greatly quickened by our recognition and assistance﻿—

===Chinese===
要指出我们在改良之路上已经走出多远，指出今日的种种社会力量正如何不可抗拒地驱策我们继续前行——哪怕我们并不自知，哪怕我们激烈反对——而这一进程，若得我们的认识与协助，便可大大加快——

===Original===
To reach in especial the thinking women of today, and urge upon them a new sense, not only of their social responsibility as individuals, but of their measureless racial importance as makers of men.

===Chinese===
要特别触及今日有思想的妇女，敦促她们树立一种新的意识：不仅意识到自己作为个体的社会责任，更意识到自己作为「人的造就者」（makers of men）所具有的无从估量的种族重要性。

===Original===
It is hoped also that the theory advanced will prove sufficiently suggestive to give rise to such further study and discussion as shall prove its error or establish its truth.

===Chinese===
同时也希望，本书所提出的理论足以发人深思，足以引出更进一步的研究与讨论，从而证明其误，或确立其真。

===Original===
Charlotte Perkins Stetson

===Chinese===
夏洛特·珀金斯·斯泰森（Charlotte Perkins Stetson）

## Proem / 序诗

===Original===
> In dark and early ages, through the primal forests faring,
 

 Ere the soul came shining into prehistoric night,
 

 Twofold man was equal; they were comrades dear and daring,
 

 Living wild and free together in unreasoning delight.

===Chinese===
> 在幽暗的远古年代，穿越原始的森林跋涉，
>
> 灵魂尚未闪耀着照进史前的黑夜，
>
> 两性之人本是平等；他们是亲爱而无畏的伙伴，
>
> 一同野居而自由，生活在无思无虑的欢悦之中。

===Original===
> Ere the soul was born and consciousness came slowly,
 

 Ere the soul was born, to man and woman, too,
 

 Ere he found the Tree of Knowledge, that awful tree and holy,
 

 Ere he knew he felt, and knew he knew.

===Chinese===
> 在灵魂降生、意识缓缓到来之前，
>
> 在灵魂降生之前——女人也同男人一样——
>
> 在他寻见知识之树、那可畏而神圣的树之前，
>
> 在他知道自己有所感、知道自己有所知之前。

===Original===
> Then said he to Pain, “I am wise now, and I know you!
 

 No more will I suffer while power and wisdom last!”
 

 Then said he to Pleasure, “I am strong, and I will show you
 

 That the will of man can seize you﻿—aye, and hold you fast!”

===Chinese===
> 于是他对「痛苦」说：「如今我有了智慧，认得你了！
>
> 只要力量与智慧还在，我便不再受苦！」
>
> 于是他对「快乐」说：「我强大，我要让你看看，
>
> 男人的意志能攫住你——是啊，还能将你牢牢握紧！」

===Original===
> Food he ate for pleasure, and wine he drank for gladness.
 

 And woman? Ah, the woman! the crown of all delight!
 

 His now﻿—he knew it! He was strong to madness
 

 In that early dawning after prehistoric night.

===Chinese===
> 他为快乐而进食，为欢愉而饮酒。
>
> 那女人呢？啊，那女人！一切欢愉的冠冕！
>
> 如今是他的了——他明白这一点！他强壮得近乎疯狂，
>
> 在那史前黑夜之后初绽的曙光里。

===Original===
> His﻿—his forever! That glory sweet and tender!
 

 Ah, but he would love her! And she should love but him!
 

 He would work and struggle for her, he would shelter and defend her﻿—
 

 She should never leave him, never, till their eyes in death were dim.

===Chinese===
> 她是他的——是他永远的了！那甜美而温柔的荣光！
>
> 啊，他会爱她的！而她也只该爱他一人！
>
> 他愿为她操劳、为她奋斗，他愿庇护她、捍卫她——
>
> 她永不该离他而去，永不，直到死亡黯淡他们的双眼。

===Original===
> Close, close he bound her, that she should leave him never;
 

 Weak still he kept her, lest she be strong to flee;
 

 And the fainting flame of passion he kept alive forever
 

 With all the arts and forces of earth and sky and sea.

===Chinese===
> 他紧紧地、紧紧地捆缚她，好叫她永不离开；
>
> 他始终使她孱弱，唯恐她强壮到足以逃走；
>
> 而那奄奄欲熄的激情之焰，他使之永不熄灭，
>
> 凭借大地、天空与海洋的一切技艺和力量。

===Original===
> And, ah, the long journey! The slow and awful ages
 

 They have labored up together, blind and crippled, all astray!
 

 Through what a mighty volume, with a million shameful pages,
 

 From the freedom of the forests to the prisons of to-day!

===Chinese===
> 啊，这漫长的旅程！那缓慢而可怖的世代，
>
> 他们一同挣扎着攀行，盲目、伤残、一路迷途！
>
> 穿过何等浩繁的一部大书，写满一百万页的耻辱，
>
> 从森林的自由，直走到今日的牢狱！

===Original===
> Food he ate for pleasure, and it slew him with diseases!
 

 Wine he drank for gladness, and it led the way to crime!
 

 And woman? He will hold her﻿—he will have her when he pleases﻿—
 

 And he never once hath seen her since the prehistoric time!

===Chinese===
> 他为快乐而进食，食物却以种种疾病杀了他！
>
> 他为欢愉而饮酒，酒却引路通向罪愆！
>
> 那女人呢？他要握住她——他兴之所至便要占有她——
>
> 而自史前时代以降，他竟一次也不曾看见过她！

===Original===
> Gone the friend and comrade of the day when life was younger,
 

 She who rests and comforts, she who helps and saves.
 

 Still he seeks her vainly, with a never-dying hunger;
 

 Alone beneath his tyrants, alone above his slaves!

===Chinese===
> 生命较年轻时日的那个朋友与伙伴去了，
>
> 那予人安息与慰藉的、那施以援手拯救危难的，去了。
>
> 他仍在徒然寻觅她，怀着一场永不死灭的饥渴；
>
> 在他的暴君之下孤独，在他的奴隶之上也孤独！

===Original===
> Toiler, bent and weary with the load of thine own making!
 

 Thou who art sad and lonely, though lonely all in vain!
 

 Who hast sought to conquer Pleasure and have her for the taking,
 

 And found that Pleasure only was another name for Pain﻿—

===Chinese===
> 劳作者啊，被你自己造下的重负压弯、压倦！
>
> 你悲伤而孤独，纵然这孤独全属枉然！
>
> 你曾想征服「快乐」，将她随手攫取，
>
> 却发现「快乐」不过是「痛苦」的另一个名字——

===Original===
> Nature hath reclaimed thee, forgiving dispossession!
 

 God hath not forgotten, though man doth still forget!
 

 The woman-soul is rising, in despite of thy transgression﻿—
 

 Loose her now, and trust her! She will love thee yet!

===Chinese===
> 自然已将你重新收回，宽恕了那场剥夺！
>
> 上帝并未忘记，尽管人至今仍在忘记！
>
> 女人的灵魂正在升起，不顾你的罪愆——
>
> 现在就放开她，信任她吧！她仍会爱你！

===Original===
> Love thee? She will love thee as only freedom knoweth!
 

 Love thee? She will love thee while Love itself doth live!
 

 Fear not the heart of woman! No bitterness it showeth!
 

 The ages of her sorrow have but taught her to forgive!

===Chinese===
> 爱你？她会爱你，以唯有自由才懂得的方式！
>
> 爱你？只要爱本身还活着，她就会一直爱你！
>
> 不要畏惧女人的心！它不显半分怨苦！
>
> 她那绵延世代的悲伤，只教会了她宽恕！

## V / 五

===Original===
The facts stated in the foregoing chapters are familiar and undeniable, the argument seems clear; yet the mind reacts violently from the conclusions it is forced to admit, and tries to find relief in the commonplace conditions of everyday life. From this looming phantom of the oversexed female of genus *homo* we fly back in satisfaction to familiar acquaintances and relatives﻿—to Mrs. John Smith and Miss Imogene Jones, to mothers and sisters and daughters and sweethearts and wives. We feel that such a dreadful state of things cannot be true, or we should surely have noticed it. We may even perform that acrobatic feat so easy to most minds﻿—admit that the statement may be theoretically true, but practically false!

===Chinese===
前几章所陈述的事实，是人们熟知而无可否认的，论证似乎也明白清楚；然而心智对于自己被迫承认的结论，反应却是激烈的抗拒，并力图在日常生活平凡的境况中寻得宽解。面对人属（genus *homo*）的雌性过度性化（oversexed）这赫然逼近的幻影，我们心满意足地飞回到熟识的故交与亲眷那里——飞回到约翰·史密斯太太（Mrs. John Smith）与伊莫金·琼斯小姐（Miss Imogene Jones）那里，飞回到母亲、姊妹、女儿、情人与妻子那里。我们觉得，如此可怕的境况不可能属实，否则我们肯定早就注意到了。我们甚至还能玩出那桩对多数心智而言轻而易举的杂技——承认这一说法在理论上也许为真，在实际上却为假！

===Original===
Two simple laws of brain action are responsible for the difficulty of convincing the human race of any large general truths concerning itself. One is common to all brains, to all nerve sensations indeed, and is cheerfully admitted to have nothing to do with the sexuo-economic relation. It is this simple fact, in popular phrase﻿—that what we are used to we do not notice. This rests on the law of adaptation, the steady, ceaseless pressure that tends to fit the organism to the environment. A nerve touched for the first time with a certain impression feels this first impression far more than the hundredth or thousandth, though the thousandth be far more violent than the first. If an impression be constant and regular, we become utterly insensitive to it, and only respond under some special condition, as the ticking of a clock, the noise of running water or waves on the beach, even the clatter of railroad trains, grows imperceptible to those who hear it constantly. It is perfectly possible for an individual to become accustomed to the most disadvantageous conditions, and fail to notice them.

===Chinese===
有两条简单的大脑活动法则，解释了为何难于使人类信服任何关于自身的重大普遍真理。其中一条为一切头脑所共有——确实也为一切神经感觉所共有——而且人们乐于承认它与性-经济关系（sexuo-economic relation）毫无干系。用通俗的话说，它就是这样一个简单的事实：我们习惯了的东西，我们便不再注意。这有赖于适应法则——那股力求使有机体契合于环境的稳定而不停歇的压力。一条神经初次接触某个印象时，对这第一印象的感受，远强于对第一百次或第一千次的感受，纵使第一千次的刺激远比第一次猛烈。倘若一个印象恒常而有规律，我们便对它全然无感，只在某种特殊条件下才会作出回应——正如时钟的滴答、流水或岸边波涛的喧声，乃至火车的哐当声，对常听见它们的人而言，渐渐隐没无闻。一个个体完全可能习惯于最不利的境况，而注意不到它们。

===Original===
It is equally possible for a race, a nation, a class, to become accustomed to most disadvantageous conditions, and fail to notice them. Take, as an individual instance, the wearing of corsets by women. Put a corset, even a loose one, on a vigorous man or woman who never wore one, and there is intense discomfort, and a vivid consciousness thereof. The healthy muscles of the trunk resent the pressure, the action of the whole body is checked in the middle, the stomach is choked, the process of digestion interfered with; and the victim says, “How can you bear such a thing?”

===Chinese===
一个种族、一个民族、一个阶级，也同样可能习惯于极不利的境况，而注意不到它们。且举一个个体的事例：妇女穿紧身胸衣（corset）。给一个从未穿过它的健壮男子或女子穿上一件紧身胸衣——哪怕是一件宽松的——都会有强烈的不适，以及对这不适的鲜活意识。躯干上健康的肌肉愤懑于这压力，全身的动作在中段受到遏制，胃部被挤压，消化的过程受到妨碍；这位受难者便说：「这样的东西你们怎么受得了？」

===Original===
But the person habitually wearing a corset does not feel these evils. They exist, assuredly, the facts are there, the body is not deceived; but the nerves have become accustomed to these disagreeable sensations, and no longer respond to them. The person “does not feel it.” In fact, the wearer becomes so used to the sensations that, when they are removed﻿—with the corset﻿—there is a distinct sense of loss and discomfort. The heavy folds of the cravat, stock, and neckcloth of earlier men’s fashions, the heavy horsehair peruke, the stiff high collar of today, the kind of shoes we wear﻿—these are perfectly familiar instances of the force of habit in the individual.

===Chinese===
但习惯性地穿着紧身胸衣的人却感觉不到这些祸害。它们确实存在，事实俱在，身体并未受骗；只是神经已经习惯了这些不快的感觉，不再对它们作出回应。此人「感觉不到它」。事实上，穿的人对这些感觉已如此习惯，以至于当它们——连同紧身胸衣一起——被除去时，竟有一种明显的失落与不适之感。早年间男式时尚里沉重的领结、颈带与颈巾，沉重的马鬃假发，今天硬挺的高领，我们所穿的那一类鞋子——这些都是个体身上习惯之力再熟悉不过的例证。

===Original===
This is equally true of racial habits. That a king should rule because he was born, passed unquestioned for thousands of years. That the eldest son should inherit the titles and estates was a similar phenomenon as little questioned. That a debtor should be imprisoned, and so entirely prevented from paying his debts, was common law. So glaring an evil as chattel slavery was an unchallenged social institution from earliest history to our own day among the most civilized nations of the earth. Christ himself let it pass unnoticed. The hideous injustice of Christianity to the Jew attracted no attention through many centuries. That the serf went with the soil, and was owned by the lord thereof, was one of the foundations of society in the Middle Ages.

===Chinese===
种族的习性同样如此。国王仅因出生便当统治，几千年间不曾受到质疑。长子继承头衔与地产，是同样少受质疑的现象。债务人应被投入监狱、从而被彻底断绝还债之路，曾是普通法。像动产奴隶制（chattel slavery）这样刺眼的邪恶，从最早的历史直到我们这个时代，在地球上一些最文明的国家里，竟是一种不曾受到挑战的社会制度。基督本人也对它未加理会。基督教对犹太人的骇人不义，许多个世纪里不曾引起注意。农奴随土地而转让、为土地之领主所有，曾是中世纪社会的一个根基。

===Original===
Social conditions, like individual conditions, become familiar by use, and cease to be observed. This is the reason why it is so much easier to criticise the customs of other persons or other nations than our own. It is also the reason why we so naturally deny and resent the charges of the critic. It is not necessarily because of any injustice on the one side or dishonesty on the other, but because of a simple and useful law of nature. The Englishman coming to America is much struck by America’s political corruption; and, in the earnest desire to serve his brother, he tells us all about it. That which he has at home he does not observe, because he is used to it. The American in England finds also something to object to, and omits to balance his criticism by memories of home.

===Chinese===
社会条件（social conditions）同个体的条件一样，因习用而变得熟稔，进而不再被观察。这正是批评他人的或他国的习俗，何以远比批评我们自己的容易。这也正是我们何以如此自然而然地否认并愤懑于批评者的指控。这未必是因为一方有什么不公、另一方有什么不诚，而是因为一条简单而有益的自然法则。来到美国的英国人，对美国的政治腐败深感震动；怀着服务兄弟的热切愿望，他把这一切都告诉了我们。而他自己国内有的那些，他却观察不到，因为他习惯了。在英国的美国人也会发现一些可指摘之处，却忘了以对祖国的记忆来平衡他的批评。

===Original===
When a condition exists among us which began in those unrecorded ages back of tradition even, which obtains in varying degree among every people on earth, and which begins to act upon the individual at birth, it would be a miracle past all belief if people should notice it. The sexuo-economic relation is such a condition. It began in primeval savagery. It exists in all nations. Each boy and girl is born into it, trained into it, and has to live in it. The world’s progress in matters like these is attained by a slow and painful process, but one which works to good ends.

===Chinese===
当一种境况存在于我们中间——它肇始于甚至比传统更早的那些无记载的年代，它以或深或浅的程度存在于地球上每一个民族之中，它自个体出生之时便开始作用于他——那么，倘若人们竟能注意到它，那将是难以置信的奇迹。性-经济关系正是这样一种境况。它肇始于太古的蛮荒。它存在于一切民族之中。每一个男孩和女孩都生而入乎其中、受训于其中，并且不得不生活于其中。世界在这类事情上的进步，是经由一个缓慢而痛苦的过程取得的——但这是一个导向善果的过程。

===Original===
In the course of social evolution there are developed individuals so constituted as not to fit existing conditions, but to be organically adapted to more advanced conditions. These advanced individuals respond in sharp and painful consciousness to existing conditions, and cry out against them according to their lights. The history of religion, of political and social reform, is full of familiar instances of this. The heretic, the reformer, the agitator, these feel what their compeers do not, see what they do not, and, naturally, say what they do not. The mass of the people are invariably displeased by the outcry of these uneasy spirits. In simple primitive periods they were promptly put to death. Progress was slow and difficult in those days. But this severe process of elimination developed the kind of progressive person known as a martyr; and this remarkable sociological law was manifested: that the strength of a current of social force is increased by the sacrifice of individuals who are willing to die in the effort to promote it. “The blood of the martyrs is the seed of the church.” This is so commonly known today, though not formulated, that power hesitates to persecute, lest it intensify the undesirable heresy. A policy of “free speech” is found to let pass most of the uneasy pushes and spurts of these stirring forces, and lead to more orderly action. Our great anti-slavery agitation, the heroic efforts of the “women’s rights” supporters, are fresh and recent proofs of these plain facts: that the mass of the people do not notice existing conditions, and that they are not pleased with those who do. This is one strong reason why the sexuo-economic relation passes unobserved among us, and why any statement of it will be so offensive to many.

===Chinese===
在社会演化的进程中，会发展出这样一些人：他们的构成使之不合于既存的境况，而有机地适应于更先进的境况。这些先进的人以尖锐而痛苦的意识回应既存的境况，并各依其识见对之高声抗议。宗教史、政治与社会改革史，充满了这类人们熟知的例子。异端者、改革者、鼓动者，这些人感受到他们的同侪所感受不到的，看到他们的同侪所看不到的，于是自然而然，说出他们的同侪所说不出的话。大众对这些不安分者的呐喊，总是心怀不悦。在淳朴的原始时代，他们会被迅速处死。那时的进步缓慢而艰难。但这一严酷的淘汰过程，发展出了那类被称为殉道者的进步人士；而且显现出这条引人注目的社会学法则：一股社会力量之流的强度，会因那些甘愿为促进它而赴死的个体的牺牲而增强。「殉道者的血是教会的种子。」这一点今天虽未成文，却已为人所共知，以至于权力不敢轻易迫害，唯恐它使那不受欢迎的异端愈演愈烈。人们发现，「言论自由」的政策能让这些躁动的力量那些不安的推挤与迸发大半得到疏解，并导向更有秩序的行动。我们伟大的废奴鼓动、「妇女权利」拥护者的英勇努力，都是这些明白事实新鲜而切近的证明：大众注意不到既存的境况，而且不喜欢那些注意到的人。这便是性-经济关系何以在我们中间悄然无察地通行、对它的任何陈述又何以会触怒众人的一个有力理由。

===Original===
The other law of brain action which tends to prevent our perception of general truth is this: it is easier to personalize than to generalize. This is due primarily to the laws of mental development, but it is greatly added to by the very relation under discussion. As a common law of mental action, the power to observe and retain an individual impression marks a lower degree of development than the power to classify and collate impressions and make generalizations therefrom. There are savages who can say “hot fire,” “hot stone,” “hot water,” but cannot say “heat,” cannot think it. Similarly, they can say “good man,” “good knife,” “good meat”; but they cannot say “goodness,” they cannot think it. They have observed specific instances, but are unable to collate them, to generalize therefrom. So, in our common life, individual instances of injustice or cruelty are observed long before the popular mind is able to see that it is a condition which causes these things, and that the condition must be altered before the effects can be removed. A bad priest, a bad king, a bad master, were long observed and pointedly objected to before it began to be held that the condition of monarchy or the condition of slavery must needs bear fruit, and that, if we did not like the fruit, we might better change the tree. Any slaveholder would admit that there were instances of cruelty, laziness, pride, among masters, and of deceit, laziness, dishonesty, among slaves. What the slaveholder did not see was that, given the relation of chattel slavery, it inevitably tended to produce these evils, and did produce them, in spite of all the efforts of the individual to the contrary. To see the individual instance is easy. To see the general cause is harder, requires a further brain development. We, as a race, have long since reached the degree of general intelligence which ought to enable us to judge more largely and wisely of social questions; but here the deteriorating effect of the sexuo-economic relation is shown.

===Chinese===
另一条妨碍我们感知普遍真理的大脑活动法则是：具象为个人，比概括为通则容易。这首先缘于心智发展的诸法则，但我们正讨论的这层关系又大大加剧了它。作为心智活动的一条普遍法则，观察并留存单个印象的能力，标志着较低的发展程度；而对种种印象加以分类、对照并从中作出概括的能力，则标志着较高的发展程度。有些野蛮人会说「烫的火」「烫的石头」「烫的水」，却说不出「热」，也想不了它。同样，他们会说「好的人」「好的刀」「好的肉」，却说不出「善」，也想不了它。他们观察到了具体的个例，却不能把它们对照整理，不能从中概括。同样，在我们的共同生活中，不义或残酷的个别事例早已被人观察到，而大众心智却迟迟看不到：是一种境况造成了这些事情，而这境况必须先被改变，那些恶果才能被消除。一个坏的祭司、一个坏的国王、一个坏的主人，早早就被观察到并受到针锋相对的指摘；而人们开始认为君主制的境况或奴隶制的境况必然结出这样的果实、认为倘若我们不喜欢这果实、不如换掉这棵树，却要晚得多。任何一个奴隶主都会承认：主人中间有残酷、怠惰、骄傲的例子，奴隶中间有欺诈、怠惰、不诚实的例子。奴隶主所看不到的是：只要有了动产奴隶制这层关系，它便必然倾向于产生这些邪恶，而且确实产生了它们——任凭个人怎样努力反其道而行。看到个别的实例是容易的。看到普遍的原因则更难，需要更进一步的大脑发展。我们作为一个种族，早已达到了那种一般的智力程度，本应能使我们对社会问题作出更宏大、更明智的判断；但在这里，性-经济关系的败坏效应便显露出来了。

===Original===
The sex relation is intensely personal. All the functions and relations ensuing are intensely personal. The spirit of “me and my wife, my son John and his wife, us four, and no more,” is the natural spirit of this phase of life. By confining half the world to this one set of functions, we have confined it absolutely to the personal. And man that is born of woman is reared by her in this same atmosphere of concentrated personality, and afterward spends a large part of his life in it. This condition tends to magnify the personal and minimize the general in our minds, with results that are familiar to us all. The difficulty of enforcing sanitary laws, where personal convenience must be sacrificed to general safety, the size of the personal grievance as against the general, the need of “having it brought home to us,” which hinders every step of public advancement, and our eager response when it is “brought home to us,”﻿—these are truisms. So far as a comparison can be made, women are in this sense more personal than men, more personally sensitive, less willing to “stand in line” and “take turns,” less able to see why a general restriction is just when it touches them or their children. This is natural enough, inevitable enough, and only mentioned here as partially explaining why people do not see the general facts as to our oversexed condition. Yet they are patent everywhere, not only patent, but painful. Being used to them, we do not notice them, or, forced to notice them, we attribute the pain we feel to the evil behavior of some individual, and never think of it as being the result of a condition common to us all.

===Chinese===
性关系是极其个人性的。由之而来的一切功能与关系，都是极其个人性的。「我同我妻，我儿约翰同他妻，我们四个，别无他人」——这种精神，正是生命这一阶段的自然精神。由于把世界的一半局限在这一套功能之中，我们便把它绝对地局限在了个人性之内。而从女人所生的男人，又被她在这种浓缩的人格气氛中养大，此后又把生命的一大部分消磨于其中。这种境况倾向于在我们心智中放大个人、缩小一般，其后果为我们众人所熟知。卫生法规之难于执行——因为必须为公共的安全牺牲个人的便利；个人的冤屈相对于公共的利害之庞大；那种「非得落到自己头上」的需要——它阻碍着公共进步的每一步；以及当事情真的「落到自己头上」时我们热烈的回应——这些都是自明之理。就所能作的比较而言，女人在这种意义上比男人更个人化，对个人更敏感，更不愿意「排队」和「轮流」，更难明白为什么一条普遍的限制在触及她们或她们的孩子时仍是正当的。这足够自然，也足够必然，在这里提及，只是作为部分解释：为什么人们看不到关于我们过度性化状况的普遍事实。然而这些事实处处显豁，不仅显豁，而且令人痛楚。因为习惯了，我们便不注意它们；或者，被迫注意到它们时，我们便把所感的痛苦归咎于某个个人的恶行，而从不想到，它是我们共有的某种境况的结果。

===Original===
If we have among us such a condition as has been stated﻿—a state of morbid and excessive sex-development﻿—it must, of course, show itself in daily life in a thousand ways. The non-observer, not having seen any such manifestation, concludes that there is none, and so denies the alleged condition﻿—says it sounds all right, but he does not see any proof of it! Having clearly in mind that, if such proof exists, such commensurate evil in common life as would naturally result from an abnormal sex-distinction, these evils must be so common and habitual as to pass unobserved; and, farther, that, when forced upon our notice, we only see them as matters of personal behavior﻿—let us, in spite of these hindrances, see if the visible results among us are not such as must follow such a cause, and let us seek them merely in the phenomena of everyday life as we know it, not in the deeper sexual or social results.

===Chinese===
倘若我们中间当真存在这样一种境况——如前所述，一种病态而过度的性发展（sex-development）的状态——那么它当然必会在日常生活中以千百种方式显示出来。不经观察的人，没有看到过任何这样的表现，便断定不存在什么表现，于是否认所指称的境况——说它听起来头头是道，可他看不出任何证据！让我们心里牢牢记住：倘若这样的证据存在，倘若日常生活中存在着反常性别区分自然会导致的那种相称的邪恶，那么这些邪恶必定已寻常到、习惯到悄然漏过观察的地步；再者，当它们被迫闯入我们注意之时，我们也只把它们看作个人行为的问题——尽管有这些阻碍，还是让我们看看，我们中间那些可见的结果，是否正是这样一种原因必有的后随；让我们仅仅在我们所熟知的日常生活的诸现象中寻找它们，而不到更深层的性的或社会的后果中去寻找。

===Original===
A concrete instance, familiar as the day, and unbelievable in its ill effects, is the attitude of the mother toward her children in regard to the sex-relation. With very few exceptions, the mother gives her daughter no warning or prevision of what life holds for her, and so lets innocence and ignorance go on perpetuating sickness and sin and pain through ceaseless generations. A normal motherhood wisely and effectively guards its young from evil. An abnormal motherhood, overanxious and under-wise, hovers the child to its harm, and turns it out defenceless to the worst of evils. This is known to millions and millions personally. Only very lately have we thought to consider it generally. And not yet do we see that it is not the fault of the individual mother, but of her economic status. Because of our abnormal sex-development, the whole field has become something of an offence﻿—a thing to be hidden and ignored, passed over without remark or explanation. Hence this amazing paradox of mothers ashamed of motherhood, unable to explain it, and﻿—measure this well﻿—lying to their children about the primal truths of life﻿—mothers lying to their own children about motherhood!

===Chinese===
一个像白昼一样为人熟知、而其恶果又令人难以置信的具体事例，便是母亲在性关系问题上对子女的态度。除极少数例外，母亲对女儿毫不预警、毫不预说生活将带给她什么，于是听任天真与无知，在无穷无尽的世代里继续制造疾病、罪愆与痛苦。正常的母职（motherhood）明智而有效地守护自己的幼者免于邪恶。反常的母职，过分焦虑而智慧不足，把孩护在翼下以至于害了它，又把它毫无防卫地推到最坏的邪恶面前。这是千千万万人亲身知道的事。只是到了最近，我们才想到一般地考察它。而我们至今仍未看到：这不是个体母亲的过错，而是她的经济地位（economic status）的过错。由于我们反常的性发展，这整个领域已变成了某种可嫌之物——一件要遮掩、要无视、要不经说明便绕开的东西。于是便有了这个骇人的悖论：母亲们以母职为耻，无法解释它，而且——请好好掂量这一点——就生命的原初真理向自己的孩子撒谎——母亲们就母职向自己的孩子撒谎！

===Original===
The pressure under which this is done is an economic one. The girl must marry: else how live? The prospective husband prefers the girl to know nothing. He is the market, the demand. She is the supply. And with the best intentions the mother serves her child’s economic advantage by preparing her for the market. This is an excellent instance. It is common. It is most evil. It is plainly traceable to our sexuo-economic relation.

===Chinese===
迫使人们这样做的压力，是一种经济压力。女孩必须出嫁：不然何以谋生？未来的丈夫宁愿女孩一无所知。他就是市场，就是需求。她则是供给。于是母亲怀着最好的用意，通过把女儿备置妥当以应市场，来服务于孩子的经济利益。这是一个绝佳的例子。它普遍。它极其邪恶。它可以明白地追溯到我们的性-经济关系。

===Original===
Another instance of so grossly unjust, so palpable, so general an evil that it has occasionally aroused some protest even from our dull consciousness is this: the enforced attitude of the woman toward marriage. To the young girl, as has been previously stated, marriage is the one road to fortune, to life. She is born highly specialized as a female: she is carefully educated and trained to realize in all ways her sex-limitations and her sex-advantages. What she has to gain even as a child is largely gained by feminine tricks and charms. Her reading, both in history and fiction, treats of the same position for women; and romance and poetry give it absolute predominance. Pictorial art, music, the drama, society, everything, tells her that she is *she*, and that all depends on whom she marries. Where young boys plan for what they will achieve and attain, young girls plan for whom they will achieve and attain. Little Ellie and her swan’s nest among the reeds is a familiar illustration. It is the lover on the red roan steed she planned for. It is Lancelot riding through the sheaves that called the Lady from her loom at Shalott: “he” is the coming world.

===Chinese===
另一桩如此蛮横不公、如此刺目、如此普遍，以至于偶尔连我们迟钝的意识也会被激起些许抗议的邪恶，便是：女人对婚姻被强制的态度。如前所述，对年轻女子而言，婚姻是通往财富、通往生活的唯一道路。她生来便是一个高度专门化了的雌性：她被精心地教育、训练，去在一切方面体认自己的性限制与性优势。连她在孩提时代所要赢得的东西，也大半靠女性的伎俩与娇媚赢得。她的读物，无论史著还是小说，讲述的都是女人同样的位置；传奇与诗歌更赋予它绝对的统治地位。绘画、音乐、戏剧、社交界，一切的一切，都告诉她：她是「她」，而一切系于她嫁给什么人。少年们盘算他们将成就什么、获致什么，少女们盘算她们为谁而成就、为谁而获致。小埃莉（Little Ellie）和她芦苇丛中的天鹅窝，是一个人们熟知的例证。她所盘算的，是骑着红沙毛骏马前来的情人。是穿行于麦捆间的兰斯洛特（Lancelot），把沙洛特女郎（the Lady of Shalott）从她的织机旁唤走：「他」，就是那将临的世界。

===Original===
With such a prospect as this before her; with an organization specially developed to this end; with an education adding every weight of precept and example, of wisdom and virtue, to the natural instincts; with a social environment the whole machinery of which is planned to give the girl a chance to see and to be seen, to provide her with “opportunities”; and with all the pressure of personal advantage and self-interest added to the sex-instinct﻿—what one would logically expect is a society full of desperate and eager husband-hunters, regarded with popular approval.

===Chinese===
面前摆着这样一幅前景；有着一副为此目的而特别发展起来的机体；受着一种把训诫与榜样、智慧与德性的一切分量都加诸自然本能之上的教育；处身于一个其全部机器都为让女孩有机会去看与被看、为她提供「机会」而设计的社会环境（social environment）之中；再加上个人的好处与自利的全部压力都叠加于性本能之上——按逻辑推想，人们所期待的，该是一个充满孤注一掷而热切的猎夫者（husband-hunter）的社会，而且为社会舆论所嘉许。

===Original===
Not at all! Marriage is the woman’s proper sphere, her divinely ordered place, her natural end. It is what she is born for, what she is trained for, what she is exhibited for. It is, moreover, her means of honorable livelihood and advancement. *But*﻿—she must not even look as if she wanted it! She must not turn her hand over to get it. She must sit passive as the seasons go by, and her “chances” lessen with each year. Think of the strain on a highly sensitive nervous organism to have so much hang on one thing, to see the possibility of attaining it grow less and less yearly, and to be forbidden to take any step toward securing it! This she must bear with dignity and grace to the end.

===Chinese===
全然不是！婚姻是女人正当的领域，是她神定的位置，是她自然的归宿。她生来为它，受训为它，被展示也为它。而且，它还是她体面谋生与进身的手段。但是——她甚至不可以显得想要它！她不可以为得到它而抬一抬手。她必须消极枯坐，任凭一季一季过去，任凭她的「机会」逐年减少。想想一个高度敏感的神经机体所承受的紧张：一切都系于这一件事，眼看达成它的可能逐年递减，却被禁止为求取它而迈出任何一步！而这，她必须始终以尊严与风度承受到底。

===Original===
To what end? To the end that, if she does not succeed in being chosen, she becomes a thing of mild popular contempt, a human being with no further place in life save as an attachée, a dependant upon more fortunate relatives, an old maid. The open derision and scorn with which unmarried women used to be treated is lessening each year in proportion to their advance in economic independence. But it is not very long since the popular proverb, “Old maids lead apes in hell,” was in common use; since unwelcome lovers urged their suit with the awful argument that they might be the last askers; since the hapless lady in the wood prayed for a husband, and, when the owl answered, “Who? who?” cried, “Anybody, good Lord!” There is still a pleasant ditty afloat as to the “Three Old Maids of Lynn,” who did not marry when they could, and could not when they would.

===Chinese===
为了什么下场？下场便是：倘若她未能成功地被选中，她便成了一件遭大众温和轻蔑的东西，一个在生活中再无位置的人——顶多做个随附者（attachée）、依附于更幸运的亲眷的依附者（dependant），一个老处女（old maid）。对未婚妇女曾经施予的公然的嘲弄与鄙夷，正随着她们朝经济独立（economic independence）的推进而逐年减轻。但那并不久远：那流行的谚语「老处女在地狱里牵猴子」还曾被普遍使用；不受欢迎的求爱者还曾用「自己也许是最后一个开口的人」这种可怕的说辞逼催婚事；树林里那位倒霉的女子还曾祈求一个丈夫，而当猫头鹰应声「谁？谁？」时，她喊道：「谁都行，好上帝！」至今还流传着一支关于「林恩的三位老处女」的轻快小调——她们能嫁时不嫁，想嫁时不能嫁了。

===Original===
The cruel and absurd injustice of blaming the girl for not getting what she is allowed no effort to obtain seems unaccountable; but it becomes clear when viewed in connection with the sexuo-economic relation. Although marriage is a means of livelihood, it is not honest employment where one can offer one’s labor without shame, but a relation where the support is given outright, and enforced by law in return for the functional service of the woman, the “duties of wife and mother.” Therefore no honorable woman can ask for it. It is not only that the natural feminine instinct is to retire, as that of the male is to advance, but that, because marriage means support, a woman must not ask a man to support her. It is economic beggary as well as a false attitude from a sex point of view.

===Chinese===
责怪女孩得不到那不允许她努力去争取的东西——这种残酷而荒谬的不公，看来无从解释；但联系性-经济关系来看，它便清楚了。婚姻虽是一种谋生手段，却并非那种可以毫无羞耻地献出自己劳动的正经职业，而是一种关系：在其中，供养被径直给予，并以法律强制维系，作为对女人的功能性服务——「为人妻为人母的职责」——的回报。因此，没有一个体面的女人能开口求它。这不仅是因为女性的自然本能是退让，一如男性的本能是进取；更是因为，既然婚姻意味着供养，女人便不可开口求男人供养她。这既是经济上的乞讨，也是性的观点上的一种虚伪姿态。

===Original===
Observe the ingenious cruelty of the arrangement. It is just as humanly natural for a woman as for a man to want wealth. But, when her wealth is made to come through the same channels as her love, she is forbidden to ask for it by her own sex-nature and by business honor. Hence the millions of mismade marriages with “anybody, good Lord!” Hence the million broken hearts which must let all life pass, unable to make any attempt to stop it. Hence the many “maiden aunts,” elderly sisters and daughters, unattached women everywhere, who are a burden on their male relatives and society at large. This is changing for the better, to be sure, but changing only through the advance of economic independence for women. A “bachelor maid” is a very different thing from “an old maid.”

===Chinese===
且看这一安排的精巧的残忍。想要财富，女人同男人一样合乎人性地自然。但当她的财富被安排得与她的爱情经由同一条渠道而来时，她的性本性与商业信誉便双双禁止她开口索求。于是有了千百万桩以「谁都行，好上帝！」作结的错配婚姻。于是有了千百万颗破碎的心，只能眼睁睁看着一生消逝，无力做任何挽回的尝试。于是有了那许多「未婚姑母」、上了年纪的姊妹与女儿、处处可见的无所依托的女人，成为男性亲眷与整个社会的负担。这确乎正在好转，但只是经由妇女经济独立的进展才好转的。「独身小姐」（bachelor maid）与「老处女」已是截然不同的两回事。

===Original===
This, then, is the reason for the Andromeda position of the possibly-to-be-married young woman, and for the ridicule and reproach meted out to her. Since women are viewed wholly as creatures of sex even by one another, and since everything is done to add to their young powers of sex-attraction; since they are marriageable solely on this ground, unless, indeed, “a fortune” has been added to their charms﻿—failure to marry is held a clear proof of failure to attract, a lack of sex-value. And, since they have no other value, save in a low order of domestic service, they are quite naturally despised. What else is the creature good for, failing in the functions for which it was created? The scorn of male and female alike falls on this sexless thing: she is a human failure.

===Chinese===
这便是那位可能出嫁的年轻女子何以处于安德洛墨达（Andromeda）般的境地、何以被施以嘲弄与责难的缘故。既然女人们被完全看作性的造物——连她们彼此也这样看——既然一切都为了增添她们年轻的性吸引（sex-attraction）之力而被做着；既然她们单凭这一点便可婚配——除非当真有一笔「财产」加在了她们的妩媚之上——那么嫁不出去便被认作未能吸引的明证、性价值的缺失。而既然她们再无别的价值——顶多在低等次的家内仆役（domestic service）中还有一点——她们自然而然便遭轻蔑。一个造物，在它受造所司的职能上失败了，还能有什么别的用处？男性与女性的鄙夷一齐落在这无性的东西身上：她是人的失败品。

===Original===
It is not strange, therefore, though just as pitiful﻿—this long chapter of patient, voiceless, dreary misery in the lives of women; and it is not strange, either, to see the marked and steady change in opinion that follows the development of other faculties in woman besides those of sex. Now that she is a person as well as a female, filling economic relation to society, she is welcomed and accepted as a human creature, and need not marry the wrong man for her bread and butter. So sharp is the reaction from this unlovely yoke that there is a limited field of life today wherein women choose not to marry, preferring what they call “their independence,”﻿—a newborn, hard-won, dear-bought independence. That any living woman should prefer it to home and husband, to love and motherhood, throws a fierce light on what women must have suffered for lack of freedom before.

===Chinese===
因此，女人生活中这漫长的、隐忍而无声的凄惨篇章，虽同样令人怜悯，却不足为怪；同样不足为怪的，是看到随着女人在性之外的别种官能的发展，舆论也随之发生显著而稳定的变化。如今她既是雌性又是人，与社会结着经济关系（economic relation），她便作为一个人被欢迎、被接纳，再不必为着面包黄油去嫁错人。对这道不美的枷锁的反拨是如此剧烈，以至于今天的生活中已有一个有限的领域，其中的女人选择不嫁，宁取她们所谓的「独立」——一种新生的、来之不易的、以高价换得的独立。一个活生生的女人竟宁愿要它而不要家与丈夫、不要爱情与母职——这一事实，惨烈地照见了从前女人因缺乏自由必定受过什么。

===Original===
This tendency need not be feared, however. It is merely a reaction, and a most natural one. It will pass as naturally, as more and more women become independent, when marriage is not the price of liberty. The fear exhibited that women generally, once fully independent, will not marry, is proof of how well it has been known that only dependence forced them to marriage as it was. There will be needed neither bribe nor punishment to force women to true marriage with independence.

===Chinese===
然而这一倾向无须畏惧。它不过是一种反拨，而且是最自然的反拨。随着越来越多的女人变得独立，随着婚姻不再是自由的代价，它也会自然而然地消退。人们表现出的那种恐惧——女人一旦完全独立便普遍不肯结婚了——恰恰证明了一件早已为人深知的事：从前正是依附把她们逼进了那样的婚姻。届时既无需利诱，也无需惩罚，女性自会带着独立走入真正的婚姻。

===Original===
Along this line it is most interesting to mark the constant struggle between natural instinct and natural law, and social habit and social law, through all our upward course. Beginning with the natural functions and instincts of sex, holding her great position as selector of the best among competing males, woman’s beautiful work is to improve the race by right marriage. The feeling by which this is accomplished, growing finer as we become more civilized, develops into that wide, deep, true, and lasting love which is the highest good to individual human beings. Following its current, we have always reverenced and admired “true love”; and our romances, from the earliest times, abound in praise of the princess who marries the page or prisoner, venerating the selective power in woman, choosing “the right man” for his own sake. Directly against this runs the countercurrent, resulting in the marriage of convenience, a thing which the true inner heart of the world has always hated. Young Lochinvar is not an eternal hero for nothing. The personified type of a great social truth is sure of a long life. The poor young hero, handsome, brave, good, but beset with difficulties, stands ever against the wealth and power of the bad man. The woman is pulled hither and thither between them, and the poor hero wins in the end. That he is heaped with honor and riches, after all, merely signifies our recognition that he is the higher good. This is better than a sun-myth. It is a race-myth, and true as truth.

===Chinese===
沿着这条线索，纵观我们整个向上的历程，最耐人寻味的，莫过于自然本能与自然法则同社会习惯与社会法则之间那不断的争斗。以性的自然功能与本能为起点，据有着在竞争的雄性中择优而取的伟大位置，女人那美好的职分，便是借正当的婚姻来改良种族。成就此事的那种情感，随着我们的日益文明而愈趋精纯，发展成那种宽广、深沉、真实而持久的爱情——它是个体的人最高的善。顺着这股水流，我们一向尊崇并赞美「真爱」；而我们的传奇故事，自远古以来，便满是对嫁给侍童或囚徒的公主的颂扬——它礼敬女人身上的选择之力，为那个男人本身的缘故择取「对的人」。与它迎面而行的，是一股逆流，其结果是权宜婚姻（marriage of convenience）——那是世界真正的内心一向憎恶的东西。青年洛钦瓦尔（Lochinvar）之成为永恒的英雄，并非无缘无故。一个重大社会真理的人格化典型，必享长久的生命。那贫穷的年轻英雄，英俊、勇敢、善良，却困难重重，永远对抗着坏人的财富与权势。女人被他们拉来扯去，而贫穷的英雄终告胜利。他到头来被荣誉与财富所堆叠，这只不过表明我们承认：他是更高的善。这比太阳神话更好。这是一个种族神话，而且真实如真理。

===Original===
So we have it among us in life today, endlessly elaborated and weakened by profuse detail, as is the nature of that life, but there yet. The girl who marries the rich old man or the titled profligate is condemned by the popular voice; and the girl who marries the poor young man, and helps him live his best, is still approved by the same great arbiter. And yet why should we blame the woman for pursuing her vocation? Since marriage is her only way to get money, why should she not try to get money in that way? Why cast the weight of all self-interest on the “practical” plane so solidly against the sex-interest of the individual and of the race? The mercenary marriage is a perfectly natural consequence of the economic dependence of women.

===Chinese===
于是，这一切今天仍存在于我们的生活之中，虽被繁冗的细节无尽地铺陈、稀释——这本是那种生活的本性——却依然在那里。嫁给有钱老头或有爵位的浪荡子的姑娘，会被公众的舆论所谴责；而嫁给贫穷青年、助他活出最好的人生的姑娘，仍为同一位伟大的仲裁者所嘉许。然而，我们为什么要责怪女人追求她的天职？既然婚姻是她弄到金钱的唯一途径，她为什么不该循此途径去弄到金钱？为什么把「实际的」层面上全部自利的分量，如此坚实地压在个体与种族的性利益的对立面？贪利的婚姻，正是妇女经济依附（economic dependence）的一个再自然不过的后果。

===Original===
On the other hand, note the effect of this dependence upon men. As the excessive sex-distinction and economic dependence of women increase, so do the risk and difficulty of marriage increase, so is marriage deferred and avoided, to the direct injury of both sexes and society at large. In simpler relations, in the country, wherever women have a personal value in economic relation as well as a feminine value in sex-relation, an early marriage is an advantage. The young farmer gets a profitable servant when he marries. The young business man gets nothing of the kind﻿—a pretty girl, a charming girl, ready for “wifehood and motherhood”﻿—so far as her health holds out﻿—but having no economic value whatever. She is merely a consumer, and he must wait till he can “afford to marry.” These are instances frequent everywhere, and familiar to us all, of the palpable effects in common life of our sexuo-economic relation.

===Chinese===
另一方面，且注意这种依附对男人的效应。随着妇女过度的性别区分与经济依附的加深，婚姻的风险与困难也随之增加，婚姻便被推迟、被回避，直接损害两性与整个社会。在较简单的关系中，在乡村，凡是女人在经济关系中兼有个人价值、在性关系中具有女性价值的地方，早婚便是一种有利。年轻的农夫结婚，得到一个有进益的劳力。年轻的商人却得不到这样的东西——一个漂亮的姑娘，一个迷人的姑娘，随时可以「为妻为母」——只要她的健康撑得住——却没有任何经济价值。她纯粹是一个消费者，于是他必须等待，等到他「养得起家」再结婚。这些事例处处频繁可见、人人熟知，都是我们的性-经济关系在日常生活中显明效应的例证。

===Original===
If there is one unmixed evil in human life, it is that known to us in all ages, and popularly called “the social evil,” consisting of promiscuous and temporary sex-relations. The inherent wrong in these relations is sociological before it is legal or moral. The recognition by the moral sense of a given thing as wrong requires that it be wrong, to begin with. A thing is not wrong merely because it is called so. The wrongness of this form of sex-relation in an advanced social state rests solidly on natural laws. In the evolution of better and better means of reproducing the species, a longer period of infancy was developed. This longer period of infancy required longer care, and it was accordingly developed that the best care during this time was given by both parents. This induced a more permanent mating. And the more permanent mating, bound together by the common interests and duties, developed higher psychic attributes in the parents by use, in the children by heredity. That is why society is right in demanding of its constituent individuals the virtue of chastity, the sanctity of marriage. Society is perfectly right, because social evolution is as natural a process as individual evolution; and the permanent parent is proven an advantageous social factor. But social evolution, deep, unconscious, slow, is one thing; and self-conscious, loud-voiced society is another.

===Chinese===
倘若人类生活中有一桩不掺别的成分的纯粹之恶，那就是历来为我们所知、俗称「社会之恶」（the social evil）的东西——由混乱而临时的性关系所构成。这些关系固有的错，首先是社会学上的，其次才轮到法律与道德。道德意识要认某事为错，先须它本来就是错的。一件事并非因为被称作错的便是错的。在一个先进的社会状态中，这种形式的性关系之错，稳稳地奠基于自然法则之上。在繁殖物种的手段愈益精良的演化中，一段更长的幼年期被发展出来。这更长的幼年期要求更长的照料，于是又相应地发展出：这段时间里最好的照料由双亲共同给予。这引向了更持久的配偶结合。而更持久的配偶结合，由共同的利益与义务联结在一起，又经由使用在双亲身上、经由遗传在儿女身上，发展出更高的心理属性。这正是社会有权要求其组成个体具有贞洁之德、视婚姻为神圣的原因。社会是完全正当的，因为社会演化同个体演化一样，是一个自然过程；而持久的亲代已被证明是一个有利的社会因子。但社会演化——深沉的、无意识的、缓慢的——是一回事；自觉而喧哗的社会则是另一回事。

===Original===
The deepest forces of nature have tended to evolve pure, lasting, monogamous marriage in the human race. But our peculiar arrangement of feeding one sex by the other has tended to produce a very different thing, and has produced it. In no other animal species is the female economically dependent on the male. In no other animal species is the sex-relation for sale. A coincidence. Where, on the one hand, every condition of life tends to develop sex in women, to crush out the power and the desire for economic production and exchange, and to develop also the age-long habit of seeking all earthly good at a man’s hands and of making but one return; where, on the other hand, man inherits the excess in sex-energy, and is never blamed for exercising it, and where he develops also the age-long habit of taking what he wants from women, for whose helpless acquiescence he makes an economic return﻿—what should naturally follow? Precisely what has followed. We live in a world of law, and humanity is no exception to it. We have produced a certain percentage of females with inordinate sex-tendencies and inordinate greed for material gain. We have produced a certain percentage of males with inordinate sex-tendencies and a cheerful willingness to pay for their gratification. And, as the percentage of such men is greater than the percentage of such women, we have worked out most evil methods of supplying the demand. But always in the healthy social heart we have known that it was wrong, a racial wrong, productive of all evil. Being a man’s world, it was quite inevitable that he should blame woman for their mutual misdoing. There is reason in it, too. Bad as he is, he is only seeking gratification natural in kind, though abnormal in degree. She is not only in some cases doing this, but in most cases showing the falseness of the deed by doing it for hire﻿—physical falsehood﻿—a sin against nature.

===Chinese===
自然最深层的诸力量，一直倾向于在人类中演化出纯洁、持久的一夫一妻制婚姻。但我们这套以一性供养另一性的独特安排，却倾向于造就一件截然不同的东西，而且确已造就了它。没有任何别的动物物种，雌性在经济上依附于雄性。没有任何别的动物物种，性关系是被出卖的。不过是巧合而已。一方面，生活的每一个条件都在倾向于发展女人的性，扑灭她经济生产与交换的能力和欲望，还培养出那源远流长的习惯——向男人之手索取尘世的一切好处，而只以一种回报相还；另一方面，男人承继着性能量（sex-energy）的过剩，行使它从不受到责难，还培养出同样源远流长的习惯——从女人身上取走他想要的东西，为她无助的顺从而付出经济上的回报——如此，自然会跟着发生什么？恰恰就是已经发生了的。我们生活在一个法则的世界里，人类对它并无例外。我们造就了一定比例的雌性，带着无度的性倾向与对物质所得的无度贪婪。我们造就了一定比例的雄性，带着无度的性倾向与为求满足而甘愿付账的愉快意愿。而由于这种男人的比例大于这种女人的比例，我们便运转出种种最邪恶的方法来供给这种需求。但在健康的社会心底，我们始终知道它是错的，一种种族性的错，滋生一切邪恶。既然这是男人的世界，他把共同的罪过归咎于女人，便是十分难免的事。这也不无道理。他尽管坏，所寻求的满足在种类上毕竟是自然的，只是程度反常。而她，不只是在某些情况下也在这样做，而是在多数情况下，以受雇而做显出了这行为本身的虚假——身体上的谎言——一种违逆自然的罪。

===Original===
It is a true instinct that revolts against obtaining bread by use of the sex-functions. Why, then, are we so content to do this in marriage? Legally and religiously, we say that it is right; but in its reactionary effect on the parties concerned and on society at large it is wrong. The physical and psychical effects are evil, though modified by our belief that it is right. The physical and psychical effects of prostitution were still evil when the young girls of Babylon earned their dowries thereby in the temple of Bela, and thought it right. What we think and feel alters the moral quality of an act in our consciousness as we do it, but does not alter its subsequent effect. We justify and approve the economic dependence of women upon the sex-relation in marriage. We condemn it unsparingly out of marriage. We follow it with our blame and scorn up to the very doors of marriage﻿—the mercenary bride﻿—but think no harm of the mercenary wife, filching her husband’s pockets in the night. Love sanctifies it, we say: love must go with it.

===Chinese===
对借性功能换取面包心怀憎恶，这是一种真实的本能。那么，为什么我们在婚姻中这样做时，却又如此心安理得？在法律上与宗教上，我们说这是对的；但就它对当事人与整个社会的反作用而言，它是错的。其身体与心理的效应是恶的，尽管被我们「这是对的」这一信念所缓和。当巴比伦的少女们在贝拉（Bela）神庙里以此挣得嫁妆、并认为这是对的时候，卖淫的身体与心理效应也依然是恶的。我们所想与所感的，只改变行为发生时它在我们的意识中的道德性质，却改变不了它随后的效应。我们对婚姻中女人借性关系而受养的经济依附，予以辩护和认可；对婚姻之外的它，则予以不留情的谴责。我们带着责难与鄙夷，一路追逼到婚姻的门前——那贪利的新娘——却对那贪利的妻子夜里从丈夫口袋里摸钱，不以为忤。爱情使它成圣，我们说：爱情必得与它同行。

===Original===
Love never yet went with self-interest. The deepest antagonism lies between them: they are diametrically opposed forces. In the beautiful progress of evolution we find constant opposition between the instincts and processes of self-preservation and the instinct and processes of race-preservation. From those early forms where birth brought death, as in the flowering aloe, the ephemeral mayfly, up to the highest glory of self-effacing love; these two forces work in opposition. We have tied them together. We have made the woman, the mother﻿—the very source of sacrifice through love﻿—get gain through love﻿—a hideous paradox. No wonder that our daily lives are full of the flagrant evils produced by this unnatural state. No wonder that men turn with loathing from the kind of women they have made.

===Chinese===
爱情从不曾与自利同行。二者之间横着最深的对立：它们是截然相反的力量。在演化那美好的进程中，我们看到自我保存（self-preservation）的本能与过程同种族保存（race-preservation）的本能与过程之间不断的对立。从那些生育即带来死亡的早期形态——如开花即亡的芦荟、朝生暮死的蜉蝣——直到自我隐没之爱的至高荣光，这两种力量始终相抗而行。我们却把它们捆在了一起。我们使女人、使母亲——那因爱而牺牲的源头本身——经由爱而获利——一个骇人的悖论。难怪我们的日常生活充满这一反常状态所生的昭彰邪恶。难怪男人们满怀憎恶地转过脸去，离开他们亲手造就的那种女人。

## VI / 六

===Original===
The peculiar combination of functions which we are studying has not only an immediate effect on individuals through sex-action, and through the sex-affected individuals upon society, but also an effect upon society through economic action, and through the economically affected society upon the individual.

===Chinese===
我们所研究的这种奇特的功能组合，不仅经由性活动而对个体产生直接的作用、再经由受性影响的个体而作用于社会，而且还经由经济活动而作用于社会、再经由受经济影响的社会而作用于个体。

===Original===
The economic aspect of the question brings it prominently forward today as influencing not only our private health and happiness and the processes of reproduction, but our public health and happiness and the processes of social economics as well. Society is confronted in this age with most pressing problems in economics, and we need the fullest understanding of the factors involved. These problems are almost wholly social rather than physical, and concern not the capacity of a given society to produce and distribute enough wealth for its maintenance, but some maladjustment of internal processes which checks that production and distribution, and develops such irregular and morbid processes of innutrition, malnutrition, and over-nutrition as continually to injure the health and activity of the social organism. Our difficulty about wealth is not in getting it out of the earth, but in getting it away from one another. We have phenomena before us in the development of social economic relations analogous to those accompanying our development in sex-relation.

===Chinese===
这一问题的经济方面，使它今天凸显于前：它不仅影响我们的私人健康与幸福以及生殖的诸过程，也影响我们的公共健康与幸福以及社会经济的诸过程。在这个时代，社会面临着最为紧迫的经济学问题，我们需要对所涉种种要素有最充分的理解。这些问题几乎完全是社会性的而非自然性的，所关涉的并非某一社会为维持自身而生产和分配足够财富的能力，而是内部过程的某种失调——它阻碍着那种生产与分配，并滋生出营养不足、营养不良与营养过度之类反常而病态的过程，持续损害着社会有机体的健康与活力。我们在财富上的困难，不在于把它从土地里采出来，而在于把它从彼此手中拿开。在我们面前，社会性经济关系的发展中所呈现的种种现象，与伴随我们的性关系发展而来的那些现象颇相类似。

===Original===
In the original constituents of society, the human animal in its primitive state, economic processes were purely individual. The amount of food obtained by a given man bore direct relation to his own personal exertions. Other men were to him merely undesirable competitors for the same goods; and, the fewer these competitors were, the more goods remained for him. Therefore, he killed as many of his rivals as possible. Given a certain supply of needed food, as the edible beasts or fruits in a forest, and a certain number of individuals to get this food, each by his own exertions, it follows that, the more numerous the individuals, the less food to be obtained by each; and, conversely, the fewer the individuals, the more food to be obtained by each. Wherefore, the primitive savage slew his fellow-man at sight, on good economic grounds. This is the extreme of individual competition, perfectly logical, and, in its time, economically right. That time is forever past. The basic condition of human life is union; the organic social relation, the interchange of functional service, wherein the individual is most advantaged, not by his own exertions for his own goods, but by the exchange of his exertions with the exertions of others for goods produced by them together. We are not treating here of any communistic theory as to the equitable division of the wealth produced, but of a clear truth in social economics—that wealth is a social product. Whatever one may believe as to what should be done with the wealth of the world, no one can deny that the production of this wealth requires the combined action of many individuals. From the simplest combination of strength that enables many men to overcome the mammoth or to lift the stone—an achievement impossible to one alone—to the subtle and complex interchange of highly specialized skilled labor which makes possible our modern house; the progress of society rests upon the increasing collectivity of human labor.

===Chinese===
在社会的原始构成成分中，在原始状态的人类动物那里，经济过程纯属个人。某个人所获得的食物数量，与他本人的劳作直接相关。他人于他不过是争夺同样财物的可厌竞争者；这样的竞争者越少，留给他的财物便越多。因此，他尽可能多地杀死自己的对手。设若有定量的必需食物，如森林中可食的兽类或果实，又有一定数目的个体各凭自身劳作去获取这些食物，那么顺理成章：个体越多，每人所能得的食物越少；反之，个体越少，每人所能得的食物越多。是以，原始的野蛮人一见到同类便将其杀死，这有着充分的经济理由。这是个人竞争的极端形式，完全合乎逻辑，且在其时代在经济上是正当的。那个时代已一去不返。人类生活的基本条件是联合；是有机的社会关系，是职能服务的交换——个体由此获得最大的利益，不是凭他为个人财物而作的独自劳作，而是凭他的劳作与他人的劳作相交换，以得他们共同生产的财物。我们在此处理的并非任何关于所产财富如何公平分配的共产主义理论，而是社会经济学中一个明白的真理——财富是一种社会产物。无论一个人对世界财富应当如何处置持何种看法，谁都不能否认：这种财富的生产需要许多个人的联合行动。从使许多人得以制服猛犸或抬起巨石的最简单的力量组合——这是单独一人无法企就的功业——到使我们的现代宅邸成为可能的高度专门化熟练劳动之间精微而复杂的交换；社会的进步，皆奠基于人类劳动日益增长的集体性。

===Original===
The evolution of organic life goes on in geometrical progression: cells combine, and form organs; organs combine, and form organisms; organisms combine, and form organizations. Society is an organization. Society is the fourth power of the cell. It is composed of individual animals of genus *homo*, living in organic relation. The course of social evolution is the gradual establishment of organic relation between individuals, and this organic relation rests on purely economic grounds. In the simplest combination of primordial cells the force that drew and held them together was that of economic necessity. It profited them to live in combination. Those that did so survived, and those that did not perished. So with the appearance of the most elaborate organisms: it profited them to become a complex bundle of members and organs in indivisible relation. A creature so constructed survived, where the same amount of living matter unorganized would have perished. And so it is, literally and exactly, in a complex society, with all its elaborate specialization of individuals in arts and crafts, trades and professions. A society so constructed survives, where the same number of living beings, unorganized, would perish. The specialization of labor and exchange of product in a social body is identical in its nature with the specialization and exchange of function in an individual body. This process, on orderly lines of evolution, involves the gradual subordination of individual effort for individual good to the collective effort for the collective good—not from any so-called "altruism," but from the economic necessities of the case. It is as natural, as "selfish," for society so to live, the individual citizens working together for the social good, as for one's own body to live by the hands and feet, teeth and eyes, heart and lungs, working together for the individual good. Social evolution tends to an increasing specialization in structure and function, and to an increasing interdependence of the component parts, with a correlative decrease through disuse of the once valuable process of individual struggle for success; and this is based absolutely on the advantage to the individual as well as to the social body.

===Chinese===
有机生命的演化以几何级数进行：细胞结合而成器官；器官结合而成有机体；有机体结合而成组织体。社会是一个组织体。社会是细胞的四次幂。它由生活在有机关系之中的人属（genus *homo*）个体动物组成。社会演进的历程，便是个体之间有机关系的逐步确立，而这种有机关系奠基于纯粹的经济理由。在最简单的原始细胞的结合中，把它们吸引并维系在一起的力量，正是经济必需的力量。结合而居，于它们有利。这样做的存活了下来，不这样做的归于灭亡。及至最精巧的有机体出现，情形亦然：成为由肢体与器官以不可分的关系构成的复杂集合，于它们有利。如此构造的造物存活了下来，而同样数量的生命物质若不经组织，早已灭亡。在一个复杂的社会中——连同其中个体在技艺与工艺、行业与职业上的一切精巧专门化——情形也恰恰如此，分毫不爽。如此构造的社会得以存活，而同样数目的生命存在若不经组织，便会灭亡。社会体中劳动的专门化与产品的交换，在本性上与个体身体中功能的专门化与交换完全相同。这一过程沿着有序的演化路线，包含着为个人之善的个人努力对为集体之善的集体努力的逐渐从属——不是出于任何所谓的「利他主义」，而是出于情势的经济必需。社会如此生活、个体公民为社会的善而共同劳作，正如人自己的身体靠手与脚、齿与眼、心与肺为个体的善而共同运作一样，是同样自然的，也是同样「自私」的。社会演进趋向于结构与功能上日益增长的专门化，趋向于各组成部分日益增长的相互依存，并伴随着曾经宝贵的个体成功斗争过程因废弃而相应衰减；而这绝对地奠基于它对个体与社会体双方的利益。

===Original===
But, as we study this process of development, noting with admiration the progressive changes in human relation, the new functions, the extended structure, the increase of sensation in the socialized individuals with its enormous possibilities of joy and healthful sensitiveness to pain, we are struck by the visible presence of some counter-force, acting against the normal development and producing most disadvantageous effects. As in our orderly progress in social sex-development we are checked by the tenacious hold of rudimentary impulses artificially maintained by false conditions, so in our orderly progress in social economic development we see the same peculiar survival of rudimentary impulses, which should have been long since easily outgrown. It is no longer of advantage to the individual to struggle for his own gain at the expense of others: his gain now requires the coordinate efforts of these others; yet he continues so to struggle.

===Chinese===
但是，当我们研究这一发展过程，赞叹地注意到人类关系中的种种进步变化、那些新的功能、那扩展了的结构、那些社会化的个体感受性的增长——连同它带来的欢乐的巨大可能和对疼痛的健康敏感——我们却为某种反向力量的显在存在所震动：它逆着正常的发展而动，产生着最为有害的效应。正如在我们社会性发展（sex-development）的有序进程中，我们受制于虚假条件人为维持的残遗冲动的顽固把持；在我们社会经济发展的有序进程中，我们同样看到这种残遗冲动的奇特存续——它们本应早被轻易地长过而抛在身后。以他人为代价而争一己之得，于个人已不再有利：他的所得如今需要这些他人的协同努力；然而他仍旧这样争斗下去。

===Original===
In this lack of adjustment between the individual and the social interest lies our economic trouble. An illustration of this may be seen in the manufacture of prepared foods. This is a process impossible to the individual singly, and of great advantage to the individual in collective relation—a perfectly natural economic process, advantageous in proportion to the amount and quality of the food manufactured. This we constantly find accompanied by a morbid process of dilution and adulteration, by which society is injured, in order that the individual concerned in the manufacture may be benefited. This is as though one of the organs of the body—the liver, for instance—should deliberately weaken or poison its quota of secretion, in order that by giving less it might retain more, and become large and fat individually. An organ can do so, does do so; but such action is morbid action, and constitutes disease. The body is injured, weakened, destroyed, and so ultimately the organ perishes also. It is a false conception of gain, and the falsehood lies in not recognizing the true relation between individual and social interests. This failure to recognize or, at least, to act up to a recognition of social interests, owing to the disproportionate pressure of individual interests, is the underlying cause of our economic distress. As society is composed of individuals, we must look to them for the action causing these morbid social processes; and, as individuals act under the pressure of conditions, we must look to the conditions affecting the individuals for the causes of their action.

===Chinese===
我们的经济疾患，正出在个人利益与社会利益之间的这种失调。这一点可以在预制食品的制造中得到说明。这是一个单独的个体所无法进行的过程，也是处于集体关系中的个体大受其惠的过程——一个完全自然的经济过程，其利益与所制食品的数量和品质成正比。然而我们不断地发现，它伴随着一个掺稀与掺假的病态过程：制造中的相关个体为了自己得利，社会由此受损。这就好比身体的一个器官——比如肝脏——故意削弱或毒化它那一份分泌，以便少给出一些而多留下一些，使自己独自长得肥大。器官能够这样做，也确实这样做；但这样的行动是病态的行动，构成的便是疾病。身体受到损害、削弱、毁坏，那个器官最终也归于灭亡。这是一种关于获益的虚假观念，其虚假就在于不曾认识到个人利益与社会利益之间的真实关系。由于个人利益的失衡压力，这种对社会利益的不认识——或至少是不依照认识去行动——便是我们经济困厄的深层原因。社会由个体组成，我们必须到他们身上去寻找造成这些病态社会过程的行动；而个体又是在条件的压力下行动的，所以我们必须到影响个体的那些条件中去寻找他们行动的原因。

===Original===
In general, under social law, men develop right action; but some hidden spring seems to force them continually into wrong action. We have our hand upon this hidden spring in the sexuo-economic relation. If we had remained on an individual economic basis, the evil influence would have had far less ill effect; but, as we grow into the social economic relation, it increases with our civilization. The sex-relation is primarily and finally individual. It is a physical relation between individual bodies; and, while it may also extend to a psychical relation between individual souls, it does not become a social relation, though it does change its personal development to suit social needs.

===Chinese===
大体而言，在社会法则之下，人们发展出正当的行动；但似乎有某种暗藏的弹簧，不断地迫使他们做出错误的行动。在性-经济关系（sexuo-economic relation）中，我们的手正落在这根暗藏的弹簧上。倘若我们仍停留在个人经济的基础上，这种恶的影响本会远没有那么大的害处；但随着我们成长为社会性的经济关系，它也随我们的文明一同增长。性关系（sex-relation）就其本源与归宿而言都是个人的。它是各个身体之间的物质关系；纵然它也可以扩展为各个灵魂之间的心理关系，它并不因此成为社会关系，尽管它确实会改变自身个人的发展以适应社会的需要。

===Original===
In all its processes, to all its results, the sex-relation is personal, working through individuals upon individuals, and developing individual traits and characteristics, to the great advantage of society. The qualities developed by social relation are built into the race through the sex-relation, but the sex-relation itself is wholly personal. Our economic relation, on the contrary, though originally individual, becomes through social evolution increasingly collective. By combining the human sex-relation with the human economic relation, we have combined a permanently individual process with a progressively collective one. This involves a strain on both, which increases in direct proportion to our socialization, and, so far, has resulted in the ultimate destruction of the social organism acted upon by such irreconcilable forces.

===Chinese===
在其一切过程中，在其一切结果上，性关系都是个人的：它经由个体作用于个体，发展出个体的特质与品格，而大有利于社会。由社会关系发展出来的品质，经由性关系被筑进种族之中；但性关系本身完全是个人的。相反，我们的经济关系（economic relation）虽然原本是个人的，却经由社会演化而日益成为集体的。把人类的性关系与人类的经济关系相结合，我们便把一个恒久个人的过程与一个日益集体的过程结合在了一起。这给双方都带来了张力，这张力与我们的社会化程度成正比地增长，而迄今为止，它的结果便是受这些不可调和之力作用的社会有机体最终归于毁灭。

===Original===
As has been shown, this combination has affected the sex-relation of individuals by bringing into it a tendency to collectivism with economic advantage, best exhibited in our distinctive racial phenomenon of prostitution. On the other hand, it has affected the economic relation of society by bringing into it a tendency to individualism with sex-advantage, best exhibited in the frequent practice of sacrificing public good to personal gain, that the individual may thereby "support his family." We are so used to considering it the first duty of a man to support his family that it takes a very glaring instance of bribery and corruption in their interests to shake our conviction; but, as a sociological law, every phase of the prostitution of public service to private gain, from the degradation of the artist to the exploitation of the helpless unskilled laborer, marks a diseased social action. Our social status rests upon our common consent, common action, common submission to the common will. No individual interests can stand for a moment against the interests of the common weal, either when war demands the last sacrifice of individual property and life or when peace requires the absolute submission of individual property and life to common law—the fixed expression of the people's will. The maintenance of "law and order" involves the very spirit of socialism—the sinking of personal interest in common interest. All this rests upon the evolution of the social spirit, the keen sense of social duty, the conscientious fulfilment of social service; and it is here that the excessive individualism maintained by our sexuo-economic relation enters as a strong and increasingly disadvantageous social factor. We have dimly recognized the irreconcilability of the sex-relation with economic relations on both sides—in our sharp condemnation of making the sex-functions openly commercial, and in the drift toward celibacy in collective institutions. Bodies of men or women, actuated by the highest religious impulses, desiring to live nobly and to serve society, have always recognized something antagonistic in the sex-relation. They have thought it inherent in the relation itself, not seeing that it was the economic side which made it reactionary. Yet this action was practically admitted by the continued existence of communal societies where the sex-relation did exist, in an unacknowledged form, and without the element of economic exchange.
