It is admitted also by the noble and self-sacrificing devotion of married missionaries of the Protestant Church, who are supported by contributions. If the missionary were obliged to earn his wife's living and his own, he could do little mission work.

===Chinese===
如前所示，这一结合影响了个体之间的性关系：它给性关系带入了一种伴随经济利益的集体主义倾向，最显著地体现在我们独特的种族现象——卖淫之中。另一方面，它也影响了社会的经济关系：给它带入了一种伴随性利益的个人主义倾向，最显著地体现在那种牺牲公益以逐私利的惯常做法之中——个人由此得以「养活他的一家」。我们如此习惯于把养家视为男子的第一义务，以致必须有一桩触目的、为其家人利益而行的贿赂与腐败实例，才能动摇我们的信念；但作为一条社会学法则，公共服务出卖为私利的每一个阶段——从艺术家的堕落，到对无助的非熟练劳工的榨取——都标示着一种病态的社会行动。我们的社会身份，有赖于我们共同的同意、共同的行动、对我们共同意志的共同服从。任何个人利益，无论在战争要求个人财产与生命作出最后牺牲之时，还是在和平要求个人财产与生命绝对服从作为人民意志之固定表达的通行法律之时，都不能与公共福祉的利益相抗衡片刻。「法律与秩序」的维持，蕴含着社会主义的真正精神——个人利益消融于共同利益之中。这一切皆有赖于社会精神的演进、对社会义务的敏锐意识、对社会服务的尽责履行；而正是在这里，我们的性-经济关系所维持的过度个人主义，作为一种强大而日益不利的因素介入进来。对于性关系与经济关系在两个方向上的不可调和，我们已有朦胧的认识——一面是我们对性功能公开商业化的严辞谴责，一面是集体机构中日渐倾向独身的趋势。那些为最高宗教冲动所驱动、渴望活得高尚并服务于社会的男性或女性团体，总在性关系之中认识到某种敌对之物。他们以为它内在于这关系本身，却没有看到，正是经济的一面使它成了反动的东西。然而，独身这一做法实际上又为那些共同体社会的持续存在所默认——在那些社会里，性关系确实存在，只是以不被承认的形式、且没有经济交换的成分。它也为新教教会已婚传教士高贵而自我牺牲的奉献所默认——他们靠捐献维持。倘若传教士必须自挣妻子与自己二人的生计，他就几乎做不成传教的工作了。

===Original===
The highest human attributes are perfectly compatible with the sex-relation, but not with the sexuo-economic relation. We see this opposition again in the tendency to collectivity in bodies of single men—their comradeship, equality, and mutual helpfulness as compared with the attitude of the same men toward one another, when married. This is why the quality of "organizability" is stronger in men than in women; their common economic interests force them into relation, while the isolated and even antagonistic economic interests of women keep them from it. The condition of individual economic dependence in which women live resembles that of the savage in the forest. They obtain their economic goods by securing a male through their individual exertions, all competing freely to this end. No combination is possible. The numerous girls at a summer resort, in their attitude toward the scant supply of young men, bear an unconscious resemblance to the emulous savages in a too closely hunted forest. And here may be given an economic reason for the oft-noted bitterness with which the virtuous women regard the vicious. The virtuous woman stands in close ranks with her sisters, refusing to part with herself—her only economic goods—until she is assured of legal marriage, with its lifelong guarantee of support. Under equal proportions of birth in the two sexes, every woman would be tolerably sure of obtaining her demands. But here enters the vicious woman, and offers the same goods—though of inferior quality, to be sure—for a far less price. Every one of such illegitimate competitors lowers the chances of the unmarried women and the income of the married. No wonder those who hold themselves highly should be moved to bitterness at being undersold in this way. It is the hatred of the trade-unionist for "scab labor."

===Chinese===
人类最高的属性与性关系完全相容，与性-经济关系却不然。这种对立，我们又见于单身男性团体中的集体化倾向——他们的同志情谊、平等与互助，较之这些男子成婚后彼此之间的态度，判然有别。这便是为什么「可组织性」这种品质在男人身上强于女人：他们共同的经济利益迫使他们结成关系，而妇女彼此孤立、甚至敌对的经济利益则使她们无从结成关系。妇女生活于其中的个人经济依附（economic dependence）的境况，类似森林中野蛮人的境况。她们凭各自的努力去赢得一个男性，从而获得自己的经济财物（economic goods），人人都在为此自由竞争。联合是不可能的。避暑胜地上的众多少女，面对着供应稀少的青年男子，其态度与一片猎得过密的森林中争胜的野蛮人有着不自觉的相似。在此还可以为良家妇女看待堕落妇女时那种常被注意到的刻毒给出一个经济学的理由。贞洁的女子与她的姐妹们并肩列阵，不肯交出她自己——她唯一的经济财物——直到她确信得到合法婚姻、连同其终身的供养保证。在两性出生比例相等的条件下，每个女子都会相当有把握地实现自己的要求。但这时堕落的女人进场了，她以低得多的价钱提供同样的货物——诚然品质较劣。每一个这样的非法竞争者，都降低了未婚女子的机会和已婚女子的进项。那些自视甚高的女子，因被人这样以低价倾销压过，竟至动了刻毒之念，这又何足怪。这正是工会会员对「工贼劳工」（scab labor）的仇恨。

===Original===
On the woman's side we are steadily maintaining the force of primitive individual competition in the world as against the tendency of social progress to develop cooperation in its place, and this tendency of course is inherited by their sons. On the man's side the same effect is produced through another feature of the relation. The tendency to individualism with sex-advantage is developed in man by an opposite process to that operating on the woman. She gets her living by getting a husband. He gets his wife by getting a living. It is to her individual economic advantage to secure a mate. It is to his individual sex-advantage to secure economic gain. The sex-functions to her have become economic functions. Economic functions to him have become sex-functions. This has confounded our natural economic competition, inevitably growing into economic cooperation, with the element of sex-competition—an entirely different force.

===Chinese===
在女人这一边，我们正在世上顽固地维持着原始个人竞争的力量，以对抗社会进步以合作取而代之的趋向，而这种个人竞争的倾向当然会由她们的儿子继承。在男人这一边，同样的效应经由这一关系的另一个特征产生。带性利益的个人主义倾向在男人身上的养成，凭借的是一个与作用于女人的过程相反的过程。她靠得到一个丈夫来谋生。他靠谋得一份生计来得到妻子。于她，是个人的经济利益要求赢得配偶；于他，是个人的性利益要求赢得经济所得。于她，性功能（sex-functions）已经成了经济功能；于他，经济功能已经成了性功能。这便把我们本然的经济竞争——它本应不可避免地长成经济合作——与性竞争（sex-competition）的成分搅在了一起：那是一种全然不同的力量。

===Original===
Competition among males, with selection by the female of the superior male, is the process of sexual selection, and works to racial improvement. So far as the human male competes freely with his peers in higher and higher activities, and the female chooses the winner, so far we are directly benefited. But there is a radical distinction between sex-competition and marriage by purchase. In the first the male succeeds by virtue of what he can do; in the second, by virtue of what he can get. The increased power to do, transmitted to the young, is of racial advantage. But mere possessions, with no question as to the method of their acquisition, are not necessarily of advantage to the individual as a father. To make the sexual gain of the male rest on his purchasing power puts the immense force of sex-competition into the field of social economics, not only as an incentive to labor and achievement, which is good, but as an incentive to individual gain, however obtained, which is bad; thus accounting for our multiplied and intensified desire to get—the inordinate greed of our industrial world. The tournament of the Middle Ages was a brutal sport perhaps, with its human injury, pain, and death, under the cry of: "Fight on, brave knights! Fair eyes are looking on you!" but it represents a healthier process than our modern method of securing the wherewithal to maintain the sex-relation. As so beautifully phrased by Jean Ingelow:—

===Chinese===
雄性之间的竞争，加上雌性对优胜雄性的挑选，便是性选择（sexual selection）的过程，它促进种族的改进。人类的雄性能在多高的活动中与同侪自由竞争、雌性能挑选胜者，我们便在多大程度上直接受益。但性竞争与买卖婚姻之间有着根本的分别。在前者，雄性凭他所能做的成功；在后者，凭他所能得到的成功。增强了的能力传递给后代，于种族有利。但单纯的占有物——不问其取得的方法——于作为一个父亲的个体却未必有利。让男性的性利益取决于他的购买力，便是把性竞争的巨力放入社会经济的领域，它不仅是劳动与成就的一种激励——这是好的，还是个人所得的一种激励，不问所得从何而来——这是坏的；由此便解释了我们那成倍而加剧的获取欲——我们产业世界那无度的贪欲。中世纪的马上比武或许是一项残酷的运动，在「战斗吧，勇敢的骑士们！美丽的眼睛正注视着你们！」的呐喊声中带着对人的伤害、痛苦与死亡；但它所代表的，仍是一个比我们现代获取维系性关系之资财的方法更健康的过程。琼·英格洛（Jean Ingelow）以优美的措辞道出了这一点：——

===Original===
> "I worked afar that I might rear
 A happy home on English soil;
 I labored for the gold and gear,
 I loved my toil.

> "Forever in my spirit spake
 The natural whisper, 'Well 'twill be
 When loving wife and children break
 Their bread with thee!'"

===Chinese===
> 「我在远方劳作，只为在英国的土地上
 建起一个幸福的家园；
 我为黄金与家当操劳，
 我爱我的劳作。

> 「我心中永远响着
 天然的低语：『那将是何等美满，
 当深爱的妻子儿女
 同你分享面包！』」

===Original===
Or, put more broadly by Kipling:—

===Chinese===
或者，如吉卜林（Kipling）以更宽的笔触所言：——

===Original===
> "But since our women must walk gay,
 And money buys their gear,
 The sealing vessels filch this way
 At hazard, year by year."

===Chinese===
> 「但既然我们的女人必须出入鲜丽，
 而金钱能买来她们的衣饰，
 捕海豹的船只便这样年复一年
 甘冒风险偷偷劫掠。」

===Original===
The contest in every good man's heart today between the "ought to" and the "must," between his best work and the "potboiler," is his personal share of this incessant struggle between social interest and self-interest. For himself and by himself he would be glad to do his best work, to be true to his ideals, to be brave in meeting loss for that truth's sake. But as the compromising capitalist says in *Put Yourself in His Place*, when his sturdy young friend—a bachelor—wonders at his giving in to unjust demands, "Marriage makes a mouse of a man." To the young business man who falls into evil courses in the sex-relation the open greed of his fair dependant is a menace to his honesty, to his business prospects. On the same man married the needs of his wife often operate in the same way. The sense of the dependence of the helpless creature whose food must come through him does not stimulate courage, but compels submission.

===Chinese===
今天，每一个善良男人心中「应当」与「必须」之间、他最好的作品与「糊口之作」之间的交战，便是他在社会利益与个人利益这场无休止斗争中的一份。单为自己、单凭自己，他本乐于做出他最好的工作，忠于他的理想，为这份忠实而勇敢地承受损失。但正如《设身处地》（*Put Yourself in His Place*）中那位妥协的资本家，在他那位健壮的年轻朋友——一个单身汉——惊讶于他向不义的要求低头时所说：「婚姻把男人变成老鼠。」对在性关系中堕入邪路的年轻生意人，他那美丽依附者（dependant）的公开贪欲，威胁着他的诚实，威胁着他的商业前途。对这同一个男人成婚之后，妻子的需要往往也以同样的方式起作用。对那个食物必须经他而来、无依无靠的生灵的依附感，并不能激发勇气，而只能逼出屈服。

===Original===
The foregoing distinction should be clearly held in mind. Legitimate sex-competition brings out all that is best in man. To please her, to win her, he strives to do his best. But the economic dependence of the female upon the male, with its ensuing purchasability, does not so affect a man: it puts upon him the necessity for getting things, not for doing them. In the lowest grades of labor, where there is no getting without doing and where the laborer always does more than he gets, this works less palpable evil than in the higher grades, the professions and arts, where the most valuable work is always ahead of the market, and where to work for the market involves a lowering of standards. The young artist or poet or scientific student works for his work's sake, for art, for science, and so for the best good of society. But the artist or student married must get gain, must work for those who will pay; and those who will pay are not those who lift and bear forward the standard of progress. Community of interest is quite possible with those who are working most disinterestedly for the social good; but bring in the sex-relation, and all such solidarity disintegrates—resolves itself into the tiny groups of individuals united on a basis of sex-union, and briskly acting in their own immediate interests at anybody's or everybody's expense.

===Chinese===
上述分别应当牢记于心。正当的性竞争把男人身上最好的一切发挥出来。为了取悦她、赢得她，他奋力做到最好。但雌性对雄性的经济依附及其随之而来的可购性，对男人的影响却不是这样：它加给他的是获取事物的必需，而不是做出事物的必需。在最低级的劳动中，不做事便无所得，且劳动者所做的总是多于他所得的，这一机制造成的恶害尚不如在较高级的劳动、种种专业与艺术中那样显见——在那些领域，最有价值的工作总是走在市场前面，而为市场工作便意味着标准的降低。年轻的艺术家、诗人或科学学子为其工作本身、为艺术、为科学而工作，因而也是为社会的最大善而工作。但成了婚的艺术家或学子就必须获得收益，必须为肯付钱的人工作；而肯付钱的人，并不是那些把进步的标准举起并扛着前行的人。与那些最无私地为社会之善而工作的人利益相通，本是完全可能的；但一旦把性关系引入，这一切团结便土崩瓦解——分解为一个个以性结合为基础联结起来的微小个体集团，为自己的眼前利益而积极地损害着任何一个人或所有人。

===Original===
The social perception of the evil resultant from the intrusion of sex-influence upon racial action has found voice in the heartless proverb, "There is no evil without a woman at the bottom of it." When a man's work goes wrong, his hopes fail, his ambitions sink, cynical friends inquire, "Who is she?" It is not for nothing that a man's best friends sigh when he marries, especially if he is a man of genius. This judgment of the world has obtained side by side with its equal faith in the ennobling influence of woman. The world is quite right. It does not have to be consistent. Both judgments are correct. Woman affecting society through the sex-relation or through her individual economic relation is an ennobling influence. Woman affecting society through our perverse combination of the two becomes a strange influence, indeed.

===Chinese===
社会对性影响侵入种族行动所生之恶的觉察，在那句无情的谚语中找到了声音：「凡祸患之下，必有一个女人。」当一个男人的工作出了岔子、希望落空、抱负沉沦，愤世的朋友们便问：「她是谁？」男人的挚友在他成婚时发出叹息——尤其倘若他是天才——这并非无缘无故。世人的这一判断，与它同样坚信的女人使人高贵的感化力并存不悖。世界是完全对的。它不必自相一致。两个判断都正确。女人经由性关系或经由她个人的经济关系影响社会，是一种使人高贵的力量；而女人经由我们对这两者的乖谬结合影响社会，就确实成了一种奇异的力量。

===Original===
One of the amusing minor results of these conditions is that, while we have observed the effect of marriage upon social economic relation and the effect of social economic relation upon marriage—seeing that the devoted servant of the family was a poor servant of society and that the devoted servant of society was a poor servant of the family, seeing the successful collectivity of celibate institutions—we have jumped to the conclusion that collective prosperity was conditioned upon celibacy, and that we did not want it. That is why the popular mind is so ready to associate socialistic theories with injury to marriage. Having seen that marriage makes us less collective, we infer conversely that collectivity will make us less married—that it will "break up the home," "strike at the roots of the family."

===Chinese===
这些状况的一个可笑的次要结果是：当我们观察到婚姻对社会经济关系的影响、以及社会经济关系对婚姻的影响——看到家庭的忠诚仆人便是社会的坏仆人，社会的忠诚仆人便是家庭的坏仆人，看到独身机构成功的集体性——我们便一跃而得出结论：集体的繁荣以独身为条件，而我们并不想要这种繁荣。这便是为什么大众的心智如此乐于把社会主义理论与损害婚姻联系在一起。既已看到婚姻使我们不那么集体化，我们便反过来推论：集体化将使我们不那么成婚——它将「拆散家庭」「动摇家庭的根基」。

===Original===
When we make plain to ourselves that a pure, lasting, monogamous sex-union can exist without bribe or purchase, without the manacles of economic dependence, and that men and women so united in sex-relation will still be free to combine with others in economic relation, we shall not regard devotion to humanity as an unnatural sacrifice, nor collective prosperity as a thing to fear.

===Chinese===
当我们向自己阐明：一种纯洁、持久、一夫一妻的性结合，可以无需贿赂或买卖、无需经济依附的镣铐而存在；以性关系如此结合起来的男人和女人，仍将在经济关系上自由地与他人结合——到那时，我们便不会再把献身于人类视为一种反自然的牺牲，也不会再把集体的繁荣视为可惧之物。

===Original===
Besides this maintenance of primeval individualism in the growing collectivity of social economic process and the introduction of the element of sex-combat into the narrowing field of industrial competition, there is another side to the evil influence of the sexuo-economic relation upon social development. This is in the attitude of woman as a nonproductive consumer.

===Chinese===
除了在社会经济过程日益增长的集体性之中维持原始的个人主义、并把性争斗的成分引入日益收窄的产业竞争领域之外，性-经济关系对社会发展的恶的影响还有另一面。这便是女人作为一个非生产性消费者的姿态。

===Original===
In the industrial evolution of the human race, that marvellous and subtle drawing out and interlocking of special functions which constitute the organic life of society, we find that production and consumption go hand in hand; and production comes first. One cannot consume what has not been produced. Economic production is the natural expression of human energy—not sex-energy at all, but race-energy—the unconscious functioning of the social organism. Socially organized human beings tend to produce, as a gland to secrete: it is the essential nature of the relation. The creative impulse, the desire to make, to express the inner thought in outer form, "just for the work's sake, no use at all i' the work!" this is the distinguishing character of humanity. "I want to mark!" cries the child, demanding the pencil. He does not want to eat. He wants to mark. He is not seeking to get something into himself, but to put something out of himself. He generally wants to do whatever he sees done—to make piecrust or to make shavings, as it happens. The pie he may eat, the shavings not; but he likes to make both. This is the natural process of production, and is followed by the natural process of consumption, where practicable. But consumption is not the main end, the governing force. Under this organic social law, working naturally, we have the evolution of those arts and crafts in the exercise of which consists our human living, and on the product of which we live. So does society evolve within itself—secrete as it were—the social structure with all its complex machinery; and we function therein as naturally as so many glands, other things being equal.

===Chinese===
在人类的产业演化中——在那构成社会有机生命的特殊功能之精妙而细微的抽引与互锁中——我们发现生产与消费携手并行；而生产居先。人不能消费未经生产之物。经济生产是人类能量的自然表达——根本不是性能量（sex-energy），而是种族能量（race-energy）——是社会有机体无意识的运转。社会化组织起来的人倾向于生产，正如腺体倾向于分泌：这是这一关系的本质本性。创造的冲动、制作的欲望、把内在思想表达为外在形式的欲望——「只为工作本身，工作里全无用场！」——这便是人类区别性的品格。「我要画！」孩子喊着，索要铅笔。他并不想吃。他想画。他不是要把什么东西收进自身，而是要把什么东西从自身放出来。他通常想去做他看到别人做的一切——碰上什么便做什么，是捏馅饼皮还是刨刨花。馅饼他或许还能吃，刨花则不能；但他两样都喜欢做。这便是自然的生产过程，继之而来的、在可行之处，便是自然的消费过程。但消费并不是主要目的，不是支配的力量。在这一有机社会法则的自然运作之下，我们有了种种技艺与工艺的演化——我们的属人生活便在于对它们的运用，我们也靠它们的产品而生活。社会便这样在自身之内演化出——可以说是分泌出——连同其全部复杂机械的社会结构；而其他条件相等时，我们便如许许多多腺体一般自然而然地在其间发挥功能。

===Original===
But other things are not equal. Half the human race is denied free productive expression, is forced to confine its productive human energies to the same channels as its reproductive sex-energies. Its creative skill is confined to the level of immediate personal bodily service, to the making of clothes and preparing of food for individuals. No social service is possible. While its power of production is checked, its power of consumption is inordinately increased by the showering upon it of the "unearned increment" of masculine gifts. For the woman there is, first, no free production allowed; and, second, no relation maintained between what she does produce and what she consumes. She is forbidden to make, but encouraged to take. Her industry is not the natural output of creative energy, not the work she does because she has the inner power and strength to do it; nor is her industry even the measure of her gain. She has, of course, the natural desire to consume; and to that is set no bar save the capacity or the will of her husband.

===Chinese===
但其他条件并不相等。人类的一半被剥夺了自由的生产性表达，被迫把自己的生产性的人类能量限制在与自己生殖的性能量相同的渠道之中。她的创造技艺被局限于直接侍奉个人身体的层面，局限于为个人缝制衣裳与烹备食物。社会的服务是不可能的。她的生产能力受到遏制，她的消费能力却因男性馈赠如阵雨般倾泻于其身的「非劳动增值」（unearned increment）而无度地增长。对女人来说，其一，不允许自由的生产；其二，她所生产的与她所消费的之间不维持任何关系。她被禁止去制作，却被鼓励去索取。她的劳作不是创造能量的自然流露，不是她因怀有内在的力量而去做的工作；她的劳作甚至也不是她所得的尺度。她当然有消费的天然欲望；而在这欲望面前，除她丈夫的能力或意愿之外，不设任何别的栅栏。

===Original===
Thus we have painfully and laboriously evolved and carefully maintain among us an enormous class of nonproductive consumers—a class which is half the world, and mother of the other half. We have built into the constitution of the human race the habit and desire of taking, as divorced from its natural precursor and concomitant of making. We have made for ourselves this endless array of "horseleech's daughters, crying, Give! give!" To consume food, to consume clothes, to consume houses and furniture and decorations and ornaments and amusements, to take and take and take forever—from one man if they are virtuous, from many if they are vicious, but always to take and never to think of giving anything in return except their womanhood—this is the enforced condition of the mothers of the race. What wonder that their sons go into business "for what there is in it"! What wonder that the world is full of the desire to get as much as possible and to give as little as possible! What wonder, either, that the glory and sweetness of love are but a name among us, with here and there a strange and beautiful exception, of which our admiration proves the rarity!

===Chinese===
就这样，我们吃力而辛劳地演化出、并在我们中间小心维持着一个庞大的非生产性消费者阶级——一个占世界之半、且又是另一半的母亲的阶级。我们把索取的习惯与欲望——把它与其自然的先导和伴随者「制作」相剥离——筑进了人类种族的构成之中。我们为自己造出了这无穷无尽的一列「蚂蟥的女儿们，喊着说：给呀！给呀！」消费食物，消费衣裳，消费房屋、家具、装饰、妆饰与娱乐，永远索取、索取、再索取——贞洁的从一个男人那里索取，堕落的从许多男人那里索取，但永远是索取，除了她们的女人身份之外，从不想到回报任何东西——这便是种族的母亲们被强加的境况。她们的儿子进入实业界「为了其中的油水」，这有什么奇怪！世人皆充满尽量多获取、尽量少付出的欲望，这有什么奇怪！爱的光荣与甜美在我们中间徒有其名——只在各处偶有奇异而美好的例外，我们对这些例外的赞叹恰恰证明了它们的稀罕，这又有什么奇怪！

===Original===
Between the brutal ferocity of excessive male energy struggling in the marketplace as in a battlefield and the unnatural greed generated by the perverted condition of female energy, it is not remarkable that the industrial evolution of humanity has shown peculiar symptoms. One of the minor effects of this last condition—this limiting of female industry to close personal necessities, and this tendency of her over-developed sex-nature to overestimate the so-called "duties of her position"—has been to produce an elaborate devotion to individuals and their personal needs—not to the understanding and developing of their higher natures, but to the intensification of their bodily tastes and pleasure. The wife and mother, pouring the rising tide of racial power into the same old channels that were allowed her primitive ancestors, constantly ministers to the physical needs of her family with a ceaseless and concentrated intensity. They like it, of course. But it maintains in the individuals of the race an exaggerated sense of the importance of food and clothes and ornaments to themselves, without at all including a knowledge of their right use and value to us all. It develops personal selfishness.

===Chinese===
一边是过剩的男性能量如临战场般在市场中挣扎的残忍凶暴，一边是被扭曲的女性能量状况所生出的反常贪欲，处在这两者之间，人类的产业演化显出种种特异的症状，也就不足为奇了。后一种状况——把女性的劳作局限于贴身的个人必需，加上她过度发展的性天性倾向于高估所谓「她职分内的义务」——的次要效应之一，便是生出一种对个人及其个人需要的繁细操持——不是去理解并发展他们更高的天性，而是去强化他们身体层面的口味与享乐。妻子与母亲，把种族力量高涨的潮水灌进当初允许她原始祖先使用的那几条老渠道，以无休止而集中的强度不断照料着她家人的身体需要。他们当然喜欢这样。但它使种族的个体们保持了一种对食物、衣裳与妆饰之于自身的夸张重要感，却完全不包含对它们之于我们大家的正当用途与价值的认识。它养成个人的自私。

===Original===
Again, the consuming female, debarred from any free production, unable to estimate the labor involved in the making of what she so lightly destroys, and her consumption limited mainly to those things which minister to physical pleasure, creates a market for sensuous decoration and personal ornament, for all that is luxurious and enervating, and for a false and capricious variety in such supplies, which operates as a most deadly check to true industry and true art. As the priestess of the temple of consumption, as the limitless demander of things to use up, her economic influence is reactionary and injurious. Much, very much, of the current of useless production in which our economic energies run waste—man's strength poured out like water on the sand—depends on the creation and careful maintenance of this false market, this sink into which human labor vanishes with no return. Woman, in her false economic position, reacts injuriously upon industry, upon art, upon science, discovery, and progress. The sexuo-economic relation in its effect on the constitution of the individual keeps alive in us the instincts of savage individualism which we should otherwise have well outgrown. It sexualizes our industrial relation and commercializes our sex-relation. And, in the external effect upon the market, the oversexed woman, in her unintelligent and ceaseless demands, hinders and perverts the economic development of the world.

===Chinese===
再者，消费着的女性，既被剥夺了一切自由生产，无法估量她那样轻易毁掉之物在制作中所费的劳作，她的消费又主要局限于那些侍奉身体享乐的东西，于是便造出了一个市场——为感官性的装饰与个人的妆饰、为一切奢华而使人萎靡之物、为此类供应中虚假而任性的花样——这个市场对真正的产业与真正的艺术构成最致命的遏制。作为消费神殿的女祭司，作为耗尽万物之物的无度索求者，她的经济影响是反动的、有害的。我们的经济能量浪费于其中的那股无用生产之流——人的气力像水泼在沙上——其中很大、很大的一部分，便系于这个虚假市场的创造与小心维持，系于这个使人类劳动消失得无影无踪、不获回报的无底洞。女人，处在她虚假的经济位置上，反过来损害着产业、艺术、科学、发现与进步。性-经济关系对个体构成的影响，在我们身上存活着野蛮个人主义的种种本能——若非如此，我们本该早已把它们长过而远远抛开。它使我们的产业关系性化，又使我们的性关系商业化。而就其对市场的外部效应而言，过度性化（oversexed）的女人，凭着她那不明智而无休止的索求，阻碍并败坏着世界的经济发展。

## VII / 七

===Original===
A condition so long established, so widespread, so permanent as the sexuo-economic relation in the human species could not have been introduced and maintained in the course of social evolution without natural causes and uses. No wildest perversion of individual will could permanently maintain a condition wholly injurious to society. Church and State and social forms move and change with our growth, and we cannot hinder them long after the time has come for further progress. Once it was of advantage to society that the sexuo-economic relation should be established. Now that it is no longer of advantage to society, the “woman’s movement” has set in; and the relation is changing under our eyes from year to year, from day to day, in spite of our traditional opposition. The change considered in these pages is not one merely to be prophesied and recommended: it is already taking place under the forces of social evolution; and only needs to be made clear to our conscious thought, that we may withdraw the futile but irritating resistance of our misguided will.

===Chinese===
像人类物种中的性-经济关系（sexuo-economic relation）这样建立已久、分布极广、又如此根深蒂固的状况，若没有自然的原因与用途，是不可能在社会演化的进程中引入并维持下来的。再狂悖的个人意志之乖张，也无法长久维持一种对社会全然有害的状况。教会、国家与种种社会形态，都随我们的成长而运动、而变迁；一旦进一步进步的时机到来，我们便无法长久阻拦它们。性-经济关系的确立，曾经一度于社会有利。如今它既已不再于社会有利，「妇女运动」便应运而起；这一关系正在我们眼前年复一年、日复一日地改变着，全然不顾我们传统上的反对。本书所考察的这一改变，并不是一种仅仅可被预言、可被提倡的改变：它已在社会演化的诸种力量之下正在发生；只须把它纳入我们自觉的思想，说个明白，好教我们撤回那徒劳而恼人的、出自我们被误导的意志的抵抗。

===Original===
The original necessity for this distinctive human phenomenon lies very deep among the primal forces of social life. The relations required to develop individual organisms failed in the further development of the social organism of organization. Coordination requires first a common interest, and then the establishment of a common consciousness. It was for the common interest of the individual cells to obtain food easily, and this drew them into closer relation. That relation being established, their coexistence became a unit, an entity, a thing with a conscious life of its own. In the fullest development of the most elaborate organisms, this holds good. There must be a common interest to be served by all this coordinate activity; and there must be a common consciousness established, whereby to serve most easily the common interest. When the component cells in our tissues shrink and fail for lack of nutrition, when the several organs weary of inaction and fretfully demand their natural exercise, the man does not say, “My tissues need replenishment” or “My organs need exercise”: he says, “I am hungry.” And that “I,” the personal consciousness directing the smooth interaction of all its parts, goes to work to get food. Social evolution rests on this common interest. Individual men are profited by social relation; and, therefore, they enter into social relation. Such relation requires a common consciousness, through which the coordinate action may take place; and the whole course of social development is marked by the constant extension of this social consciousness and its necessary vehicles. Language is our largest common medium, and leads on into literature, which is but preserved speech. The brain of man is the social organ, the organ of communication. Through it flows the current of thought, whereby we are enabled to work together. By so much as our brains hold in common, we can understand each other; and, therefore, some degree of common education is essential to free social development.

===Chinese===
这一人类特有现象的原初必然性，深藏于社会生活的原初力量之中。发展个体有机体所需的种种关系，到了组织这一社会有机体要进一步发展时，便不敷使用了。协调首先要求一个共同的利益，继而要求一个共同意识（common consciousness）的建立。个体细胞易于获得食物，这合乎它们的共同利益，便把它们引入更紧密的关系。这一关系一经确立，它们的共存便成为一个单元、一个实体、一个有着自身自觉生命的东西。在最精巧的有机体最充分的发展中，这一点依然成立。必须有某个共同利益，由这一切协调活动来服务；也必须确立某种共同意识，借以最便利地服务于那共同利益。当我们组织中的细胞因缺乏营养而萎缩失能，当各个器官厌于无所事事、烦躁地要求其自然的运动时，人并不说「我的组织需要补养」或「我的器官需要运动」：他说，「我饿了。」而那个「我」，那个指挥着全身各部分顺畅互动的个人意识，便去张罗食物。社会演化正奠基于这一共同利益之上。个人因社会关系而得利；因此，他们便进入社会关系。这样的关系要求一种共同意识，协调的行动须经由它而发生；而社会发展的整个历程，都以这一社会意识及其必要载体的不断扩展为标志。语言是我们最大的共同媒介，并进而引出文学——文学无非是被保存下来的言语。人的大脑是社会的器官、交流的器官。思想之流经它而过，使我们得以协同做工。我们的大脑有多少共通之处，我们彼此便能理解到什么程度；因此，某种程度的共同教育，是自由的社会发展所不可或缺的。

===Original===
At the very beginning of this process, when the human animal was still but an animal﻿—but an individual﻿—came the imperative demand for the establishment of a common consciousness between these hitherto irreconcilable individuals. The first step in nature toward this end is found in the relation between mother and child. Where the young, after birth, are still dependent on the mother, the functions of the one separate living body needing the service of another separate living body, we have the overlapping of personality, the mutual need, which brings with it the essential instinct that holds together these interacting personalities. That instinct we call love. The child must have the mother’s breast. The mother’s breast must have the child. Therefore, between mother and child was born love, long before fatherhood was anything more than a momentary incident. But the common consciousness, the mutual attraction between mother and child, stopped there absolutely. It was limited in range to this closest relation; in duration, to the period of infancy.

===Chinese===
在这一进程的最初，当人类这动物还仅仅是动物——仅仅是一个个体——之时，便出现了那不可违逆的要求：在这些迄今势不两立的个体之间，建立起一种共同意识。自然界朝此目标迈出的第一步，见于母亲与孩子的关系。凡幼崽出生之后仍须仰赖母亲之处——一个独立生命体的功能需要另一个独立生命体的服务——我们便有了人格的重叠、相互的需要，它随之带来那种把这两个相互作用的个性维系在一起的根本本能。那本能，我们称之为爱。孩子必须有母亲的乳房。母亲的乳房必须有孩子。因此，早在父职还不过是一桩一时的偶然之事时，母亲与孩子之间便诞生了爱。但那共同意识、那母亲与孩子之间的相互吸引，到此便绝对止步了。它在范围上限于这一最亲密的关系；在时间上，限于婴孩时期。

===Original===
The common interest of human beings must be served by racial faculties, not merely by the sex-functions of the female, or the duties of mother to child. As the male, acting through his natural instincts, steadily encroached upon the freedom of the female until she was reduced to the state of economic dependence, he thereby assumed the position of provider for this creature no longer able to provide for herself. He was not only compelled to serve her needs, but to fulfil in his own person the thwarted uses of maternity. He became, and has remained, a sort of man-mother, alone in creation in his remarkable position. By this common interest, existing now not only between mother and child, but between father, mother, and child, grew up a wider common consciousness. And, as the father served the child not through sex-function, but through race-function, this service was open to far wider development and longer duration than the mother’s alone could ever have reached. Maternal energy is the force through which have come into the world both love and industry. It is through the tireless activity of this desire, the mother’s wish to serve the young, that she began the first of the arts and crafts whereby we live. While the male savage was still a mere hunter and fighter, expressing masculine energy, the katabolic force, along its essential line, expanding, scattering, the female savage worked out in equally natural ways the conserving force of female energy. She gathered together and saved nutrition for the child, as the germ-cell gathers and saves nutrition in the unconscious silences of nature. She wrapped it in garments and built a shelter for its head as naturally as the same maternal function had loved, clothed, and sheltered the unborn. Maternal energy, working externally through our elaborate organism, is the source of productive industry, the main current of social life.

===Chinese===
人类的共同利益，必须由种族的官能来服务，而不仅仅由女性的性功能（sex-functions）、或母亲对孩子的职责来服务。当雄性循其自然本能步步进逼雌性的自由、直至她沦入经济依附（economic dependence）的境地时，他也就此担起了供养者的位置，供养这个再不能自养其生的造物。他不但被迫服务于她的需要，还须以自己的亲身，去实现那被挫抑的生育（maternity）之用途。他成了、并且一直是一种男人母亲（man-mother），在造物之中独占着这一奇特的位置。借着这一共同利益——如今它不仅存在于母亲与孩子之间，而且存在于父亲、母亲与孩子之间——一种更为宽广的共同意识生长起来。而且，由于父亲服务孩子并非经由性功能，而是经由种族功能（race-function），这一服务便得以有远为宽广的发展与远为长久的延续，非母亲独自所能企及。母性能量正是那样的力量，爱与产业皆由它而来到世间。正是经由这一欲望——母亲要服务于幼小的愿望——的不倦活动，她开创了第一种赖以为生的技艺与手艺。当雄性野蛮人还只是一个猎者与斗士，循其本源的路线宣泄着男性能量即那分解代谢之力（katabolic force），扩展着、播散着之时；雌性野蛮人便以同样自然的方式，操演出女性能量那种保存之力。她为孩子聚集并储蓄养分，一如生殖细胞在自然那无意识的沉寂中聚集并储蓄养分。她把它裹进衣裳、为它的头颅搭起遮蔽，做得自然而然，恰如那同一的母性机能从前爱抚、包覆、荫庇着那未生者一样。母性能量经由我们这精巧的有机体向外做工，便是生产性产业的源头，是社会生活的主脉。

===Original===
But not until this giant force could ally itself with others and work cooperatively, overcoming the destructive action of male energy in its blind competition, could our human life enter upon its full course of racial evolution. This is what was accomplished through the suppression of the free action of maternal energy in the female and its irresistible expression through the male. The two forces were combined, and he was the active factor in their manifestation. It was one of nature’s calm, unsmiling miracles, no more wonderful than where she makes the guileless, greedy bee, who thinks he is merely getting his dinner, serve as an agent of reproduction to countless flowers. The bee might resent it if he knew what office he performed, and that his dinner was only there that he might fulfil that office. The subjection of woman has involved to an enormous degree the maternalizing of man. Under its bonds he has been forced into new functions, impossible to male energy alone. He has had to learn to love and care for someone besides himself. He has had to learn to work, to serve, to be human. Through the sex-passion, mightily overgrown, the human race has been led and driven up the long, steep path of progress, over all obstacles, through all dangers, carrying its accompanying conditions of disease and sin (and surmounting them), up and up in spite of all, until at last a degree of evolution is reached in which the extension of human service and human love makes possible a better way.

===Chinese===
但是，非等到这股巨力能与别的力量结盟、协同做工，胜过男性能量在其盲目竞争中的破坏作用，我们的人类生活才能进入其种族演化的完整历程。而这是通过压抑女性身上母性能量的自由行动、并使它经由男性不可抗拒地表达出来，方才完成的。两股力量就此结合，而他便是它们显现之中的能动因素。这是自然那从容而不带笑意的奇迹之一，其奇妙也不多让于她使那只无辜而贪馋的蜜蜂充当无数花朵的生殖媒介——而那蜜蜂自以为不过是去吃一顿饭。倘若蜜蜂知道自己履行的是怎样一种职分，知道自己那顿饭之所以在那里，不过是为的使他履行那职分，它或许会愤愤不平。女人的屈从，在极大的程度上牵涉到男人的母性化。在它的束缚之下，他被迫担负起单凭男性能量所不可能的新功能。他不得不学会去爱、去照料自己以外的某个人。他不得不学会劳作、学会服务、学会成为人。凭着那过度滋长的性激情，人类被引领着、驱策着走上进步那漫长而陡峭的路，越过一切障碍，历经一切危险，背负着疾病与罪孽这些相伴的境况（并且一一战胜了它们），不顾一切地向上、再向上，直到终于达到这样一个演化程度：人类服务与人类之爱的扩展，使一条更好的道路成为可能。

===Original===
By the action of his own desires, through all its byproducts of evil, man was made part mother; and so both man and woman were enabled to become human. It was an essential step in our racial progress, a means to an end. It should not be considered as an extreme maternal sacrifice, but as a novel and thorough system of paternal sacrifice﻿—the male of genus *homo* coerced by sex-necessity into the expression of maternal energy. The naturally destructive tendencies of the male have been gradually subverted to the conservative tendencies of the female, and this so palpably that the process is plainly to be observed throughout history. Into the male have been bred, by natural selection and unbroken training, the instincts and habits of the female, to his immense improvement. The female was dependent upon the male in individual economic relation. She was in a state of helpless slavery. She was treated with unspeakable injustice and cruelty. But nature’s processes go on quite undisturbed among incidents like these. To blend the opposing sex-tendencies of two animals into the fruitful powers of a triumphant race was a painful process, but that does not matter. It was essential, and it has been fulfilled. There should be an end to the bitterness of feeling which has arisen between the sexes in this century. Right as is the change of attitude in the woman of today, she need feel no resentment as to the past, no shame, no sense of wrong. With a full knowledge of the initial superiority of her sex and the sociological necessity for its temporary subversion, she should feel only a deep and tender pride in the long patient ages during which she has waited and suffered, that man might slowly rise to full racial equality with her. She could afford to wait. She could afford to suffer.

===Chinese===
凭着自身欲望的作用——连同它的一切恶的副产品——男人被造成了半个母亲；男人与女人便由此得以双双成为人。这是我们种族进步中必不可少的步骤，一种达乎目的的手段。它不应被看作一种极端的母性牺牲，而应被看作一套新颖而彻底的父性牺牲体系——人属（genus *homo*）的雄性，被性的必然胁迫着去表达母性能量。雄性那天然具破坏性的种种倾向，被渐渐移转为雌性的保守倾向，而且转变得如此显然，以至于这一过程在整部历史中都明白可见。凭着自然选择（natural selection）与不曾间断的训导，雌性的本能与习性被培育进雄性体内，使他获益至巨。雌性在个体的经济关系中依附于雄性。她处于一种无告的奴役状态。她曾遭受难以言说的不公与残酷。但自然的过程在这些变故之间照常进行，不为所扰。把两只动物彼此相反的性倾向，融铸成一个得胜种族的丰饶力量，是一个痛苦的过程，但那无关紧要。它是必要的，而它已经完成。本世纪两性之间生出的怨怼之情，也该有个尽头了。今日妇女态度的转变尽管正当，她却无须对过去怀有怨恨，无须羞愧，无须冤屈之感。既充分知晓她的性别原初的优越，又知晓那暂时倾覆的社会学必然，她所应感到的，只应是一种深沉而温柔的骄傲——为着那漫长坚忍的世纪，她在其中等候着、受着苦，好教男人得以慢慢升到与她完全的种族平等。她等得起。她也苦得起。

===Original===
It is high time that women began to understand their true position, primarily and eternally, and to see how little the long years of oppression have altered it. It was not well for the race to have the conservative processes of life so wholly confined to the female, the male being merely a temporary agent in reproduction and of no further use. His size, strength, and ferocity﻿—admirable qualities in maintaining the life of an individual animal﻿—were not the most desirable to develop the human race. We needed most the quality of coordination﻿—the facility in union, the power to make and to save rather than to spend and to destroy. These were female qualities. Acting from his own nature, man could not manifest traits that he did not possess. Throned as woman’s master, chained as her servant, he has, through this strange combination of functions, acquired these traits under the heavy law of necessity. Originally, the two worked on divers lines, he spending and scattering, she saving and building. She was the deep, steady, main stream of life, and he the active variant, helping to widen and change that life, but rather as an adjunct than as an essential. Races there were and are which reproduce themselves without the masculine organism﻿—by hermaphroditism and parthenogenesis.

===Chinese===
妇女们早该开始理解自己那原初而永恒的真实位置，并看清这漫长的压迫岁月究竟把它改变了多少。把生活中种种保守的过程全然局限于雌性一身，而雄性不过是生殖中一时的媒介、此外再无用处，这对种族并没有好处。他的体型、力量与凶猛——这些在维持一只个体动物的生命上可敬的品质——却不是发展人类种族所最需要的。我们最需要的是协调的品质——联合的便利，建造与储蓄而非花费与毁弃的力量。这些本是女性的品质。男人凭自己的本性行事，便无从显现他并不具备的品性。高踞为女人的主人、又被锁链拴作她的仆人，他正是经由这一奇特的功能组合，在必然那沉重的法则之下习得了这些品性。起初，两者各行其道：他花费与播散，她储蓄与建造。她是生命那深沉、稳定的主流，而他是一个活跃的变体，有助于拓宽并改变那生命，但其身份毋宁是附属，而非本体。过去有、如今也有这样的种族，无需雄性机体而自行繁殖——凭着雌雄同体（hermaphroditism）与单性生殖（parthenogenesis）。

===Original===
As the evolution of species progressed, we find a long series of practical experiments in males﻿—very tiny, transient, and inferior devices at first, but gradually developed into fuller and fuller equality with the female. In some of the lower forms, as in rotifers, insects, and crustaceans, are found the most inferior males, often none at all; and, where they do exist, they have no use save as an agent in reproduction. The most familiar instance of this is among the bees, where the drone, after fulfilling his functions, dies or is destroyed by the sturdy co-mothers of the hive. The common spider, too, has a tiny male, who tremblingly achieves his one brief purpose, and is then eaten up by his mate. She is the spider, a permanent flycatcher. He is merely a fertilizing agent. The little green aphis, so numerous on our rosebushes, can reproduce parthenogenetically so long as conditions are good﻿—while it is warm and there is enough to eat; but, when conditions grow hard, males are developed, and the dual method of reproduction is introduced.

===Chinese===
随着物种演化的推进，我们在雄性身上发现了一系列漫长的实际试验——起初是非常微小、短暂而低劣的设计，却渐渐发展得与雌性愈来愈充分的平等。在一些较低等的形态中，如轮虫（rotifers）、昆虫与甲壳动物（crustaceans），雄性最为低劣，甚至常常全然没有；即便存在，也除充当生殖的媒介之外别无用处。这方面最广为人知的例证是在蜜蜂中间：雄蜂（drone）在履行完他的功能之后，便死去，或被蜂巢里那些健壮的共同母亲们弄死。普通蜘蛛也有一个微小的雄性，颤颤兢兢地成就他唯一而短促的目的，随后便被他的配偶吃掉。她才是蜘蛛，一个常设的捕蝇者。他不过是一个授精的媒介。我们玫瑰丛上为数众多的小绿蚜虫，只要条件良好——天气温暖、食物充足——便能行单性生殖；而一旦条件转劣，雄性便被育出，双重生殖的方法随之引入。

===Original===
In the two great activities of life, self-preservation and race-preservation, the female in these lower species is better equipped than the male for the first, and carries almost the whole burden of the second. His short period of functional use is as nothing compared to her long period of gestation, and the services she performs, in many cases, in providing for her young after their birth. Race-preservation has been almost entirely a female function, sometimes absolutely so. But it has been proven better for the race to have two highly developed parents rather than to have one. Therefore, sexual equality has been slowly evolved, not only by increasing the importance of the male element in reproduction, but by developing race-qualities in the male, so long merely a reproductive agent. The last step of this process has been the elevation of the male of genus *homo* to full racial equality with the female, and this has involved her temporary subjection. Both her physical and psychical tendencies have been transplanted into the organism of the male. He has been made the working mother of the world. The sexuo-economic relation was necessary to raise and broaden, to deepen and sweeten, to make more feminine, and so more human, the male of the human race. If the female had remained in full personal freedom and activity, she would have remained superior to him, and both would have remained stationary. Since the female had not the tendency to vary which distinguished the male, it was essential that the expansive forces of masculine energy be combined with the preservative and constructive forces of feminine energy. The expansive and variable male energy, struggling under its new necessity for constructive labor, has caused that labor to vary and progress more than it would have done in feminine hands alone. Out of her wealth of power and patience, liking to work, to give, she toils on forever in the same primitive industries. He, impatient of obstacles, not liking to work, desirous to get rather than to give, splits his task into a thousand specialties, and invents countless ways to lighten his labors. Male energy made to expend itself in performing female functions is what has brought our industries to their present development. Without the economic dependence of the female, the male would still be merely the hunter and fighter, the killer, the destroyer; and she would continue to be the industrious mother, without change or progress.

===Chinese===
在生命的两大活动——自我保存（self-preservation）与种族保存（race-preservation）——之中，这些较低物种里的雌性在第一项上装备优于雄性，在第二项上则几乎背负全部重担。比起她漫长的孕期、比起她在许多情形下于幼崽出生后所行的供养之劳，他那短短一段功能用途的时日，简直算不得什么。种族保存几乎一直是雌性的功能，有时且是绝对的。但已被证明：对种族而言，有两个高度发达的亲代，胜过只有一个。因此，性平等被缓慢地演化出来——不仅通过提高雄性因素在生殖中的重要性，还通过在雄性——这个长久以来纯粹的生殖媒介——身上发展出种种种族品质。这一过程的最后一步，是把人属的雄性提升到与雌性完全的种族平等；而这一步牵动了她暂时的屈从。她身体上与心理上的种种倾向，都被移植进了雄性的机体。他被造成了世界的劳作母亲。性-经济关系是必要的：借它来提升并拓宽、加深并甜美化人类种族的雄性，使他更女性化，从而也更人性化。倘若雌性始终保有完全的个人自由与活动，她将始终优于他，而两者都将停滞不动。既然雌性不具备雄性那种显著的变异倾向，那么，男性能量的扩张之力就必须与女性能量的保存之力、建构之力相结合。那扩张而多变的男性能量，在它对建构性劳作的新必然之下挣扎着，遂使这劳作所发生的变化与进步，远非单在女性之手所能达到。她出于力量与耐心之丰盈，又乐于劳作、乐于给予，便在那些一成不变的原始产业里永远劳碌下去。他则不耐障碍，不喜劳作，渴望获取而非给予，把他的活计拆成千百种专门行当，又发明出数不清的法子来减轻自己的辛劳。被迫把男性能量耗用在履行女性功能之上，正是这一点，把我们诸产业带到了今天的发展地步。若没有雌性的经济依附，雄性将仍然只是猎者与斗士、杀戮者与毁灭者；而她也将依然是那个勤勉的母亲，没有改变，没有进步。

===Original===
> “What the children of Israel delighted in making
 

 The children of Egypt delighted in breaking,”

===Chinese===
> 「以色列的儿女所乐于建造的，
 

 埃及的儿女所乐于拆毁的，」

===Original===
runs the old rhyme; but there is small gain in such a process. In her subordinate position, under every disadvantage, through the very walls of her prison, the constructive force of woman has made man its instrument, and worked for the upbuilding of the world. As his energy was purely individualistic, and only to be controlled by the power of sex-attraction, it needed precisely this form of union, with its peculiar exaggeration of sex-faculty, to hold him to his task. Woman’s abnormal development of sex, restrained and imprisoned by every law, has acted like a coiled spring upon the only free agent in society﻿—man. Under its intense stimulus he has moved mountains. All the world has seen it; and we have always murmured admiringly, “Oh, ’tis love, ’tis love, ’tis love that makes the world go round.” It has done so, indeed, or, at least, has driven man round the world in one long range of struggle and conquest, of work and war. And every man who loves, and says, “I am yours: do with me what you will,” knows the power, and honors it.

===Chinese===
古老的歌谣如是吟咏；但这样的过程，所得无几。身处从属的位置，在一切不利之下，穿过她牢狱的四壁，女人的建构之力把男人用作它的工具，为世界的筑造而做工。既然他的能量纯粹是个人主义的，只能被性吸引（sex-attraction）的力量所支配，那么，恰恰需要这种形式的结合、连同它对性机能那特有的夸张，才能把他拴在他的活计上。女人那反常的性发展，被一切法律所约束、所监禁，却像一根盘紧的弹簧，作用在社会唯一的自由行动者——男人——身上。在它强烈的刺激之下，他搬动了群山。全世界都看到了这一点；我们一直赞叹着低声念叨：「啊，是爱情，是爱情，是爱情使这世界运转。」它确实使世界运转了，或者至少，驱使男人在一长串争斗与征服、劳作与战争之中，把世界跑了个遍。而每一个恋爱着的、说着「我是你的：随你处置我」的男人，都知晓这力量，并且敬重它。

===Original===
Human development thus far has proceeded in the male line, under the force of male energy, spurred by sex-stimulus, and by the vast storage battery of female energy suppressed. Women can well afford their period of subjection for the sake of a conquered world, a civilized man. In spite of the agony of the process, the black, long ages of shame and pain and horror, women should remember that they are still here; and, thanks to the blessed power of heredity, they are not so far aborted that a few generations of freedom will not set them abreast of the age. When the centuries of slavery and dishonor, of torture and death, of biting injustice and slow, suffocating repression, seem long to women, let them remember the geologic ages, the millions and millions of years when puny, pygmy, parasitic males struggled for existence, and were used or not, as it happened, like a half-tried patent medicine. What train of wives and concubines was ever so ignominiously placed as the extra husbands carried among the scales of the careful female cirriped, lest she lose one or two! What neglect of faded wives can compare with the scorned, unnoticed death of the drone bee, starved, stung, shut out, walled up in wax, kept only for his momentary sex-function, and not absolutely necessary for that! What Bluebeard tragedy or cruelty of bride-murdering Eastern king can emulate the ruthless slaughter of the hapless little male spider, used by his ferocious mate “to coldly furnish forth a marriage breakfast”! Never once in the history of humanity has any outrage upon women compared with these sweeping sacrifices of helpless males in earlier species. The female has been dominant for the main duration of life on earth. She has been easily equal always up to our own race; and in our race she has been subjugated to the male during the earlier period of development for such enormous racial gain, such beautiful and noble uses, that the sacrifice should never be mentioned nor thought of by a womanhood that knows its power. For the upbuilding of human life on earth she could afford to have her own held back; and﻿—closer, tenderer, lovelier service﻿—for the raising of her fierce sex-mate to a free and gentle brotherhood, for the uplifting of the human soul in her dear son, she could have borne not only this, but more﻿—borne it smilingly, ungrudgingly, gladly, for his sake and the world’s.

===Chinese===
迄今为止，人类的发展是沿男性的路线进行的：凭着男性能量的力量，受着性刺激的鞭策，还受着那被压抑的女性能量所构成的巨大蓄电池的驱动。为了一个被征服的世界、一个文明化了的男人，妇女们完全付得起她们那段屈从的岁月。纵有这一过程的苦痛，纵有那羞辱、疼痛与恐怖的漫长黑世纪，妇女们应当记住：她们如今仍在这里；而且，托遗传那可祝的力量之福，她们并未被摧折到这样的地步，以致几代人的自由还不能使她们重新并肩于这个时代。当那奴役与蒙羞、酷刑与死亡、刺骨的不公与缓慢窒息的压抑的诸世纪，在妇女们看来显得漫长时，让她们想想那些地质年代，想想那千百万年间，弱小、侏儒般、寄生的雄性们挣扎求存，或被用上一用，或被弃置不顾，全凭际遇，像一瓶只试了一半的专利成药。哪一支妻妾的长队，曾像那只谨慎的雌性蔓足类动物（cirriped）鳞壳之间随身携带的备偶丈夫们那样，落得如此不体面的安排——只因生怕失落一两个！对色衰妻子的冷落，又哪能比得上雄蜂那受尽鄙夷、无人理会的死亡——被饿、被蜇、被拒之门外、被蜡封在墙里，养着只为那顷刻的性功能，而且就连那也并非绝对必需！蓝胡子（Bluebeard）的惨剧、东方杀新娘的君王的残酷，又有哪一样比得上那不幸的小雄蜘蛛遭到的无情屠戮——他被凶悍的配偶拿去「冷冷地备办一席婚宴早餐」！在人类历史上，加诸妇女的任何暴行，从不曾有一次比得上早先物种中这些对无助雄性的扫荡性牺牲。雌性在地球上生命的大部分时间里一直占据支配地位。直到我们这个种族为止，她始终轻易地保持着平等；而在我们种族这里，她在发展的早期被降伏于雄性，为的是如此巨大的种族收益、如此美好而高贵的用途，以致这份牺牲，本不该被一个知晓自身力量的女性世界提起或记挂。为了地上人类生命的筑造，她承受得起自己的发展被延宕；而——那更切近、更温柔、更可爱的服务——为了把她那凶悍的性伴侣抬升到自由而温和的手足之谊，为了在她亲爱的儿子身上提升人的灵魂，她本可以不仅承受这一切，还承受更多——含笑地、无怨地、欣然地承受，为了他，也为了这个世界。

===Original===
And now that the long strain is over, now that the time has come when neither he nor the world is any longer benefited by her subordination, now that she is coming steadily out into direct personal expression, into the joy of racial action in full freedom, of power upon the throne instead of behind it, it is unworthy of this supreme new birth to waste one regret upon the pain that had to be.

===Chinese===
而如今，漫长的重负既已卸下，时候既已到来——他不再因她的屈从而受益，世界也不再受益——她正稳步地走出来，进入直接的个人表达，进入以充分的自由从事种族行动的喜悦，进入那坐上王座而非退居王座之后的权力；在这至高的新生面前，再为那不得不经受的痛苦浪费一丝追悔，都是不相称的。

===Original===
Thus it may be seen that, even allowing for the injury to the individual and to society through the check to race-development and the increase of sex-development in woman, with its transmitted effects; allowing, further, that our highly specialized motherhood cannot be shown to be an advantage to humanity﻿—still it remains true that our sexuo-economic relation, with its effect of carrying on human life through the male side only, in activities driven by intensified sex-energy, has reacted to the benefit of the individual and of the race in many ways, as already suggested: in the extension of female function through the male; in the blending of faculties which have resulted in the possibility of our civilization; in the superior fighting power developed in the male, and its effects in race-conquest, military and commercial; in the increased productivity developed by his assumption of maternal function; and by the sex-relation becoming mainly proportioned to his power to pay for it. Even motherhood has been indirectly the gainer in that, although the mother herself has been checked in direct maternal service, serving the race far more through her stimulation of male activities than through any activities of her own; yet the child has ultimately profited more by the materno-paternal services than he would have done by the maternal services alone.

===Chinese===
由此可见，即便把种族发展受遏制、女人身上性发展增长及其遗传效应给个体与社会造成的伤害都考虑在内；再退一步，即便承认我们那高度专门化的母职（motherhood）无法被证明于人类有利——下述一点仍然成立：我们的性-经济关系，连同它那仅经由男性一方、在被强化了的性能量所驱动的诸活动中延续人类生活的效应，曾从多方面反哺个体与种族的利益，如前文已提示的：经由男性扩展了女性功能；种种官能的融合，带来了我们文明的可能性；在男性身上发展出的优势战斗力，及其在军事与商业的种族征服中的效应；他承担起母性功能后发展出的更高的生产率；以及性关系之变得大体上与他的支付能力相称。就连母职也间接受益于此：虽然母亲本人在直接的母性服务上受到遏制，她之于种族的服务，远多是通过她对男性活动的激发，而非通过她自己的任何活动；但孩子最终从这父母合一的服务中所得的好处，仍多于他单从母亲一方的服务中所能得到的。

===Original===
All this may be granted as having been true in the past. And many, reassured by this frank admission, will ask, if it is so clear that the subjection of woman was useful, that this evil-working, monstrous sexuo-economic relation was after all of racial advantage, how we know that it is time to change. Principally, because we are changing. Social development is not caused by the promulgators of theories and by the writers of books. When Rousseau wrote of equality, free France was being born﻿—the spirit of the times thrilled through the human mind; and those who had ears to hear heard, those who had pens to write wrote. The condition of chattel slavery, working to its natural end, roused Garrison and Phillips and Harriet Beecher Stowe. They did not make the movement. The period of women’s economic dependence is drawing to a close, because its racial usefulness is wearing out. We have already reached a stage of human relation where we feel the strength of social duty pull against the sex-ties that have been for so long the only ties that we have recognized. The common consciousness of humanity, the sense of social need and social duty, is making itself felt in both men and women. The time has come when we are open to deeper and wider impulses than the sex-instinct; the social instincts are strong enough to come into full use at last. This is shown by the twin struggle that convulses the world today﻿—in sex and economics﻿—the “woman’s movement” and the “labor movement.” Neither name is wholly correct. Both make a class issue of what is in truth a social issue, a question involving every human interest. But the women naturally feel most the growing healthful pain of their position. They personally revolt, and think it is they who are most to be benefited. Similarly, since the “laboring classes” feel most the growing healthful pain of their position, they as naturally revolt under the same conviction. Sociologically, these conditions, which some find so painful and alarming, mean but one thing﻿—the increase of social consciousness. The progress of social organization has produced a corresponding degree of individualization, which has reached at last even to women﻿—even to the lowest grade of unskilled labor. This higher degree of individualization means a sharp personal consciousness of the evils of a situation hitherto little felt. With this higher growth of individual consciousness, and forming a part of it, comes the commensurate growth of social consciousness. We have grown to care for one another.

===Chinese===
这一切都可以被承认在过去是真实的。而许多人为这一坦率的承认宽了心，便会问道：倘若女人的屈从既然如此显然地有用，这个作恶的、怪诞的性-经济关系毕竟于种族有利，我们又如何知道，变革的时机已经到了呢？主要地，凭我们正在变革。社会的发展并不是由理论的颁布者与书籍的写作者造成的。当卢梭（Rousseau）著书论平等之时，自由的法兰西正在诞生——时代的精神激荡过人心；有耳能听者听见了，有笔能写者写下了。动产奴隶制（chattel slavery）的处境渐近其自然的终点，唤起了加里森（Garrison）、菲利普斯（Phillips）与哈丽特·比彻·斯托（Harriet Beecher Stowe）。并不是他们造成了那场运动。妇女经济依附的时期正临尾声，因为它的种族功用正在耗竭。我们已经达到人类关系的一个阶段：在这里，我们感到社会义务的力量，正在拉扯那长久以来被我们承认的唯一纽带——性的纽带。人类的共同意识、对社会需要与社会义务的感觉，正在男人和女人身上同时显示自己。这样的时刻已经到来：我们已能接受比性本能更深、更广的冲动；社会的诸本能终于强壮到可以充分投入使用了。这一点由今日撼动世界的那场孪生的斗争显示出来——在性与经济两个方面的——「妇女运动」与「劳工运动」。两个名称都不全然正确。两者都把一个实为社会议题的问题——一个牵涉每一项人类利益的问题——弄成了阶级议题。但妇女们自然最切肤地感到她们处境中日增的那健康的痛楚。她们亲身起而反抗，并且以为受益最多的便是她们。同样地，既然「劳动阶级」最切肤地感到他们处境中日增的健康痛楚，他们便也在同一确信之下自然起而反抗。从社会学上看，这些被某些人认为如此痛苦、如此骇人的状况，只意味着一件事——社会意识的增长。社会组织的前进产生出相应程度的个体化（individualization），它终于甚至到达了妇女——甚至到达了最低等级的非熟练劳动者。这更高程度的个体化，意味着对一种迄今少被觉察的处境之恶的尖锐的个人意识。随着个人意识这一更高的成长、并作为其一部分而来的，是与之相称的社会意识的成长。我们已经成长为彼此关切的人。

===Original===
The woman’s movement rests not alone on her larger personality, with its tingling sense of revolt against injustice, but on the wide, deep sympathy of women for one another. It is a concerted movement, based on the recognition of a common evil and seeking a common good. So with the labor movement. It is not alone that the individual laborer is a better educated, more highly developed man than the stolid peasant of earlier days, but also that with this keener personal consciousness has come the wider social consciousness, without which no class can better its conditions. The traits incident to our sexuo-economic relation have developed till they forbid the continuance of that relation. In the economic world, excessive masculinity, in its fierce competition and primitive individualism; and excessive femininity, in its inordinate consumption and hindering conservatism; have reached a stage where they work more evil than good.

===Chinese===
妇女运动不仅立足于她那更阔大的人格、连同那对不公起而反抗的灼热之感，也立足于妇女彼此之间宽广而深沉的同情。它是一场协同的运动，奠基于对一个共同之恶的认识，并追求一种共同的善。劳工运动亦是如此。这不仅因为个体劳动者比起从前麻木的农夫，是受过更好教育、发展程度更高的人；还因为随着这更敏锐的个人意识而来的，是更宽广的社会意识——没有它，任何阶级都无法改善自身的境况。我们这种性-经济关系所附带的种种特质，已经发展到禁止这一关系继续下去的地步。在经济世界里，过度男性化——那激烈的竞争与原始的个人主义；与过度女性化——那无度的消费与碍事的保守主义；都已达到一个作恶多于行善的阶段。

===Original===
The increasing specialization of the modern woman, acquired by inheritance from the ceaselessly specializing male, makes her growing racial faculties strain against the primitive restrictions of a purely sexual relation. The desire to produce﻿—the distinctive human quality﻿—is no longer satisfied with a status that allows only reproduction. In our present stage of social evolution it is increasingly difficult and painful for women to endure their condition of economic dependence, and therefore they are leaving it. This does not mean that at a given day all women will stand forth free together, but that in slowly gathering numbers, now so great that all the world can see, women in the most advanced races are so standing free. Great advances along social lines come slowly, like the many-waved progress of the tide: they are not sudden jumps over yawning chasms.

===Chinese===
现代妇女与日俱增的专门化——得自对那不断专门化着的男性的遗传——使她那日益成长的种族官能紧绷起来，挣抗纯粹性关系的种种原始束缚。生产的欲望——人之为人的独特品质——不再满足于一种只容生殖的身份。在我们当下这个社会演化阶段，妇女要忍受她们经济依附的处境，正变得日益艰难而痛苦，因此她们正在离开它。这并不意味着在某一天，所有妇女将一齐挺立为自由人；而是说，在缓慢聚拢的人数之中——如今已多到全世界都看得见——最先进诸种族中的妇女正这样挺立为自由人。社会路线上的伟大进展来得缓慢，像潮水一浪接一浪的推进：它们不是纵身跃过裂壑的突然一跳。

===Original===
But, besides this first plain perception that our strange relation is coming to an end, we may see how in its own working it develops forces which must end it or us. The method of action of our peculiar cat’s-paw combination of the sexes﻿—the mother-father doing the work of the helpless creature he carries on his back; the parasite mate devouring even when she should most feed﻿—has been this, as repeatedly shown: because of sex-desire the male subjugates the female. Lest he lose her, he feeds her, and, perforce, her young. She, obtaining food through the sex-relation, becomes oversexed, and acts with constantly increasing stimulus on his sex-activities; and, as these activities are made economic by their relation, she so stimulates industry and all progress. But﻿—and here is the natural end of an unnatural position, a position that serves its purpose for a time, but holds in itself the seeds of its own destruction﻿—through the unchecked sex-energy, accumulated under the abnormal pressure of the economic side of the relation, such excess is developed as tends to destroy both individual and race; and such psychic qualities are developed as tend also to our injury and extinction.

===Chinese===
但是，除了这第一个明白的觉察——我们这奇特的关系正走向终结——之外，我们还可以看到，它在自身的运转中如何发展出一些力量，这些力量不终结这关系，便终结我们。我们这种奇特的、被用作猫爪的两性组合——那身兼母亲的父亲，替他背上驮着的无助造物操劳；那寄生的配偶，在最该哺喂的时候却吞噬不休——其作用的方式，如前文反复表明的，是这样：出于性欲，雄性降伏了雌性。为免失去她，他供养她，并且，迫于必然，也供养她的幼小。她经由性关系获得食物，便变得过度性化（oversexed），并以不断增强的刺激作用于他的性活动；而这些活动又因那关系而成为经济性的，她便这样刺激了产业与一切进步。但是——这里便是一个反常位置的自然尽头，一个一时服务于其目的、却自身怀藏毁灭之种子的位置——经由那不受遏制的性能量——它在关系的经济一翼的反常压力之下积累起来——所发展出的那种过度，已趋向于毁灭个体与种族；所发展出的种种心理品质，也趋向于对我们的伤害与绝灭。

===Original===
A relation that inevitably produces abnormal development cannot be permanently maintained. The intensification of sex-energy as a social force results in such limitless exaggeration of sex-instinct as finds expression sexually in the unnatural vices of advanced civilization, and, socially, in the strained economic relation between producer and consumer which breaks society in two. The sexuo-economic relation serves to bring social development to a certain level. After that level is reached, a higher relation must be adopted, or the lifting process comes to an end; and either the race succumbs to the morbid action of its own forces or some fresher race comes in, and begins the course of social evolution anew.

===Chinese===
一种不可避免地产生反常发展的关系，是不可能被永久维持的。性能量作为一种社会力量的强化，导致了性本能漫无限度的夸张：它在性方面的表现，是先进文明中种种反常的恶习；在社会方面的表现，是生产者与消费者之间那把社会撕裂为二的紧张的经济关系。性-经济关系把社会发展带到某一水平。一旦达到那一水平，就必须采用一种更高的关系，否则提升的过程便告终止；于是，要么这一种族屈服于自身诸力量的病态作用，要么某个更新鲜的种族进来，重新开始社会演化的历程。

===Original===
Under the stimulus of the sexuo-economic relation, one civilization after another has climbed up and fallen down in weary succession. It remains for us to develop a newer, better form of sex-relation and of economic relation therewith, and so to grasp the fruits of all previous civilizations, and grow on to the beautiful results of higher ones. The true and lasting social progress, beyond that which we have yet made, is based on a spirit of inter-human love, not merely the intersexual; and it requires an economic machinery organized and functioned for human needs, not sexual ones. The sexuo-economic relation drives man up to where he can become fully human. It deepens and develops the human soul until it is able to conceive and fulfil the larger social uses in which our further life must find expression. But, unless the human soul sees these new forces, feels them, gives way to them in loyal service, it fails to reach the level from which all further progress must proceed, and falls back. Again and again society has so risen, so failed to grasp new duties, so fallen back.

===Chinese===
在性-经济关系的刺激之下，一个又一个文明疲惫地相继攀起、又跌落。留待我们去做的，是发展出一种更新、更好的性关系形式、及与之相随的经济关系形式，从而把握住此前一切文明的果实，继续生长到更高文明的美善境地。超出我们至今所已取得的、真正而持久的社会进步，奠基于一种人对人的爱的精神，而不仅仅是性对性的爱；它还需要一部为人的需要、而非为性的需要而组织并运转的经济机器。性-经济关系把人驱赶到他能够完全成人的境地。它加深并发展人的灵魂，直到它能够构想并履行那些更大的社会用途——我们此后的生命必须在其中找到表达。但是，人的灵魂若看不见这些新的力量，感受不到它们，不肯在忠实的服役中向它们让路，它便达不到那一水平——一切进一步的进步都必须从那里出发的水平——而跌落回去。社会一次又一次地这样升起，这样错失新的义务，这样跌落回去。

===Original===
Today it will not so fall again, because the social consciousness is at last so vital a force in both men and women that we feel clearly that our human life cannot be fully lived on sex-lines only. We are so far individualized, so far socialized, that men can work without the tearing spur of exaggerated sex-stimulus, work for someone besides mate and young; and women can love and serve without the slavery of economic dependence﻿—love better and serve more. Sex-stimulus begins and ends in individuals. The social spirit is a larger thing, a better thing, and brings with it a larger, nobler life than we could ever know on a sex-basis solely.

===Chinese===
今天，它不会再这样跌落了，因为社会意识终于在男人和女人身上都成了一股有生气的力量，使我们清楚地感到：我们的人类生活不能只在性的路线上充分展开。我们已经个体化到如此地步、社会化到如此地步，以致男人们能够无需那被夸张了的性刺激撕裂式的鞭策而劳作，为配偶与幼小之外的什么人而劳作；女人们也能够去爱、去服务，而无需经济依附的奴役——爱得更好，服务得更多。性刺激始于个体、终于个体。社会精神是一件更广大的东西、一件更美好的东西，它带来一种更广大、更高尚的生活，远非我们单凭性的基础所能知晓。

===Original===
Moreover, it should be distinctly understood, as it is already widely and vaguely felt, that the higher development of social life following the economic independence of women makes possible a higher sex-life than has ever yet been known. As fast as the human individual rises in social progress to a certain degree of development, so fast this primitive form of sex-union chafes and drags: it is felt to be unsatisfying and injurious. This is a marked feature in modern life. The long, sure, upward trend of the human race toward monogamous marriage is no longer helped, but hindered by the economic side of the relation. The best marriage is between the best individuals; and the best individuals of both sexes today are increasingly injured by the economic basis of our marriage, which produces and maintains those qualities in men and women and their resultant industrial conditions which make marriage more difficult and precarious every day.

===Chinese===
再者，应当明确理解——这一点如今已被广泛而朦胧地感到——随着妇女经济独立（economic independence）而来的社会生活的更高发展，使一种迄今未有的更高的性生活成为可能。人类个体在社会进步中每上升到一定程度的发展，这种原始的性结合形式便以同样的速度磨损着、拖曳着：人们感到它不能令人满足，而且有害。这是现代生活的一个显著特征。人类种族朝向一夫一妻制婚姻（monogamous marriage）的那漫长而确实向上的趋势，不再被这一关系的经济一翼所助，反为所阻。最好的婚姻存在于最好的个体之间；而今日两性中最好的个体，正日益为我们婚姻的经济基础所伤害——这基础产生并维持着男女身上那些品质及其导致的产业状况，使婚姻一天比一天更艰难、更不稳。

===Original===
The woman’s movement, then, should be hailed by every right-thinking, farseeing man and woman as the best birth of our century. The banner advanced proclaims “equality before the law,” woman’s share in political freedom; but the main line of progress is and has been toward economic equality and freedom. While life exists on earth, the economic conditions must underlie and dominate each existing form and its activities; and social life is no exception. A society whose economic unit is a sex-union can no more develop beyond a certain point industrially than a society like the patriarchal, whose political unit was a sex-union, could develop beyond a certain point politically.

===Chinese===
因此，妇女运动理应被每一个思想端正、目光深远的人——男人和女人——欢呼为本世纪最好的新生。它推进的旗帜宣告着「法律面前的平等」，即妇女在政治自由中的份额；但进步的主线一向是、如今仍是朝向经济的平等与自由。只要地球上还存在生命，经济条件就必然居于一切现存形式及其活动之下并支配之；社会生活也不例外。一个以性结合为经济单位的社会，在产业上不可能发展到超过某一点；正如父权制式的社会——其政治单位曾是性结合——在政治上不可能发展到超过某一点。

===Original===
The last freeing of the individual makes possible the last combination of individuals. While sons must bend to the will of a patriarchal father, no democracy is possible. Democracy means, requires, is, individual liberty. While the sexuo-economic relation makes the family the centre of industrial activity, no higher collectivity than we have today is possible. But, as women become free, economic, social factors, so becomes possible the full social combination of individuals in collective industry. With such freedom, such independence, such wider union, becomes possible also a union between man and woman such as the world has long dreamed of in vain.

===Chinese===
个体的最后一次解放，使个体的最后一次联合成为可能。只要儿子们还必须屈从于父权制父亲的意志，民主便无可能。民主意味着、要求着、也就是个人自由。只要性-经济关系还使家庭居产业活动的中心，便不可能有比我们今日更高的集体性。但是，随着妇女成为自由的、经济的、社会的因素，个体在集体产业中的充分社会联合便成为可能。有了这样的自由、这样的独立、这样更宽广的联合，男人与女人之间的一种结合也随之成为可能——那样的结合，正是这个世界久已梦寐而不能得的。

## VIII / 八

===Original===
In the face of so vital and radical a change in human life as this change of economic base in the position of women, it is well to call attention more at length to the illustrations of everyday facts in our common lives, which he who runs may read, if he knows how to read. We do not, as a rule, know how to read the most important messages to humanity﻿—the signs of the times. Historic crises, which have been slowly maturing, burst upon us in sudden birth before the majority of the people imagine that anything is going on. The first gun fired at Fort Sumter was an extreme surprise to most of the citizens of the Union. The Boston Tea Party was, no doubt, an unaccountable piece of insolence to many worthy Britons. When “the deluge” did pour over the noblesse of France, few had been really foreseeing enough to avoid it.

===Chinese===
面对妇女地位的经济基础之改变这样一场关乎人类生活、如此重大而根本的变革，我们很有必要更充分地唤起人们对日常生活中种种实例的注意——这些实例，凡知道怎样读的人，随跑随读便能读懂。而我们通常并不知道怎样去读那些发给人类的最重要讯息——时代的种种征兆。历史上种种缓慢成熟的危机，早在大多数人还料想不到正在发生什么事之前，便已骤然临盆，扑面而来。萨姆特堡（Fort Sumter）打响的第一炮，对联邦的大多数公民而言是一场极大的意外。波士顿倾茶事件（Boston Tea Party），对许多可敬的英国人而言，无疑是一件无从解释的无礼行径。而当「大洪水」当真漫过法国贵族的头顶时，真正有先见之明而得以避开它的人，寥寥无几。

===Original===
Fortunately, the laws of social evolution do not wait for our recognition or acceptance: they go straight on. And this greater and more important change than the world has ever seen, this slow emergence of the long-subverted human female to full racial equality, has been going on about us full long enough to be observed. It is seen more prominently in this country than in any other, for many reasons.

===Chinese===
所幸，社会演化的诸法则并不等待我们的承认或接受：它们径自前行。而这比世界历来所见任何变化都更重大、更重要的变化——这长期遭受颠覆的人类雌性向完全的种族平等的缓慢浮现——已在我们周围进行了足够长久，足资观察。这一变化在我们这个国家比在任何其他国家都更为醒目，其原因有许多。

===Original===
The Anglo-Saxon blood, that English mixture of which Tennyson sings﻿—“Saxon and Norman and Dane though we be,”﻿—is the most powerful expression of the latest current of fresh racial life from the north﻿—from those sturdy races where the women were more like men, and the men no less manly because of it. The strong, fresh spirit of religious revolt in the new church that protested against and broke loose from the old, woke and stirred the soul of woman as well as the soul of man, and in the equality of martyrdom the sexes learned to stand side by side. Then, in the daring and exposure, the strenuous labor and bitter hardship of the pioneer life of the early settlers, woman’s very presence was at a premium; and her labor had a high economic value. Sex-dependence was almost unfelt. She who molded the bullets, and loaded the guns while the men fired them, was co-defender of the home and young. She who carded and dyed and wove and spun was co-provider for the family. Men and women prayed together, worked together, and fought together in comparative equality. More than all, the development of democracy has brought to us the fullest individualization that the world has ever seen. Although politically expressed by men alone, the character it has produced is inherited by their daughters. The Federal Democracy in its organic union, reacting upon individuals, has so strengthened, freed, emboldened, the human soul in America that we have thrown off slavery, and with the same impulse have set in motion the long struggle toward securing woman’s fuller equality before the law.

===Chinese===
盎格鲁-撒克逊血统——丁尼生（Tennyson）所歌咏的那种英格兰混合血统：「尽管我们是撒克逊人、诺曼人和丹麦人」——是自北方涌来的新鲜种族生命的最新一股潮流最有力的表达——来自那些坚韧的种族：在那里，女人更像男人，而男人也并不因此稍减丈夫气。那个抗议旧教会并与之决裂的新教会，其强烈而新鲜的宗教反叛精神，既唤醒并激荡了男人的灵魂，也唤醒并激荡了女人的灵魂；而在殉道的平等之中，两性学会了并肩而立。其后，在早期移民拓荒生活的冒险露宿、辛劳与苦楚之中，女人的存在本身便极受珍视，她的劳动有着很高的经济价值。性依附几乎无从察觉。那些为男人熔铸弹丸、在他们开枪时装填弹药的女人，是家园与幼小者的共同保卫者。那些梳棉、染色、织布、纺线的女人，是家庭的共同供养者。男人与女人一同祈祷、一同劳作、一同战斗，处在相对的平等之中。更重要的，是民主的发展给我们带来了世界迄今所见最充分的个体化（individualization）。它虽只在政治上由男人单独表达，它所造就的品格却由他们的女儿承继。联邦民主制（Federal Democracy）以其有机的联合反作用于个体，已如此地强化、解放、鼓舞了美国的人的灵魂，以至于我们甩掉了奴隶制，又怀着同一股冲动，发动了那场为妇女争取法律面前更充分平等的漫长斗争。

===Original===
This struggle has been carried on unflaggingly for fifty years, and fast nears its victorious end. It is not only in the four States where full suffrage is exercised by both sexes, nor in the twenty-four where partial suffrage is given to women, that we are to count progress; but in the changes legal and social, mental and physical, which mark the advance of the mother of the world toward her full place. Have we not all observed the change even in size of the modern woman, with its accompanying strength and agility? The Gibson Girl and the Duchess of Towers﻿—these are the new women; and they represent a noble type, indeed. The heroines of romance and drama today are of a different sort from the Evelinas and Arabellas of the last century. Not only do they look differently, they behave differently. The false sentimentality, the false delicacy, the false modesty, the utter falseness of elaborate compliment and servile gallantry which went with the other falsehoods﻿—all these are disappearing. Women are growing honester, braver, stronger, more healthful and skilful and able and free, more human in all ways.

===Chinese===
这场斗争已不屈不挠地进行了五十年，并正迅速逼近其胜利的终点。我们要计的进步，不仅在两性都行使完全选举权的那四个州，也不在给予妇女部分选举权的那二十四个州，而在那些法律与社会的、精神与身体的变化之中——它们标志着世界之母朝向其完整位置的行进。现代女性连身量都变了，还伴随着气力与敏捷——这变化我们谁不曾亲眼见到？吉布森女郎（Gibson Girl）与塔楼公爵夫人（Duchess of Towers）——这些就是新女性；而且她们确乎代表了一种高贵的类型。今日传奇与戏剧中的女主人公，已不同于上个世纪的那些伊芙琳娜（Evelina）们与阿拉贝拉（Arabella）们。她们不仅看起来不同了，举止行为也不同了。虚假的感伤，虚假的娇柔，虚假的羞怯，还有与这些虚假相伴行的、繁文缛节的恭维与卑躬屈膝的献殷勤之彻头彻尾的虚假——这一切都正在消失。女人们正变得更诚实、更勇敢、更强壮、更健康、更灵巧、更能干、更自由，在一切方面都更是人了。

===Original===
The change in education is in large part a cause of this, and progressively a consequence. Day by day the bars go down. More and more the field lies open for the mind of woman to glean all it can, and it has responded most eagerly. Not only our pupils, but our teachers, are mainly women. And the clearness and strength of the brain of the woman prove continually the injustice of the clamorous contempt long poured upon what was scornfully called “the female mind.” There is no female mind. The brain is not an organ of sex. As well speak of a female liver.

===Chinese===
教育的变化，在很大程度上是上述变化的原因，又渐进地是其结果。樊篱日复一日地拆除。任凭女性的心智尽量拾穗的田野日甚一日地敞开，而它也报以最热切的回应。不仅是我们的学生，连我们的教师也主要是女性。而女性大脑的清晰与有力，不断地证明着长久倾泻在那被讥称为「女性心智」之物上的喧嚣鄙夷是何等不公。世上并没有什么女性心智。大脑不是一个性的器官。这就好比说什么女性的肝脏。

===Original===
Woman’s progress in the arts and sciences, the trades and professions, is steady; but it is most unwise to claim from these relative advances the superiority of women to men, or even their equality, in these fields. What is more to the purpose and easily to be shown is the superiority of the women of today to those of earlier times, the immense new development of racial qualities in the sex. No modern proverbs, if we expressed ourselves in proverbs now, would speak with such sweeping, unbroken contumely of the women of today as did those unerring exhibitors of popular feeling in former times.

===Chinese===
女性在艺术与科学、行业与专业中的进步是稳步的；但若凭这些相对的进展便断言女性在这些领域优于男性、甚至与男性比肩，那是极不明智的。更切中要害、也更容易表明的是：今日的女性优于往昔的女性——这一性别之中种族品质的巨大新发展。倘若今天我们还以谚语表达自己，也没有哪条现代谚语，会像昔日那些大众情感准确无误的显示器那样，以那般一概而无间断的辱骂来谈论今日的妇女。

===Original===
The popular thought of our day is voiced in fiction, fluent verse, and an incessant play of humor. By what is freely written by most authors and freely read by most people is shown our change in circumstances and change in feeling. In old romances the woman was nothing save beautiful, highborn, virtuous, and perhaps “accomplished.” She did nothing but love and hate, obey or disobey, and be handed here and there among villain, hero, and outraged parent, screaming, fainting, or bursting into floods of tears as seemed called for by the occasion.

===Chinese===
我们这个时代的大众思想，由小说、流畅的诗句与无休止的幽默戏谑道出。多数作者自由写下的、多数人自由阅读的东西，显示出我们境况的改变与情感的改变。在旧的传奇里，女人除了美丽、出身高贵、贞静贤淑、或许还「多才多艺」之外，便什么也不是。她什么都不做，只是去爱与恨、服从或不服从，在恶棍、英雄与被激怒的家长之间被推来递去，随着场合的需要而尖叫、晕厥，或泪如泉涌。

===Original===
In the fiction of today women are continually taking larger place in the action of the story. They are given personal characteristics beyond those of physical beauty. And they are no longer content simply to *be*: they *do*. They are showing qualities of bravery, endurance, strength, foresight, and power for the swift execution of well-conceived plans. They have ideas and purposes of their own; and even when, as in so many cases described by the more reactionary novelists, the efforts of the heroine are shown to be entirely futile, and she comes back with a rush to the self-effacement of marriage with economic dependence, still the efforts were there. Disapprove as he may, use his art to oppose and contemn as he may, the true novelist is forced to chronicle the distinctive features of his time; and no feature is more distinctive of this time than the increasing individualization of women. With lighter touch, but with equally unerring truth, the wit and humor of the day show the same development. The majority of our current jokes on women turn on their “newness,” their advance.

===Chinese===
在今日的小说里，女性在故事行动中所占的位置不断增大。她们被赋予了形貌之美以外的个人特质。而且她们不再满足于单纯地*存在*（*be*）：她们要*做事*（*do*）。她们表现出勇敢、坚忍、力量、远见，以及将深思熟虑的计划迅速付诸执行的才能。她们有自己的想法与目的；而且即便如较为反动的小说家们所描绘的那么多情形那样，女主人公的努力被证明全然徒劳，她又急匆匆地回到那自我隐没的、带着经济依附（economic dependence）的婚姻中去，那些努力毕竟存在过。真正的小说家，无论他多么不以为然，无论他怎样运用自己的艺术去反对与非难——都不得不为他时代的独特面貌作编年；而没有一个面貌比女性的日益个体化更能标志这个时代了。以较轻灵的笔触、却同样确凿的真实，当日的机智与幽默也显示着同一发展。我们眼下关于女人的笑话，大多围着她们的「新」打转，围着她们的进步打转。

===Original===
No sociological change equal in importance to this clearly marked improvement of an entire sex has ever taken place in one century. Under it all, the crux of the whole matter, goes on the one great change, that of the economic relation. This follows perfectly natural lines. Just as the development of machinery constantly lowers the importance of mere brute strength of body and raises that of mental power and skill, so the pressure of industrial conditions demands an ever-higher specialization, and tends to break up that relic of the patriarchal age﻿—the family as an economic unit.

===Chinese===
在一个世纪之内，从未有过任何社会学变化，其重要性足以与这整个性别之清晰可辨的进步等量齐观。而在这一切之下，作为整桩事情的关键，进行着那一个伟大的变化——经济关系（economic relation）的变化。它循着全然自然的路线。正如机械的发展不断降低单纯蛮力的重要、提升心力与技艺的重要，产业状况的压力也要求着愈来愈高的专门化，并倾向于瓦解父权时代的遗物——作为经济单位的家庭。

===Original===
Women have been led under pressure of necessity into a most reluctant entrance upon fields of economic activity. The sluggish and greedy disposition bred of long ages of dependence has by no means welcomed the change. Most women still work only as they “have to,” until they can marry and “be supported.” Men, too, liking the power that goes with money, and the poor quality of gratitude and affection bought with it, resent and oppose the change; but all this disturbs very little the course of social progress.

===Chinese===
女性是在必要性的压力之下，被引向极不情愿地进入诸经济活动领域的。长期依附所养成的怠惰与贪得的脾性，绝没有欢迎这一变化。多数女人仍然只在「不得不」时才工作，直到能够嫁人、「受人供养」。男人们呢，喜爱随金钱而来的权力，以及用它买来的那种劣质的感激与柔情，也愤懑并反对这一变化；但这一切对社会进步的进程扰动甚微。

===Original===
A truer spirit is the increasing desire of young girls to be independent, to have a career of their own, at least for a while, and the growing objection of countless wives to the pitiful asking for money, to the beggary of their position. More and more do fathers give their daughters, and husbands their wives, a definite allowance﻿—a separate bank account﻿—something which they can play is all their own. The spirit of personal independence in the women of today is sure proof that a change has come.

===Chinese===
一种更真实的精神，是年轻姑娘们对独立的日益增长的渴望——想拥有一份自己的事业，哪怕只拥有一阵子；是无数妻子对那可怜兮兮的伸手讨钱、对自己地位中的乞讨性的日益增长的抗拒。越来越多的父亲给女儿、丈夫给妻子一份固定的津贴——一个单独的银行账户——一件她们可以权且当作完完全全归自己所有的东西。今日妇女身上的个人独立精神，确凿地证明变化已然到来。

===Original===
For a while the introduction of machinery which took away from the home so many industries deprived woman of any importance as an economic factor; but presently she arose, and followed her lost wheel and loom to their new place, the mill. Today there is hardly an industry in the land in which some women are not found. Everywhere throughout America are women workers outside the unpaid labor of the home, the last census giving three million of them. This is so patent a fact, and makes itself felt in so many ways by so many persons, that it is frequently and widely discussed. Without here going into its immediate advantages or disadvantages from an industrial point of view, it is merely instanced as an undeniable proof of the radical change in the economic position of women that is advancing upon us. She is assuming new relations from year to year before our eyes; but we, seeing all social facts from a personal point of view, have failed to appreciate the nature of the change.

===Chinese===
有一段时期，机械的引进把如此之多的劳作从家庭中夺走，剥夺了女性作为经济要素（economic factor）的任何重要性；但不久她便起身，追随着她失去的纺轮与织机，来到它们的新去处——工厂。今天这片土地上，几乎没有任何一个行业找不到女性的身影。遍及美国，处处都有家庭无酬劳动之外的女性劳动者，最近一次人口普查给出的数字是三百万。这是如此显豁的一桩事实，又以如此多的方式被如此多的人感受到，以致它被频繁而广泛地讨论着。在这里且不谈它从产业观点看的眼前利弊；它只是被引作一个不可否认的证明，证明妇女的经济地位那场正朝我们推进的根本变化。她正在我们眼前一年又一年地承担起新的关系；但我们惯于从个人的观点看一切社会事实，未能体察这一变化的性质。

===Original===
Consider, too, the altered family relation which attends this movement. Entirely aside from the strained relation in marriage, the other branches of family life feel the strange new forces, and respond to them. “When I was a girl,” sighs the gray-haired mother, “we sisters all sat and sewed while mother read to us. Now every one of my daughters has a different club!” She sighs, be it observed. We invariably object to changed conditions in those departments of life where we have established ethical values. For all the daughters to sew while the mother read aloud to them was esteemed right; and, therefore, the radiating diffusion of daughters among clubs is esteemed wrong﻿—a danger to home life. In the period of the common sewing and reading the women so assembled were closely allied in industrial and intellectual development as well as in family relationship. They all could do the same work, and liked to do it. They all could read the same book, and liked to read it. (And reading, half a century ago, was still considered half a virtue and the other half a fine art.) Hence the ease with which this group of women entered upon their common work and common pleasure.

===Chinese===
也请想一想伴随这场运动而来的改变了的家庭关系。完全撇开婚姻中那紧张的关系不谈，家庭生活的其他分支也感到了这些陌生的新力量，并对之作出回应。「我做姑娘的时候，」灰发的母亲叹道，「我们姊妹几个都坐着做针线，母亲念书给我们听。可如今我的女儿们，一人有一个不一样的俱乐部！」请注意，她是在叹息。在那些我们已确立了伦理价值的生活领域，我们对改变了的境况总是不满。所有女儿坐着做针线、母亲高声念给她们听，被视为正当；因此，女儿们向各家俱乐部四散辐射，便被视为不当——是对家庭生活的威胁。在共同做针线、共同读书的那个时期，这样聚在一起的女人们，在产业与智识的发展上、也在家庭亲属关系上紧密相连。她们全都能做同样的活计，而且乐意做。她们全都能读同一本书，而且乐意读。（而在半个世纪前，读书还被视为半是美德、半是高雅艺术。）因此，这群女人才那样轻易地投入她们共同的劳作与共同的乐趣。

===Original===
The growing individualization of democratic life brings inevitable change to our daughters as well as to our sons. Girls do not all like to sew, many do not know how. Now to sit sewing together, instead of being a harmonizing process, would generate different degrees of restlessness, of distaste, and of nervous irritation. And, as to the reading aloud, it is not so easy now to choose a book that a well-educated family of modern girls and their mother would all enjoy together. As the race become more specialized, more differentiated, the simple lines of relation in family life draw with less force, and the more complex lines of relation in social life draw with more force; and this is a perfectly natural and desirable process for women as well as for men.

===Chinese===
民主生活日益增长的个体化，给我们的儿子带来无可避免的改变，也给我们的女儿带来无可避免的改变。姑娘们并不都喜欢做针线，许多人根本不会。如今再让她们坐在一起做针线，非但不再是一种彼此调和的工序，反而会生出程度不等的躁动、厌倦与神经性的恼怒。至于高声念书，如今要挑出一本能让一个受过良好教育的现代女孩之家连同她们的母亲都一同喜爱的书，也不那么容易了。随着种族变得愈加专门化、愈加分化，家庭生活中那些简单的联系线索拉力渐弱，而社会生活中那些更复杂的联系线索拉力渐强；这对于女人和对于男人一样，都是一个全然自然而且可取的过程。

===Original===
It may be suggested, in passing, that one of the causes of “Americanitis” is this increasing nervous strain in family relation, acting especially upon woman. As she becomes more individualized, she suffers more from the primitive and undifferentiated conditions of the family life of earlier times. What “a wife” and “a mother” was supposed to find perfectly suitable, this newly specialized wife and mother, who is also a personality, finds clumsy and ill-fitting﻿—a mitten where she wants a glove. The home cares and industries, still undeveloped, give no play for her increasing specialization. Where the embryonic combination of cook-nurse-laundress-chambermaid-housekeeper-waitress-governess was content to be “jack of all trades” and mistress of none, the woman who is able to be one of these things perfectly, and by so much less able to be all the others, suffers doubly from not being able to do what she wants to do, and from being forced to do what she does not want to do. To the delicately differentiated modern brain the jar and shock of changing from trade to trade a dozen times a day is a distinct injury, a waste of nervous force. With the larger socialization of the woman of today, the fitness for and accompanying desire for wider combinations, more general interest, more organized methods of work for larger ends, she feels more and more heavily the intensely personal limits of the more primitive home duties, interests, methods. And this pain and strain must increase with the advance of women until the new functional power makes to itself organic expression, and the belated home industries are elevated and organized, like the other necessary labors of modern life.

===Chinese===
