It belittles his impressions of life at the start. It accustoms him to magnify the personal duties and minify the social ones, and it greatly retards his adjustment to larger life. This servant-motherhood, with all its unavoidable limitation and ill results, is the concomitant of the economic dependence of woman upon man, the direct and inevitable effect of the sexuo-economic relation.

===Chinese===
正是家务劳作中这种私人性，使它无可救药地居于低下。努力与达成之间那短近的射程，对个人需要那须臾不离的照管，对男人有害，对女人更有害，对孩子最有害。它在起头处就贬小了他对生活的印象。它使他惯于放大个人的职责、缩小社会的职责，并大大阻碍他对更广大生活的适应。这种仆役式的母职，连同它一切不可避免的局限与恶果，正是妇女对男人经济依附（economic dependence）的伴生物，是性-经济关系（sexuo-economic relation）直接的、不可避免的结果。

===Original===
The child is affected by it during his most impressionable years, and feels the effect throughout life. The woman is permanently retarded by it; the man, less so, because of his normal social activities, wherein he is under more developing influence. But he is injured in great degree, and our whole civilization is checked and perverted.

===Chinese===
孩子在感受力最强的岁月里受它浸染，并终身感到这影响。女人因它而永久地受阻滞；男人受的阻滞较轻，因为他有正常的社会活动，在其中置身于更多促其发展的影响之下。但他也受到很大程度的伤害，而我们的整个文明则被遏阻、被带偏。

===Original===
We suffer also, our lives long, from an intense self-consciousness, from a sensitiveness beyond all need; we demand measureless personal attention and devotion, because we have been born and reared in a very hotbed of these qualities. A baby who spent certain hours of every day among other babies, being cared for because he was a baby, and not because he was “my baby,” would grow to have a very different opinion of himself from that which is forced upon each new soul that comes among us by the ceaseless adoration of his own immediate family. What he needs to learn at once and for all, to learn softly and easily, but inexorably, is that he is one of many. We all dimly recognize this in our praise of large families, and in our saying that “an only child is apt to be selfish.” So is an only family. The earlier and more easily a child can learn that human life means many people, and their behavior to one another, the happier and stronger and more useful his life will be.

===Chinese===
我们还终身苦于一种强烈的自我意识，苦于一种全无必要的敏感；我们要求无边无际的个人关注与个人奉献，因为我们正是生养于这些品质的温床之中。一个每天在别的婴孩中间度过若干时辰、因为他是孩子、而不是因为他是「我的宝宝」而受照料的孩子，长大以后对自己所抱的见解，会同每个来到我们中间的新灵魂被迫抱有的见解大不相同——后者被迫领受的，是自己小家庭那无休无止的膜拜。他需要一劳永逸地学会、需要温柔轻省地、然而毫不通融地学会的是：他只是许多人中的一个。我们在对多子之家的赞许里、在「独生子往往自私」这句老话里，都影影绰绰地承认了这一点。独生的家庭也是如此。一个孩子越是早、越是容易懂得人类生活意味着许多人、意味着人们彼此相待的行为，他的一生就越幸福、越坚强、也越有用。

===Original===
This could be taught him with no difficulty whatever, under certain conditions, just as he is taught his present sensitiveness and egotism by the present conditions. It is not only temperature and diet and rest and exercise which affect the baby. “He does love to be noticed,” we say. “He is never so happy as when he has a dozen worshippers around him.” But what is the young soul learning all the while? What does he gather, as he sees and hears and slowly absorbs impressions? With the inflexible inferences of a clear, young brain, unsupplied with any counter-evidence until later in life, he learns that women are meant to wait on people, to get dinner, and sweep and pick up things; that men are made to bring home things, and are to be begged of according to circumstances; that babies are the object of concentrated admiration; that their hair, hands, feet, are specially attractive; that they are the heated focus of attention, to be passed from hand to hand, swung and danced and amused most violently, and also be laid aside and have nothing done to them, with no regard to their preference in either case.

===Chinese===
在某些条件下，教他这一点毫无难处，正如在现行条件下，他被教出了现在这份敏感与自负。影响婴儿的不只是温度、饮食、休息与活动。「他就是爱被人关注，」我们说。「身边围上一打崇拜者，他再没有更开心的时候了。」可这颗幼小的灵魂一直在学些什么呢？他看啊、听啊、慢慢吸收着印象，都领会到了什么呢？凭一颗清澈年轻的头脑那不容通融的推论——在日后生活之前得不到任何反证——他学到的是：女人生来是伺候人的，是做饭、扫地、拾掇东西的；男人生来是往家里带东西的，得看情形去求他们；婴孩是众人凝神赞叹的对象；他们的头发、小手、小脚格外惹人爱；他们是众目灼灼的焦点，被一双双手递来递去，被使劲地摇、使劲地颠、使劲地逗，又被搁在一边、没人理睬——两种情形都全不顾他们的喜好。

===Original===
And then, in the midst of all this tingling self-consciousness and desire for loving praise, he learns that he is “naughty”! The grief, the shame, the anger at injustice, the hopeless bewilderment, the morbid sensitiveness of conscience or the stolid dulling of it, the gradual retirement of the baffled brain from all these premature sensations to a contentment with mere personal gratification and a growing ingenuity in obtaining it—all these experiences are the common lot of the child among us, our common lot when we were children. Of course, we don’t remember. Of course, we loved our mother, and thought her perfect. Comparisons among mothers are difficult for a baby. Of course, we loved our homes, and never dreamed of any other way of being “brought up.” And, of course, when we have children of our own, we bring them up in the same way. What other way is there? What is there to be said on the subject? Children always were brought up at home. Isn’t that enough?

===Chinese===
而后，就在这一片刺痛的自我意识与对慈爱夸赞的渴求之中，他又学到了他是「不乖」的！那悲伤，那羞耻，那对不公的愤怒，那无可奈何的惶惑，那良心病态的敏感——或它麻木的钝化，那受挫的头脑从这一切早熟的感触中渐渐退身，退到只求个人快意的知足、以及在求得这快意上日益增长的机巧——这些经历全是我们中间孩子的共同命运，是我们幼年时的共同命运。当然，我们都不记得了。当然，我们爱我们的母亲，觉得她十全十美。叫婴儿在母亲们之间作比较是很难的。当然，我们爱我们的家宅，做梦也想不到还有别的「带大」法。于是当然，等我们有了自己的孩子，我们又照原样把他们带大。还能有什么别的法子呢？这事还有什么可说的呢？孩子从来都是在家里带大的。这难道还不够吗？

===Original===
And yet, insidiously, slowly, irresistibly, while we flatter ourselves that things remain the same, they are changing under our very eyes from year to year, from day to day. Education, hiding itself behind a wall of books, but consisting more and more fully in the grouping of children and in the training of faculties never mentioned in the curriculum—education, which is our human motherhood, has crept nearer and nearer to its true place, its best work—the care and training of the little child. Some women there are, and some men, whose highest service to humanity is the care of children. Such should not concentrate their powers upon their own children alone—a most questionable advantage—but should be so placed that their talent and skill, their knowledge and experience, would benefit the largest number of children. Many women there are, and many men, who, though able to bring forth fine children, are unable to educate them properly. Simply to bear children is a personal matter—an animal function. Education is collective, human, a social function.

===Chinese===
然而，阴移暗转、缓慢而不容抗拒地，正当我们自喜诸事如故时，它们正在我们眼皮底下一年年、一天天地改变着。教育——它藏在书本这堵墙后面，实则愈来愈充分地体现为孩子们的编组、体现为对课程表上从不提及的诸种官能的训练——这教育，我们人类的母职，已悄悄爬到离它真正的位置、它最好的工作——幼小孩子的照料与训练——越来越近的地方。确有一些女人，也有一些男人，他们对人类最高尚的服务便是照料孩子。这样的人不该把力量只集中于自己的孩子身上——那好处大可怀疑——而该被安置得使他们的才能与技艺、知识与经验，能惠及最大数目的孩子。也有许多女人、许多男人，虽然生得出好孩子，却教不好他们。单单生孩子是一件个人的事——一种动物的机能。教育则是集体的、人的，是一种社会机能。

===Original===
As we now arrange life, our children must take their chances while babies, and live or die, improve or deteriorate, according to the mother to whom they chance to be born. An inefficient mother does not prevent a child from having a good school education or a good college education; but the education of babyhood, the most important of all, is wholly in her hands. It is futile to say that mothers should be taught how to fulfil their duties. You cannot teach every mother to be a good school educator or a good college educator. Why should you expect every mother to be a good nursery educator? Whatever our expectations, she is not; and our mistrained babies, such of them as survive the maternal handling, grow to be such people as we see about us.

===Chinese===
在我们现在这样安排的生活里，我们的孩子在婴孩时期只能听凭运气，碰巧生在什么样的母亲手里，便或活或死、或进或退。一个不称职的母亲，碍不着孩子受良好的学校教育或良好的大学教育；可婴孩期的教育——那最重要的一切——却全在她手里。说母亲们应当受教以尽其职，是一句空话。你无法把每个母亲都教成好的学校教师或好的大学教师，为什么却指望每个母亲都是好的保育教师呢？无论我们怎样指望，她都不是；而我们那些受错了训练的婴孩，凡在母亲的摆弄下幸存下来的，就长成了我们在四周看到的这种人。

===Original===
The growth and change in home and family life goes steadily on under and over and through our prejudices and convictions; and the education of the child has changed and become a social function, while we still imagine the mother to be doing it all.

===Chinese===
家宅与家庭生活的生长与变迁，从我们的偏见与成见底下、上面、中间穿行，稳稳地进行着；孩子的教育已然改变、已然成为一项社会机能，而我们还在想象这一切都由母亲一人做着。

===Original===
In its earliest and most rudimentary manifestations, education was but part of the individual maternal function of the female animal. But no sooner did the human mind begin to show capacity for giving and receiving its impressions through language (thus attaining the power of acquiring information through sources other than its own experience) than the individual mother ceased to be the sole educator. The young savage receives not only guidance from his anxious mother, but from the chiefs and elders of his tribe. For a long time the aged were considered the only suitable teachers, because the major part of knowledge was still derived from personal experience; and, of course, the older the person, the greater his experience, other things being equal, and they were rather equal then. This primitive notion still holds among us. People still assume superior wisdom because of superior age, putting mere number of experiences against a more essential and better arranged variety, and quite forgetting that the needed wisdom of today is not the accumulation of facts, but the power to think about them to some purpose.

===Chinese===
在其最早、最简陋的表现里，教育不过是雌性动物个体母性机能的一部分。可人类心智一经显露出通过语言传递并领受印象的能力（由此获得了从自身经验以外的来源获取信息的本事），个体的母亲便不再是唯一的教育者了。年幼的野蛮人不只从他那殷殷关切的母亲那里领受教导，也从他部落的首领与长老们那里领受。有很长一段时间，年长者被视为唯一合宜的教师，因为知识的大部分仍得自个人经验；而且，当然啦，在其他条件相等时——那时诸条件倒是相当相等的——人越老，经验越多。这种原始观念至今仍存留于我们中间。人们仍因年齿较高便自居智慧较高，拿经验的单纯数量去顶替更本质、安排得更妥当的多样性，全然忘了今日所需要的智慧，不是事实的堆积，而是就这些事实想出些名堂来的思考之力。

===Original===
With our increased power to preserve and transmit individual experience through literature, and to disseminate such information through systematic education, we see younger and younger people, more rich in, say, chemical or electrical experience than “the oldest inhabitant” could have been in earlier times. Therefore, the teacher of today is not the graybeard and beldame, but the man and woman most newly filled with the gathered experience of the world. As this change from age to youth has taken place in the teacher, it has also shown itself in the taught. Grown men frequented the academic groves of Greece. Youths filled the universities of the Middle Ages. Boys and, later, girls were given the increasing school advantages of progressive centuries.

===Chinese===
随着我们通过文学保存并传递个人经验、通过系统教育散布这类信息的力量日益增长，我们看到一批比一批更年轻的人，他们——比如说——在化学或电学上的经验，比早年间「最年老的居民」所能有的还要丰富。因此，今日的教师不再是白胡子老翁与干瘪老妪，而是最新注满了世界汇集之经验的男人与女人。教师身上发生的这种由老而少的变化，也同样显现在受教育者身上。成年男子常出入于希腊的学园林荫。青年们坐满了中世纪的大学。男孩们、再后来的女孩们，则得到了诸个进步世纪里日益增多的学校之利。

===Original===
Today the beautiful development of the kindergarten has brought education to the nursery door. Even our purblind motherhood is beginning to open that door; and we have at last entered upon the study of babyhood, its needs and powers, and are seeing that education begins with life itself. It is no new and daring heresy to suggest that babies need better education than the individual mother now gives them. It is simply a little further extension of the steadily expanding system of human education which is coming upon us, as civilization grows. And it no more infringes upon the mother’s rights, the mother’s duties, the mother’s pleasures, than does the college or the school.

===Chinese===
今天，幼儿园那美妙的发展已把教育送到了育婴室的门口。连我们这半盲的母职也开始把那扇门打开；我们终于着手研究婴孩时期——它的需要与能力——并且正看到教育与生命本身一同开始。说婴儿需要的比个体母亲如今所给的更好的教育，已算不上什么新奇大胆的异端。它不过是随文明生长而向我们涌来的人类教育制度稳步扩张中的又一小步延伸。而它对母亲的权利、母亲的职责、母亲的乐趣的触犯，并不比学院或学校更多。

===Original===
We think no harm of motherhood because our darlings go out each day to spend long hours in school. The mother is not held neglectful, nor the child bereft. It is not called a “separation of mother and child.” There would be no further harm or risk or loss in a babyhood passed among such changed surroundings and skilled service as should meet its needs more perfectly than it is possible for the mother to meet them alone at home.

===Chinese===
我们的心肝宝贝每天出门、在学校里消磨长长的时辰，我们并不因此认为母职受了什么损害。没有谁说母亲疏于照管，也没有谁说孩子被夺去了什么。没有人把这称作「母亲与孩子的分离」。倘若婴孩时期在那样一种改变了的环境与娴熟的服务中度过——它们能比母亲独自在家所能做到的更完满地满足婴孩的需要——也不会再有更多的伤害、风险或损失。

===Original===
Better surroundings and care for babies, better education, do not mean, as some mothers may imagine, that the tiny monthling is to be taught to read, or even that it is to be exposed to cabalistical arrangements of color and form and sound which shall mysteriously force the young intelligence to flower. It would mean, mainly, a far quieter and more peaceful life than is possible for the heavily loved and violently cared for baby in the busy household; and the impressions which it did meet would be planned and maintained with an intelligent appreciation of its mental powers. The mother would not be excluded, but supplemented, as she is now, by the teacher and the school.

===Chinese===
更好的环境与照护、更好的教育，并不意味着——如某些母亲可能想象的那样——要教那出生方一月的小东西认字，甚至也不意味着要把它暴露在色彩、形状与声音的种种神秘排列之前，好让那幼小的智力神秘地开花。它的意思主要是：一种远为安静、远为平和的生活，远非忙碌人家那个被爱得太重、被伺候得太猛的婴孩所能有；而它所遇到的种种印象，都将凭着对其心智能力的明智体察来筹划并维持。母亲不会被排除在外，而是像现在这样，由教师与学校来补足。

===Original===
Try and imagine for yourself, if you like, a new kind of coming alive—the mother breast and mother arms there, of course, fulfilling the service which no other, however tender, could supervene; but there would be other service also. The long, bright hours of the still widening days would find one in sunny, soft-colored rooms, or among the grass and flowers, or by the warm sand and waters. There would be about one more of one’s self, others of the same size and age, in restful, helpful companionship. A year means an enormous difference in the ages of babies. Think what a passion little children have for playmates of exactly their own age, because in them alone is perfect equality; and then think that the home-kept baby never has such companionship, unless, indeed, there are twins!

===Chinese===
不妨试着为你自己想象一种新式的降生人间——母亲的乳房与母亲的手臂自然都在那里，尽着那无论多温柔的人都无从替代的职分；可此外还会有别的服务。那日渐展宽的白昼里长长的、明亮的时辰，会让人置身于洒满阳光、色调柔和的房间里，或置身于草丛与花间，或在温暖的沙与水边。自己身旁还会有别的自己——同一般大小、同一般年纪的——彼此作安适而有益的伴。一岁之差，在婴孩的年纪上意味着天壤之别。想想小孩子对正巧同岁的玩伴怀有怎样的热情吧——因为唯有在他们中间才有完全的平等；再想想那个被关在家里的婴孩从来得不到这样的伴侣——除非当真生了双胞胎！

===Original===
In this larger grouping, in full companionship, the child would unconsciously absorb the knowledge that “we” were humanity, that “we” were creatures to be so fed, so watched, so laid to sleep, so kissed and cuddled and set free to roll and play. The mother-hours would be sweetest of all, perhaps. Here would be something wholly one’s own, and the better appreciated for the contrast. But the long, steady days would bring their peaceful lessons of equality and common interest instead of the feverish personality of the isolated one-baby household, or the innumerable tyrannies and exactions, the forced submissions and exclusions, of the nursery full of brothers and sisters of widely differing ages and powers. Mothers accustomed to consider many babies besides their own would begin, on the one hand, to learn something of mere general babyness, and so understand that stage of life far better, and, on the other, to outgrow the pathetic idolatry of the fabled crow—to recognize a difference in babies, and so to learn a new ideal in their great work of motherhood.

===Chinese===
在这样更大的编组里，在充分的伴侣之谊中，孩子会不知不觉地吸收这样的认识：「我们」就是人类，「我们」是就这样被喂食、就这样被看护、就这样被安放入睡、就这样被亲吻爱抚、又就这样被放开去打滚玩耍的生灵。母亲的时辰或许仍是一切中最甜的。那将是全然属于自己的东西，也因这份对照而更被珍惜。可那漫长而平稳的日子，会带来平等与共同利益的平和课程，来代替那孤零零独生婴孩之家的狂热个人性，或那满屋年龄能力悬殊的兄弟姐妹的育婴室里数不清的暴虐与强索、被迫的顺从与排挤。惯于在自家孩子之外还照看许多婴孩的母亲们，一方面会开始懂得一点婴孩之为婴孩的一般道理，从而远更好地理解生命的这一阶段；另一方面，也将挣脱对那传说中衔来婴儿之乌鸦的可悲膜拜——认出婴孩彼此之间的差异，从而在她们伟大的母职事业中学得一种新的理想。

===Original===
This alone is reason good for a wider maternity. As long as each mother dotes and gloats upon her own children, knowing no others, so long this animal passion overestimates or underestimates real human qualities in the child. So long as this endures, we must grow up with the false, unbalanced opinion of ourselves forced upon us in our infancy. We may think too well of ourselves or we may think too ill of ourselves; but we think always too much of ourselves, because of this untrained and unmodified concentration of maternal feeling. Our whole attitude toward the child is too intensely personal. Through all our aching later life we labor to outgrow the false perspective taught by primitive motherhood.

===Chinese===
单是这一条，便足以成为一种更宽广的母性的理由。只要每一位母亲还只溺爱着、贪看着自家的孩子而不识别的孩子，这种动物性的激情就会对孩子的真实人类品质或高估、或低估。只要这种情况延续下去，我们就必须在成长中随身带着婴孩时期强加给我们的那关于自己的虚假而不平衡的意见。我们可能把自己看得太高，也可能把自己看得太低；但我们永远把自己看得太多，全因这份未经训练、未经调节的母性情感的倾注。我们对孩子的整个态度私人性太强了。在后来整个苦痛的人生里，我们都劳苦着力图长出原始母职教给我们的那副虚假的透视。

===Original===
A baby, brought up with other babies, would never have that labor or that pain. However much his mother might love him, and he might enjoy her love, he would still find that for most of the time he was treated precisely like other people of the same age. Such a change would not involve any greater loss to home and family life than does the school or kindergarten. It would not rob the baby of his mother nor the mother of her baby. And such a change would give the mother certain free hours as a human being, as a member of a civilized community, as an economic producer, as a growing, self-realizing individual. This freedom, growth, and power will make her a wiser, stronger, and nobler mother.

===Chinese===
一个同别的婴孩一起带大的孩子，便永远不会有那份劳苦、那份疼痛。无论他的母亲怎样爱他、他怎样享用她的爱，他仍会发现：一天里的大部分时间，他受到的对待同别的同龄人一模一样。这样的改变，给家宅与家庭生活带来的损失，不会比学校或幼儿园带来的更多。它不会夺走婴孩的母亲，也不会夺走母亲的婴孩。而这样的改变，会还给母亲一些自由的时辰——作为一个人的、作为一个文明社会成员的、作为一个经济生产者的、作为一个生长着的、自我实现的个体的时辰。这自由、这生长、这力量，将使她成为一个更智慧、更坚强、更高贵的母亲。

===Original===
After all is said of loving gratitude to our unfailing mother-nurse, we must have a most exalted sense of our own personal importance so to canonize the service of ourselves. The mother as a social servant instead of a home servant will not lack in true mother duty. She will love her child as well, perhaps better, when she is not in hourly contact with it, when she goes from its life to her own life, and back from her own life to its life, with ever new delight and power. She can keep the deep, thrilling joy of motherhood far fresher in her heart, far more vivid and open in voice and eyes and tender hands, when the hours of individual work give her mind another channel for her own part of the day. From her work, loved and honored though it is, she will return to the home life, the child life, with an eager, ceaseless pleasure, cleansed of all the fret and friction and weariness that so mar it now.

===Chinese===
在对那从不懈怠的母亲乳母道尽了满怀爱意的感激之后，我们必须承认：非得对自己个人的重要怀有一种登峰造极之感，才会这样把我们自己的服侍奉为圣事。母亲作为社会的仆役而非家宅的仆役，不会亏缺真正的为母之职。当她不必与孩子时时相接，当她从他的生活走进自己的生活、又从自己的生活回到他的生活、怀着常新的喜悦与力量时，她会同样爱她的孩子——也许爱得更好。当个人的工作时段为她的一天把自己的心思开了另一条河道时，她便能把母职那深沉震颤的喜悦，远为新鲜地保存在心里，在声音里、眼睛里、温柔的手里，都远为鲜活而敞亮。从她的工作——那工作虽受着热爱与敬重——她将带着热切而不倦的欢愉，回到家居生活、孩子的生活中来，洗净了如今如此败坏着这一切的烦躁、摩擦与疲惫。

===Original===
The child, also, will feel this beneficent effect. It is a mistake to suppose that the baby, more than the older child, needs the direct care and presence of the mother. Careful experiment has shown that a newborn baby does not know its own mother, and that a new-made mother does not know her own baby. They have been changed without the faintest recognition on either side.

===Chinese===
孩子也会感到这有益的影响。以为婴孩比大孩子更需要母亲亲自的照料与亲临，是一种错误。细致的实验已经表明：新生儿并不认识自己的母亲，新做母亲的也不认识自己的婴儿。人们把婴儿调了包，双方都毫无半点察觉。

===Original===
The services of a foster-mother, a nurse, a grandma, are often liked by a baby as well as, and perhaps better than, those of its own mother. The mere bodily care of a young infant is as well given by one wise, loving hand as another. It is that trained hand that the baby needs, not mere blood-relationship. While the mother keeps her beautiful prerogative of nursing, she need never fear that any other will be dearer to the little heart than she who is the blessed provider of his highest known good. A healthy, happy, rightly occupied motherhood should be able to keep up this function longer than is now customary—to the child’s great gain. Aside from this special relationship, however, the baby would grow easily into the sense of other and wider relationship.

===Chinese===
乳母、护士、奶奶的服侍，常为婴孩所喜欢，喜欢的程度不亚于自己母亲的服侍，甚至可能更喜欢。对幼小婴儿身体上的照管，任何一只明智而慈爱的手都给得同另一只一样好。婴孩需要的是那受过训练的手，而不是单纯的血缘。只要母亲保有着哺乳这一美妙的天赋特权，她就永远不必害怕有谁会比她——那他已知至高福祉的有福供给者——更亲于那颗小小的心。一个健康、快乐、正业在身的母职，本应能把这一机能维持得比现在的惯例更久——那将使孩子大大受益。然而撇开这一层特殊关系不谈，婴孩会轻松地长入对别样的、更宽广的关系的感知之中。

===Original===
In the freedom and peace of his baby bedroom and baby parlor, in his easy association with others of his own age, he would absorb a sense of right human relation with his mother’s milk, as it were—a sense of others’ rights and of his own. Instead of finding life a place in which all the fun was in being carried round and “done to” by others, and a place also in which these others were a tyranny and a weariness unutterable; he would find life a place in which to spread out, unhindered, getting acquainted with his own unfolding powers of body and mind in an atmosphere of physical warmth and ease and of quiet peace of mind.

===Chinese===
在他那婴孩卧室与婴孩客厅的自由与安宁里，在同同龄人的轻松相处中，他会仿佛随着母亲的乳汁，一同吸进一种正当人间关系的意识——一种对他人权利与自身权利的意识。他将发现，生活不是一个这样的地方——在那里全部的乐趣在于被人抱来抱去、被人「摆弄」，在那里这些他人又是一场说不出的暴虐与疲惫；他将发现生活是一个可以不受阻碍地舒展开来的地方，在身体的温暖安适与心境的静穆平和之中，渐渐结识自己那层层展开的身心力量。

===Original===
Direct, concentrated, unvarying personal love is too hot an atmosphere for a young soul. Variations of loneliness, anger, and injustice, are not changes to be desired. A steady, diffused love, lighted with wisdom, based always on justice, and varied with rapturous draughts of our own mother’s depth of devotion, would make us into a new people in a few generations. The bent and reach of our whole lives are largely modified by the surroundings of infancy; and those surroundings are capable of betterment, though not to be attained by the individual mother in the individual home.

===Chinese===
直接、集中、一成不变的个人之爱，对一颗幼小的灵魂是太灼热的空气。以孤独、愤怒与不公来作变化，也不是可欲的改变。一种稳定而普施的爱，以智慧点亮，永远植根于公道，又不时注入我们自己母亲那深处奉献涌出的、令人心醉的杯盏——这样的爱，能在几代人之内把我们造就成一个新的人类。我们整个一生的取向与所及，大半为婴孩时期的环境所塑造；而那些环境是能够改善的——尽管不是由单独家宅里的单独母亲所能达成。

===Original===
There are three reasons why the individual mother can never be fit to take all the care of her children. The first two are so commonly true as to have much weight, the last so absolutely and finally true as to be sufficient in itself alone.

===Chinese===
个体母亲之所以永远无法胜任独自照料她孩子的一切，理由有三。前两条因其普遍成立而颇有分量，最后一条则绝对而终局地成立，单凭自身便已足够。

===Original===
First, not every woman is born with the special qualities and powers needed to take right care of children: she has not the talent for it. Second, not every woman can have the instruction and training needed to fit her for the right care of children: she has not the education for it. Third, while each woman takes all the care of her own children herself, no woman can ever have the requisite experience for it. That is the final bar. That is what keeps back our human motherhood. No mother knows more than her mother knew: no mother has ever learned her business; and our children pass under the well-meaning experiments of an endless succession of amateurs.

===Chinese===
第一，并非每个女人都生而具备妥善照料孩子所需的特殊品质与能力：她没有那份天才。第二，并非每个女人都能受到使她胜任妥善照料孩子的教导与训练：她没有那份教育。第三，只要每个女人还都亲自照料自己孩子的一切，就没有哪个女人能获得必要的经验。这便是最后的障碍。这便是拖住我们人类母职的东西。没有哪个母亲懂得比她母亲更多：没有哪个母亲曾经学过她这一行；而我们的孩子，便在一茬又一茬没完没了的业余人士那些好心的试验之下长大。

===Original===
We try to get “an experienced nurse.” We insist on “an experienced physician.” But our idea of an experienced mother is simply one who has borne many children, as if parturition was an educative process!

===Chinese===
我们设法请「有经验的护士」。我们坚持要「有经验的医生」。可我们心目中有经验的母亲，不过是生过许多孩子的女人，仿佛分娩是一个受教养的过程似的！

===Original===
To experience the pangs of childbirth, or the further pangs of a baby’s funeral, adds nothing whatever to the mother’s knowledge of the proper care, clothing, feeding, and teaching of the child. The educative department of maternity is not a personal function: it is in its very nature a social function; and we fail grievously in its fulfilment.

===Chinese===
经历分娩的阵痛，或再经历婴儿葬礼的进一步的痛，对母亲关于孩子恰当照料、衣着、喂哺与教导的知识，没有丝毫增益。生育的教育之部不是一种个人机能：它在本性上就是一种社会机能；而我们在履行它时，失职得可悲。

===Original===
The economically independent mother, widened and freed, strengthened and developed, by her social service, will do better service as mother than it has been possible to her before. No one thing could do more to advance the interests of humanity than the wiser care and wider love of organized human motherhood around our babies. This nobler mother, bearing nobler children, and rearing them in nobler ways, would go far toward making possible the world which we want to see. And this change is coming upon us overpoweringly in spite of our foolish fears.

===Chinese===
那经济独立的母亲，因她的社会服务而放宽了、自由了、强健了、发展了，她作为母亲将做出比她从前所能做到的更好的服务。再没有什么能比围绕我们婴儿四周的、有组织的人类母职那更智慧的照料与更宽广的爱，更能推进人类的利益了。这更高贵的母亲，生养更高贵的儿女，又以更高贵的方式养育他们，将大有助于使我们所愿看见的那个世界成为可能。而这变化，正不顾我们愚蠢的恐惧，排山倒海地向我们涌来。

## XIV / 十四

===Original===
The changes in our conception and expression of home life, so rapidly and steadily going on about us, involve many far-reaching effects, all helpful to human advancement. Not the least of these is the improvement in our machinery of social intercourse.

===Chinese===
我们关于家居生活的观念与表达，正在我们四周迅速而稳步地变化着；这些变化牵涉许多影响深远的后果，无一不有助于人类的进步。其中绝非微末的一桩，便是我们社会交往机制的改进。

===Original===
This necessity of civilization was unknown in those primitive ages when family intercourse was sufficient for all, and when any further contact between individuals meant war. Trade and its travel, the specialization of labor and the distribution of its products, with their ensuing development, have produced a wider, freer, and more frequent movement and interchange among the innumerable individuals whose interaction makes society. Only recently, and as yet but partially, have women as individuals come to their share of this fluent social intercourse which is the essential condition of civilization. It is not merely a pleasure or an indulgence: it is the human necessity.

===Chinese===
文明的这一必需，在那些原始时代里是无从知晓的：那时家庭之内的交往已足以应付一切，个人之间任何进一步的接触都意味着战争。贸易及其往来、劳动的专门化及其产品的分配，连同随之而来的种种发展，已在无数的个人之间造成了更宽广、更自由、更频繁的流动与交流——正是这些个人的相互作用构成了社会。只是到了晚近，女人们作为个人才开始部分地分得这种流畅的社会交往——它是文明的本质条件。这不仅仅是一种愉悦或一种放纵：这是人的必需。

===Original===
For women as individuals to meet men and other women as individuals, with no regard whatever to the family relation, is a growing demand of our time. As a social necessity, it is perforce being met in some fashion; but its right development is greatly impeded by the clinging folds of domestic and social customs derived from the sexuo-economic relation. The demand for a wider and freer social intercourse between the sexes rests, primarily, on the needs of their respective natures, but is developed in modern life to a far subtler and higher range of emotion than existed in the primitive state, where they had but one need and but one way of meeting it; and this demand, too, calls for a better arrangement of our machinery of living.

===Chinese===
女人作为个人、全然不顾家庭关系地与男人和其他女人相见，是我们时代一个日益增长的要求。作为一种社会必需，它正被迫以某种方式得到满足；但其正当的发展，却大受导源于性-经济关系（sexuo-economic relation）的家内习俗与社会习俗那层层缠裹的褶襞所阻遏。两性之间更宽广、更自由的社会交往，这一要求首先植根于双方各自天性的需要，但在现代生活中，它已发展到远比原始状态精微、高超的情感范围——在原始状态里，他们只有一种需要，也只有一条满足它的途径；而这一要求，也同样呼唤着对我们的生存机制作一番更好的安排。

===Original===
Always in social evolution, as in other evolution, the external form suited to earlier needs is but slowly outgrown; and the period of transition, while the new functions are fumbling through the old organs, and slowly forcing mechanical expression for themselves, is necessarily painful. So far in our development, acting on a deep-seated conviction that the world consisted only of families and the necessary business arrangements involved in providing for those families, we have conscientiously striven to build and plan for family advantage, and either unconsciously or grudgingly have been forced to make transient provision for individuals. Whatever did not tend to promote family life, and did tend to provide for the needs of individuals not at the time in family relation, we have deprecated in principle, though reluctantly forced to admit it in practice.

===Chinese===
在社会演化中，一如在其他演化中，适合于较早需要的外在形式总是被缓慢地长过去；而过渡的时期——新的机能还在旧器官里摸索前行、缓慢地为自己逼出机械表达的时期——必然是痛苦的。在我们迄今的发展中，我们抱着一个根深蒂固的信念行事：世界只由家庭，以及赡养这些家庭所必需的种种商务安排构成；于是我们本着良知孜孜营建与规划，为的是家庭的利益，而对个人的供给，则要么出于无心、要么勉勉强强地被迫做出一时的安排。凡不倾向于促进家庭生活、又倾向于供给那些当时不在家庭关系之中的个人之所需的，我们在原则上都加以贬抑，尽管在实践中又被迫勉强承认它。

===Original===
To this day articles are written, seriously and humorously, protesting against the increasing luxury and comfort of bachelor apartments for men, as well as against the pecuniary independence of women, on the ground that these conditions militate against marriage and family life. Most men, even now, pass through a period of perhaps ten years, when they are individuals, business calling them away from their parental family, and business not allowing them to start new families of their own. Women, also, more and more each year, are entering upon a similar period of individual life. And there is a certain permanent percentage of individuals, "odd numbers" and "broken sets," who fall short of family life or who are left over from it; and these need to live.

===Chinese===
时至今日，还有文章在写——既郑重其事、又带谐谑——抗议男人的独身公寓（bachelor apartment）日渐奢华舒适，也抗议女人金钱上的独立，理由是这些境况有损于婚姻与家庭生活。大多数男人，即便现在，都要经历一段或许长达十年的时期：在这段时期里他们是个人，营业把他们从父母之家召走，而营业又不允许他们自己另立新家。女人也一年比一年更多地进入一段类似的个人生活时期。此外，总有一定比例的常设的个人——那些「零单之数」与「残缺之套」（"odd numbers" and "broken sets"）——他们够不上家庭生活，或是从家庭生活里剩了下来；而这些人也需要生活。

===Original===
The residence hotel, the boardinghouse, club, lodging-house, and restaurant are our present provision for this large and constantly increasing class. It is not a travelling class. These are people who want to live somewhere for years at a time, but who are not married or otherwise provided with a family. Home life being in our minds inextricably connected with married life, a home being held to imply a family, and a family implying a head, these detached persons are unable to achieve any home life, and are thereby subjected to the inconvenience, deprivation, and expense, the often unhygienic, and sometimes immoral influences, of our makeshift substitutes.

===Chinese===
住宅旅馆（residence hotel）、供膳寄宿舍（boardinghouse）、俱乐部、租宿屋（lodging-house）与餐馆，是我们目前为这个庞大且不断增长的阶级所做的供给。这并不是一个旅行的阶级。这些人是想在某处一住经年的人，只是他们没有结婚，也没有以别的方式得到一个家庭。家居生活既在我们心中与婚姻生活纠缠不解地相连，家宅又被认为蕴含一个家庭，而家庭又蕴含一位家长，于是这些离析无属的个人便无从获得任何家居生活，从而遭受着我们那些权宜替代品的不便、匮乏与花费，遭受着其中常常不卫生、有时不道德的影响。

===Original===
What the human race requires is permanent provision for the needs of individuals, disconnected from the sex-relation. Our assumption that only married people and their immediate relatives have any right to live in comfort and health is erroneous. Every human being needs a home—bachelor, husband, or widower, girl, wife, or widow, young or old. They need it from the cradle to the grave, and without regard to sex-connections. We should so build and arrange for the shelter and comfort of humanity as not to interfere with marriage, and yet not to make that comfort dependent upon marriage. With the industries of home life managed professionally, with rooms and suites of rooms and houses obtainable by any person or persons desiring them, we could live singly without losing home comfort and general companionship, we could meet bereavement without being robbed of the common conveniences of living as well as of the heart's love, and we could marry in ease and freedom without involving any change in the economic base of either party concerned.

===Chinese===
人类这个种族所需要的，是与性关系（sex-relation）脱钩的、对个人需要的永久性供给。我们假定唯有已婚者及其近亲才有权活得舒适健康，这一假定是错误的。每一个人都需要一个家宅——独身者、丈夫或鳏夫，姑娘、妻子或寡妇，年轻的或年老的。他们从摇篮到坟墓都需要它，而不问性的结合如何。我们应当这样为人类营建和安排栖身之所与舒适：既不干扰婚姻，又不使那份舒适依赖于婚姻。当家宅生活的种种产业交由职业化的管理，当房间、成套的房间与整座的房屋可为任何想要它们的一人或数人所得，我们便能独居而不失居家的舒适与寻常的伴侣之谊，我们便能遭逢丧恸而不至在失去心中之爱的同时、又被夺去生活的寻常便利，我们便能在从容与自由中缔结婚姻，而不牵动任何一方经济根基的变更。

===Original===
Married people will always prefer a home together, and can have it; but groups of women or groups of men can also have a home together if they like, or contiguous rooms. And individuals even could have a house to themselves, without having, also, the business of a home upon their shoulders.

===Chinese===
已婚的人将永远宁愿共有一个家宅，也能够有；但成群的女人或成群的男人，只要愿意，同样可以共有一个家宅，或者毗连的房间。甚至个人也可以独得一座房屋，而无须同时把一座家宅的操持压上自己的肩头。

===Original===
Take the kitchens out of the houses, and you leave rooms which are open to any form of arrangement and extension; and the occupancy of them does not mean "housekeeping." In such living, personal character and taste would flower as never before; the home of each individual would be at last a true personal expression; and the union of individuals in marriage would not compel the jumbling together of all the external machinery of their lives—a process in which much of the delicacy and freshness of love, to say nothing of the power of mutual rest and refreshment, is constantly lost. The sense of lifelong freedom and self-respect and of the peace and permanence of one's own home will do much to purify and uplift the personal relations of life, and more to strengthen and extend the social relations. The individual will learn to feel himself an integral part of the social structure, in close, direct, permanent connection with the needs and uses of society.

===Chinese===
把厨房从房屋里取出去，你便留下了可以向任何形式的安排与扩展敞开的房间；而居用这些房间，并不意味着「操持家务」。在这样的生活里，个人的品格与趣味将开出前所未有的花朵；每个人的家宅终将成为一种真正的个人表达；而个人在婚姻中的结合，也将不必强使双方生活中全部外在的机器胡乱挤作一堆——在这一过程里，爱的多少精微与清新，更不必说彼此休憩与恢复的效力，正不断地丧失。终生自由与自尊之感、自己家宅的安宁与恒久之感，将大大有助于净化并提升生活中的种种个人关系，而更助于加强并扩展种种社会关系。个人将学会感到自己是社会构造的一个有机部分，与社会的需要和用途有着贴近、直接而恒久的联系。

===Original===
This is especially needed for women, who are generally considered, and who consider themselves, mere fractions of families, and incapable of any wholesome life of their own. The knowledge that peace and comfort may be theirs for life, even if they do not marry—and may be still theirs for life, even if they do—will develop a serenity and strength in women most beneficial to them and to the world. It is a glaring proof of the insufficient and irritating character of our existing form of marriage that women must be forced to it by the need of food and clothes, and men by the need of cooks and housekeepers. We are absurdly afraid that, if men or women can meet these needs of life by other means, they will cheerfully renounce the marriage relation. And yet we sing adoringly of the power of love!

===Chinese===
这于女人尤为需要：人们普遍把她们、她们也把自己仅仅看作家庭的一些碎块，看作不能过任何属于自己的健康生活的人。倘若知道安宁与舒适可以终身归于她们——即使不结婚；也仍可以终身归于她们——即使结了婚——这种认识将在女人身上发展出一种沉静与力量，于她们、于世界都大有裨益。我们的现行婚姻形式，其不充分而恼人的性质有一桩昭彰的明证：女人必得靠衣食之需被逼入婚姻，男人必得靠对厨师和女管家的需要被逼入婚姻。我们荒唐地害怕：倘若男人或女人能够靠别的手段满足这些生活的需要，他们便会欣然弃绝婚姻关系。而我们却还在满怀崇敬地歌颂爱的力量！

===Original===
In reality, we may hope that the most valuable effect of this change in the basis of living will be the cleansing of love and marriage from this base admixture of pecuniary interest and creature comfort, and that men and women, eternally drawn together by the deepest force in nature, will be able at last to meet on a plane of pure and perfect love. We shame our own ideals, our deepest instincts, our highest knowledge, by this gross assumption that the noblest race on earth will not mate, or, at least, not mate monogamously, unless bought and bribed through the common animal necessities of food and shelter, and chained by law and custom.

===Chinese===
在现实中，我们可以期望：生存根基上这一变化最可贵的成效，将是把爱与婚姻从金钱利益与肉体安逸这卑下的掺和里涤净；而男人与女人——被自然中最深沉的力量永恒地牵到一处——终将能够在纯粹而圆满的爱的层面上相见。我们凭着这样一个粗鄙的假定，羞辱了我们自己的理想、我们最深的本能、我们最高的知识：大地上最高贵的种族将不肯婚配，或者至少不肯一夫一妻地婚配，除非靠食物与栖身这些寻常的动物必需来收买他们、贿赂他们，再用法律与习俗把他们锁住。

===Original===
The depth and purity and permanence of the marriage relation rest on the necessity for the prolonged care of children by both parents—a law of racial development which we can never escape. When parents are less occupied in getting food and cooking it, in getting furniture and dusting it, they may find time to give new thought and new effort to the care of their children. The necessities of the child are far deeper than for bread and bed: those are his mere racial needs, held in common with all his kind. What he needs far more and receives far less is the companionship, the association, the personal touch, of his father and mother. When the common labors of life are removed from the home, we shall have the time, and perhaps the inclination, to make the personal acquaintance of our children. They will seem to us not so much creatures to be waited on as people to be understood. As the civil and military protection of society has long since superseded the tooth-and-claw defence of the fierce parent, without in the least endangering the truth and intensity of the family relation, so the economic provision of society will in time supersede the bringing home of prey by the parent, without evil effects to the love or prosperity of the family. These primitive needs and primitive methods of meeting them are unquestionably at the base of the family relation; but we have long passed them by, and the ties between parent and child are not weakened, but strengthened, by the change.

===Chinese===
婚姻关系的深度、纯度与恒久，系于双亲长期照料孩童的必要——这是一条我们永远逃不开的种族发展法则。当父母不那么忙于弄到食物并烹煮它、忙于弄到家具并掸拭它的时候，他们或许便能找出时间，把新的心思与新的努力给予孩子的照料。孩子的需要，远深于面包与床铺：那不过是他的种族需要，与他的一切同类所共有。他远更需要、又远更少得到的，是父亲与母亲的相伴、交往与亲身的接触。当生活共同的劳作从家宅里移出去之后，我们将有时间、或许也有心愿，去亲自结识我们的孩子。他们之于我们，将不再那么是等候伺候的对象，而是有待理解的人。正如社会的民政与军事保护早已取代了凶猛家长的以牙与爪相卫，却丝毫无损于家庭关系的真实与强度，社会的经济供给也终将取代家长把猎物带回家里的做法，而不给家庭的爱或兴旺带来恶果。这些原始的需要与满足它们的原始方法，确乎位于家庭关系的根基处；但我们早已把它们抛在身后，而亲子之间的纽带并不因这一变化而削弱，反而加强了。

===Original===
The more we grow away from these basic conditions, the more fully we realize the deeper and higher forms of relation which are the strength and the delight of human life. Full and permanent provision for individual life and comfort will not cut off the forces that draw men and women together or hold children to their parents; but it will purify and intensify these relations to a degree which we can somewhat foretell by observing the effect of such changes as are already accomplished in this direction. And, in freeing the individual, old and young, from enforced association on family lines, and allowing this emergence into free association on social lines, we shall healthfully assist the development of true social intercourse.

===Chinese===
我们愈是从这些基本条件下长出去，便愈充分地实现那些更深、更高的关系形式——它们是人生的力量与欢愉。对个人生活与舒适的充分而恒久的供给，不会切断把男人与女人牵到一起、把孩子系于父母的力量；它将净化这些关系并使之更加深切，其深切的程度，我们多少可以借观察这一方向上已成的变化之效来预卜。而且，把个人——无论老幼——从家庭线路上的强制交往里解放出来，容许他们涌现到社会线路上的自由交往中去，我们将健康地助成真正社会交往的发展。

===Original===
The present economic basis of family life holds our friendly and familiar intercourse in narrow grooves. Such visiting and mingling as is possible to us is between families rather than between individuals; and the growing specialization of individuals renders it increasingly unlikely that all the members of a given family shall please a given visitor or he please them. This, on our present basis, either checks the intercourse or painfully strains the family relation. The change of economic relation in families from a sex-basis to a social basis will make possible wide individual intercourse without this accompanying strain on the family ties.

===Chinese===
家庭生活目前的经济基础，把我们友好而亲熟的交往拘在狭窄的辙槽里。我们所能有的那种互访与交融，是家庭与家庭之间的，而非个人与个人之间的；而个人日益增长的专门化，又使一个既定家庭的全体成员都投合某位访客、或访客投合他们的可能性愈发渺茫。在我们目前的基础上，这要么遏制交往，要么痛苦地绷紧家庭关系。家庭中的经济关系从性的基础改到社会的基础，将使宽广的个人交往成为可能，而不再伴随这种对家庭纽带的绷紧。

===Original===
This outgoing impulse among members of families, their growing desire for general and personal social intercourse, has been considered as a mere thirst for amusement, and deprecated by the moralist. He has so far maintained that the highest form of association was association with one's own family, and that a desire for a wider and more fluent relationship was distinctly unworthy. "He is a good family man," we say admiringly of him who asks only for his newspaper and slippers in the evening; and for the woman who dares admit that she wishes further society than that of her husband we have but one name. With the children, too, our constant effort is to "keep the boys at home," to "make home attractive," so that our ancient ideal, the patriarchal ideal, of a world of families and nothing else, may be maintained.

===Chinese===
家庭成员中间这种向外走的冲动、他们对一般与个人社会交往日增的渴望，一向只被看作一种寻欢的渴，遭道德家贬抑。他至今还在主张：交往的最高形式是与自己家庭的交往，而渴望更宽广、更流畅的关系，分明是不配称许的。「他是个顾家的好男人」——对晚间只消一份报纸、一双拖鞋便心满意足的人，我们如此赞赏地说；而对那个敢于承认自己还渴望丈夫之外的社交的女人，我们便只有一个名目给她。对待孩子，我们不变的苦心也是「把男孩们留在家里」、「把家宅弄得诱人」，好让我们那古老的理想——父权制的理想——一个只有家庭、别无他物的世界——得以维系。

===Original===
But this is a world of persons as well as of families. We are persons as soon as we are born, though born into families. We are persons when we step out of families, and persons still, even when we step into new families of our own. As persons, we need more and more, in each generation, to associate with other persons. It is most interesting to watch this need making itself felt, and getting itself supplied, by fair means or foul, through all these stupid centuries. In our besotted exaggeration of the sex-relation, we have crudely supposed that a wish for wider human relationship was a wish for wider sex-relationship, and was therefore to be discouraged, as in Spain it was held unwise to teach women to write, lest they become better able to communicate with their lovers, and so shake the foundations of society.

===Chinese===
然而这是一个属于人的世界，一如它是一个属于家庭的世界。我们一降生便是人，尽管生在家庭里。我们走出家庭时是人，而即便走进自己的新家庭，也仍然是人。作为人，我们一代比一代更需要与别的人交往。看着这一需要在所有这些愚钝的世纪里让自己被感觉到、又用正当或不正当的手段让自己得到满足，是最有意思的事。在我们对性关系那痴醉的夸大里，我们粗糙地以为：对更宽广的人际关系的愿望，便是对更宽广的性关系的愿望，因而应予劝阻——正如在西班牙，人们曾认为教女人写字是不智之举，唯恐她们因此更便于同情人鱼雁往来，从而动摇社会的根基。

===Original===
But, when our sex-relation is made pure and orderly by the economic independence of women, when sex-attraction is no longer a consuming fever, forever convulsing the social surface, under all its bars and chains, we shall not be content to sit down forever with half a dozen blood relations for our whole social arena. We shall need each other more, not less, and shall recognize that social need of one another as the highest faculty of this the highest race on earth.

===Chinese===
然而，当我们的性关系因女人的经济独立（economic independence）而变得纯净而有序，当性吸引（sex-attraction）不再是一场蚀骨的热病——在那一切栅栏与锁链之下永远地痉挛着社会的表层——我们便不会满足于永远守着六七个血亲，权当整个社交的天地。我们将彼此更需要，而非更少，并将把这种对彼此的社会需要，认作大地上这最高贵的种族的最高官能。

===Original===
The force which draws friends together is a higher one than that which draws the sexes together—higher in the sense of belonging to a later race-development. "Passing the love of women" is no unmeaning phrase. Children need one another: young people need one another. Middle-aged people need one another: old people need one another. We all need one another, much and often. Just as every human creature needs a place to be alone in, a sacred, private "home" of his own, so all human creatures need a place to be together in, from the two who can show each other their souls uninterruptedly, to the largest throng that can throb and stir in unison.

===Chinese===
把朋友牵到一起的力量，高于把两性牵到一起的力量——其所以为高，在于它属于更晚近的种族发展（race-development）。「过于妇女的爱情」（passing the love of women）绝非一句无意义的空话。孩子需要彼此：年轻人需要彼此。中年人需要彼此：老年人需要彼此。我们全都需要彼此，多多地、常常地。正如每一个人都需要一个独处之所、一座属于他自己的神圣而私密的「家宅」，一切人也都需要一个共处之所——从那能够不间断地向彼此敞示灵魂的两个人，直到那能够一同悸动、一同奋起的最大的人群。

===Original===
Humanity means being together, and our unutterably outgrown way of living keeps us apart. How many people, if they dare face the fact, have often hopelessly longed for some better way of seeing their friends, their own true friends, relatives by soul, if not by body!

===Chinese===
人之所以为人，便意味着共处一堂；而我们那无可言喻地过了时的生活方式，却把我们隔开。多少人——倘若他们敢于直面这一事实——曾时常无望地渴盼某种更好的方式，去见他们的朋友、他们真正的朋友——那些纵非血肉之亲、也是灵魂之亲的人！

===Original===
Acting always under the heated misconceptions of our oversexed minds, we have pictured mankind as a race of beasts whose only desire to be together was based on one great, overworked passion, and who were only kept from universal orgies of promiscuity by being confined in homes. This is not true. It is not true even now in our oversexed condition. It will be still less true when we are released from the artificial pressure of the sexuo-economic relation and grow natural again.

===Chinese===
我们始终在过度性化（oversexed）的心灵那发热的误解之下行事，把人类描画成一个兽的种族：他们想共处的唯一欲望，系于那一桩被驱策过度的盛大激情；他们只因被锁在各自的家宅里，才没有酿成遍及全体的杂交放纵。这不是实情。即便在我们现在这过度性化的状态下，这也不是实情。当我们从性-经济关系的人为压力下解放出来、重新长得自然时，它就更不将是实情了。

===Original===
Men, women, and children need freedom to mingle on a human basis; and that means to mingle in their daily lives and occupations, not to go laboriously to see each other, with no common purpose. We all know the pleasant acquaintance and deep friendship that springs up when people are thrown together naturally, at school, at college, on shipboard, in the cars, in a camping trip, in business. The social need of one another rests at bottom on a common, functional development; and the common, functional service is its natural opportunity.

===Chinese===
男人、女人和孩子，都需要一种在人的层面上彼此交融的自由；而这就意味着：在他们的日常生活与职业中交融，而不是费劲地专程去彼此探望，却无半点共同的目的。那种愉快的相识与深厚的友谊，我们全都知道——当人们被自然地聚到一处时，它便萌生起来：在学校，在大学，在船上，在车厢里，在露营的旅途中，在共事之中。对彼此的社会需要，归根结底立足于共同的机能的发展；而共同的机能性服务，便是它的天然机会。

===Original===
The reason why friendship means more to men than to women, and why they associate so much more easily and freely, is that they are further developed in race-functions, and that they *work together*. In the natural association of common effort and common relaxation is the true opening for human companionship. Just to put a number of human beings in the same room, to relate their bodies as to cubic space, does not relate their souls. Our present methods of association, especially for women, are most unsatisfactory. They arise, and go to "call" on one another. They solemnly "return" these calls. They prepare much food, and invite many people to come and eat it; or some dance, music, or entertainment is made the temporary ground of union. But these people do not really meet one another. They pass whole lifetimes in going through the steps of these elaborate games, and never become acquainted. There is a constant thirst among us for fuller and truer social intercourse; but our social machinery provides no means for quenching it.

===Chinese===
友谊于男人之所以比于女人意味更多、他们之所以远更容易更自由地交往，是因为他们在种族功能（race-functions）上发展得更远，因为他们*一同劳作*。在共同出力与共同放松的自然交往里，才有人间伴侣之谊真正的开口。单是把若干人放进同一个房间，让他们的身体按立方容积发生关系，并不能让他们的灵魂发生关系。我们现行的交往方式，尤其是女人的，最不能令人满意。她们起身，去彼此「拜客」（call）。她们郑重其事地「回拜」。她们备下许多食物，请许多客人来吃掉它；不然就以某场舞会、某种音乐或余兴，充当一时的联合基点。可是这些人并不真正相会。他们终生走完这些繁缛游戏的种种步骤，却从不相识。我们中间始终有一种对更充分、更真实的社会交往的渴；可我们的社会机器却不提供任何解渴的手段。

===Original===
Men have satisfied this desire in large measure; but between women, or between men and women, it is yet far from accomplishment. Men meet one another freely in their work, while women work alone. But the difference is sharpest in their play. "Girls don't have any fun!" say boys, scornfully; and they don't have very much. What they do have must come, like their bread and butter, on lines of sex. Some man must give them what amusement they have, as he must give them everything else. Men have filled the world with games and sports, from the noble contests of the Olympic plain to the brain and body training sports of today, good, bad, and indifferent. Through all the ages the men have played; and the women have looked on, when they were asked. Even the amusing occupation of seeing other people do things was denied them, unless they were invited by the real participants. The "queen of the ballroom" is but a wallflower, unless she is asked to dance by the real king.

===Chinese===
男人已在很大程度上满足了这一欲望；但在女人与女人之间、或男人与女人之间，它还远未实现。男人在工作中自由相见，女人却独自劳作。而差别最尖锐之处，在于他们的玩乐。「女孩们没有玩乐！」男孩们轻蔑地说；她们确实没有多少。她们仅有的那一点，也必须像她们的黄油面包一样，循着性的线路而来。她们的那点消遣，必得由某个男人赐予，正如其余的一切也都必得由他赐予。男人用种种游戏与运动填满了世界——从奥林匹克平原上高贵的竞逐，直到今日锻炼头脑与身体的种种运动，好的、坏的、平平的。历尽一切时代，男人一直在玩耍；女人只是受请的时候，才在旁观看。连看别人做事这桩有趣的消遣，也不给他们，除非真正的参与者发出邀请。「舞会的女王」若非真正的王邀她起舞，也不过是一朵「壁花」（wallflower）。

===Original===
Even today, when athletics are fast opening to women, when tennis and golf and all the rest are possible to them, the two sexes are far from even in chances to play. To want a good time is not the same thing as to want the society of the other sex, and to make a girl's desire for a good time hang so largely on her power of sex-attraction is another of the grievous strains we put upon that faculty. That people want to see each other is construed by us to mean that "he" wants to see "her," and "she" wants to see "him." The fun and pleasure of the world are so interwound with the sex-dependence of women upon men that women are forced to court "attentions," when not really desirous of anything but amusement; and, as we force the association of the sexes on this plane, so we restrict it on a more wholesome one.

===Chinese===
即便在今天，当种种体育运动正向女人迅速敞开、当网球、高尔夫以及其余种种她们都已可从事时，两性在玩乐的机会上仍远非均等。想要一段快活时光，与想要异性的社交并不是一回事；而使一个姑娘对快活时光的渴望，如此沉重地悬在她的性吸引之力上，又是我们加诸那种官能的又一重痛楚的绷扭。人们想彼此相见，在我们这里却被解释成「他」想见「她」、「她」想见「他」。世间的欢乐与消遣，同女人对男人的性依附（sex-dependence）缠绕得如此之深，以致女人们纵然真心只求娱乐、别无他图，也被迫去乞求「殷勤」（attentions）；而且，我们既在这一层面上强使两性交往，便也在一个更健全的层面上限制这交往。

===Original===
Even our little children in their play are carefully trained to accentuate sex; and a line of conduct for boys, differing from that for girls, is constantly insisted upon long before either would think of a necessity for such difference. Girls and boys, as they associate, are so commented on and teased as to destroy all wholesome friendliness, and induce a premature sex-consciousness. Young men and women are allowed to associate more or less freely, but always on a strictly sex-basis, friendship between man and woman being a common laughingstock. Every healthy boy and girl resents this, and tries to hold free, natural relation; but such social pressure is hard to resist. She may have as many "beaux" as she can compass, he may "pay attention" to as many girls as he pleases; but that is their only way to meet.

===Chinese===
连我们的小孩子，在玩耍中也受到精心的调教，教他们把性别凸显出来；男孩子的一套行为规矩，有别于女孩子的一套，早在任何一方想到需要这种分别之前许久，便被不断地强行灌输。姑娘们与小伙子们只要有所交往，便遭到那样的议论与取笑，以致一切健全的友谊都被毁掉，一种早熟的性意识被催生出来。青年男女获准多少自由地交往，却永远严格地立在性的基础上——男人与女人之间的友谊是一个人尽可讥的笑柄。每一个健康的男孩女孩都对此心怀愤懑，都试图保持自由而自然的关系；但这样的社会压力难以抗拒。她可以尽量多地收罗她的「追求者」（beaux），他可以随意向多少姑娘「献殷勤」；可除此之外，他们再无别的相见之道。

===Original===
The general discontinuance of all friendly visiting, upon the engagement of either party, proves the nature of the bond. Having chosen the girl he is to marry, why care to call upon any others? having chosen the man she is to marry, why receive attention from any others? these "calls" and "attentions" being all in the nature of tentative preliminaries to possible matrimony. And, after marriage, the wife is never supposed to wish to see any other man than her husband, or the husband any other woman than his wife. In some countries, we vary this arrangement by increasing the social freedom of married people; but the custom is accompanied by a commensurate lack of freedom before marriage, which causes questionable results, both in married life and in social life. In the higher classes of society there is always more freedom of social intercourse between the sexes after marriage; but, speaking generally of America, there is very little natural and serious acquaintance between men and women after the period of pre-matrimonial visiting.

===Chinese===
一经订婚，双方便普遍中止一切友好的互访——这一通例恰恰证明了这条纽带的性质。既已选定要娶的姑娘，何必再去拜访别的姑娘？既已选定要嫁的男人，何必再接受别人的殷勤？——这些「拜客」与「殷勤」，本就都是通往可能之婚姻的试探性前奏。而婚后的妻子，从不被指望愿见丈夫以外的任何男人；丈夫也同样不被指望愿见妻子以外的任何女人。在某些国家，我们把这一安排变通了变通，给已婚者更多的社交自由；但这一习俗伴随着婚前相应的自由的匮乏，从而在婚姻生活与社会生活里都引出可疑的结果。在上流社会中，婚后两性间的社交往来总归更多些；但就美国的一般情形而论，在婚前互访的时期过后，男女之间便再很少有自然而郑重的相识了。

===Original===
Even the friendship which may have existed between husband and wife before marriage is often destroyed by that relation and its economic complications. They have not time to talk about things as they used: they are too near together, and too deeply involved in the industrial and financial concern of their new business. This works steadily against the development of higher and purer relations between men and women, and tends to keep them forever to the one primitive bond of sex-union.

===Chinese===
甚至夫妻婚前可能已有的那份友谊，也常常被婚姻关系本身及其经济上的种种纠葛毁掉。他们不再有时间像从前那样谈论种种事情：他们彼此挨得太近，又太深地陷在他们这门新生意的产业与财务的操劳里。这就持续地妨害着男人与女人之间更高、更纯的关系的发展，而趋向于把他们永远锁死在性结合（sex-union）这唯一的原始纽带上。

===Original===
A young man goes to a city to live and work. He needs the society of women as well as of men. Formerly he had his mother, his sisters, and his sisters' friends, his schoolmates. Now he must face our constrained social conditions. He may visit two kinds of women—those whom we call "good," and those whom we call "bad." (This classification rests on but one moral quality, and that a sexual one.) He naturally prefers the good. The good are divided, again, into two kinds—married and single. If he visit a married woman frequently, it is remarked upon: it becomes unpleasant, he does not do it. If he visit an unmarried woman frequently, it is also remarked upon; and he is considered to have "intentions." His best alternative is to visit a number of unmarried women, and distribute his attentions so cautiously that no one can claim them as personal.

===Chinese===
一个青年男子到一座城市去生活、去工作。他需要男人的社交，也需要女人的社交。从前他有他的母亲、他的姐妹们、他姐妹的朋友、他的同学们。如今他必须面对我们这局促的社交境况。他可以拜访两种女人——我们唤作「好」的那些，和我们唤作「坏」的那些。（这一分类只立在一种道德品质上，而且是一种性方面的品质。）他自然更喜欢好的。好的又分为两种——已婚的与未婚的。他若频繁拜访一位已婚女人，会惹人物议：事情变得不愉快，他便不再去了。他若频繁拜访一位未婚女人，同样惹人物议；而且会被认为怀有「意图」。他最好的出路，是拜访相当一批未婚女子，把他的殷勤分派得如此谨慎，以至于谁也无法把它们认作专属自己的。

===Original===
Here he enters on the first phase of our sexuo-economic relation: he cannot even visit girls freely without paying for it. Simply to see the girl by calling on her in the family circle is hardly what either wants of the other. One does not meet half a dozen people of various ages and of both sexes as one meets a friend alone. To seek to see her alone is an "attention." To "take her out" costs money, and he cheerfully pays it. But he cannot do this too often, or he will become involved in what is naturally considered a "serious" affair; and every step of the acquaintance is watched and commented upon from a sexual point of view.

===Chinese===
在这里，他踏进了我们性-经济关系的第一阶段：他连自由地造访姑娘们，也不能不为之破费。只靠到她家的家庭圈子里拜会她而见到她，很难说是任何一方对另一方所想要的。在半打年龄各殊、男女皆有的人们中间会面，与单独会见一位朋友，根本不是一回事。想要单独见她，便是一种「殷勤」。「带她出去」是要花钱的，而他高高兴兴地花。但他又不能做得太频，否则便会卷入一件自然会被视为「正经」大事的事情里；而这番相识的每一步，都被人从性的视点加以注视、加以评说。

===Original===
There is no natural, simple medium of social intercourse between men and women. The young man can but learn that his popularity depends largely on his pocketbook. The money that he might be saving for marriage is wasted on these miscellaneous preliminaries. As he sees what women like and how much it costs to please them, his hope of marriage recedes farther and farther. The period during which he must live as an individual grows longer; and he becomes accustomed to superficial acquaintance with many women, on the shallowest side of life, with no opportunity for genuine association and true friendship. What wonder that the other kind of woman, who also costs money, it is true, but who does not involve permanent obligation, has come to be so steady a factor in our social life? The sexuo-economic relation promotes vice in more ways than one.

===Chinese===
男女之间，不存在任何自然而单纯的社会交往媒介。这青年男子便只能认识到：他的人缘，很大程度上系于他的钱袋。他本可以为结婚而攒下的钱，都虚掷在这些五花八门的前奏上。随着他看清女人们喜欢什么、取悦她们要花多少钱，他对婚姻的希望便愈退愈远。他必须作为个人生活的时期愈拖愈长；他也惯于同许多女人保持浮泛的相识——停在人生最浅薄一面上的相识——而没有真正交往与真挚友谊的机会。无怪乎那另一种女人——她也确实要花钱，却不牵累永久的义务——竟成了我们社交生活中如此稳定的一个因子。性-经济关系以不止一种方式助长着恶行。

===Original===
The economic independence of woman will change all these conditions as naturally and inevitably as her dependence has introduced them. In her specialization in industry, she will develop more personality and less sexuality; and this will lower the pressure on this one relation in both women and men. And, in our social intercourse, the new character and new method of living will allow of broad and beautiful developments in human association. As the private home becomes a private home indeed, and no longer the woman's social and industrial horizon; as the workshops of the world—woman's sphere as well as man's—become homelike and beautiful under her influence; and as men and women move freely together in the exercise of common racial functions—we shall have new channels for the flow of human life.

===Chinese===
女人的经济独立，将如她的依附当初把这些境况带进来时一样自然、一样必然地把它们统统改变。在她在产业中的专门化里，她将发展出更多的人格、更少的性色彩；而这将降低这一重关系压在女人与男人身上的压力。而在我们的社会交往中，新的品格与新的生活方式，将容许人类的交往得到宽广而美妙的发展。当私人的家宅成为真正私人的家宅、不再仅仅是女人的社交与产业的地平线；当世界的种种工场——它们既是男人的天地，也是女人的天地——在她影响之下变得如家宅般亲切而美丽；当男人与女人在共同种族功能的行使中自由地并肩而行——我们将有人类生命奔流的新的河道。

===Original===
We shall not move from the isolated home to the sordid shop and back again, in a world torn and dissevered by the selfish production of one sex and the selfish consumption of the other; but we shall live in a world of men and women humanly related, as well as sexually related, working together, as they were meant to do, for the common good of all. The home will be no longer an economic entity, with its cumbrous industrial machinery huddled vulgarly behind it, but a peaceful and permanent expression of personal life as withdrawn from social contact; and that social contact will be provided for by the many common meeting-places necessitated by the organization of domestic industries.

===Chinese===
我们将不再从孤立的家宅挪到肮脏的店铺、再从店铺挪回来，活在一个被一种性别的自私生产与另一种性别的自私消费撕扯离析的世界里；我们将生活在一个这样的世界里：其间的男人与女人，既有性的关联，也有人的关联，一同劳作——如他们本该做的那样——为着一切人的共同之善。家宅将不再是一个经济实体，身后粗俗地挤着它那笨重的产业机器，而将成为个人生活退离社交接触之后安宁而恒久的表达；而那种社交接触，则将由家内产业的组织所必需的许多公共聚会场所来供给。

===Original===
The assembling-room is as deep a need of human life as the retiring-room—not some ballroom or theatre, to which one must be invited of set purpose, but great common libraries and parlors, baths and gymnasia, workrooms and playrooms, to which both sexes have the same access for the same needs, and where they may mingle freely in common human expression. The kind of buildings essential to the carrying out of the organization of home industry will provide such places. There will be the separate rooms for individuals and the separate houses for families; but there will be, also, the common rooms for all. These must include a place for the children, planned and built for the happy occupancy of many children for many years—a home such as no children have ever had. This, as well as rooms everywhere for young people and old people, in which they can be together as naturally as they can be alone, without effort, question, or remark.

===Chinese===
聚合之室（assembling-room）与退隐之室（retiring-room），同是人类生活同样深切的需要——不是那种必须被专门邀请才能去的舞厅或剧院，而是宏大的公共图书馆与客厅、浴场与体育馆、作业室与游艺室：两性为着同样的需要有同样的门径进入，并可以在共同的人性表达里自由交融。为实行家内产业的组织所必需的那一类建筑，自会提供这样的场所。那里将有供个人的单独房间、供家庭的单独屋舍；但也将有供所有人的公共房间。这些场所还须包括一个属于孩子们的地方——为许多孩子许多年的快乐居用而规划、而建造——一个从来没有任何孩子拥有过的家宅。同样，还须处处都有为年轻人与老年人备下的房间，让他们能够像独处一样自然地共处，无须费力、无须盘问、也无须旁人置喙。

===Original===
Such an environment would allow of free association among us, on lines of common interest; and, in its natural, easy flow, we should develop far higher qualities than are brought out by the uneasy struggles of our present "society" to see each other without wanting to. It would make an enormous difference to woman's power of choosing the right man. Cut off from the purchasing power which is now his easiest way to compass his desires, freely seen and known in his daily work and amusements, a woman could know and judge a man as she is wholly unable to do now. Her personality developed by a free and useful life, clearheaded and open-eyed—a woman still, but a personality as well as a woman—the girl trained to economic independence, and associating freely with young men in their common work and play, would learn a new estimate of what constitutes noble manhood.

===Chinese===
这样一个环境，将容许我们循着共同兴趣的线路自由交往；而在它自然、从容的流转里，我们将发展出远为高尚的品质——远比我们现今那个「社交界」里彼此并不想见、却还勉强挣扎着互相张望的不安苦斗所逼出来的品质高尚。它将使女人挑选合意男人的眼力发生极大的改观。当那购买力——如今他实现欲望最便捷的路——被斩断，当他在日常的工作与消遣中被自由地看见、自由地认识，一个女人便能了解并判断一个男人，如她今日全然做不到的那样。她的人格既经自由而有用的生活发展起来，头脑清明、目光敞亮——仍是一个女人，但不只是女人，还是一个人格——这个受教养走向经济独立的姑娘，在共同的工作与游戏中与青年男子自由交往，将学会一副新的眼光，去掂量什么才构成高贵的男性品格。

===Original===
The young man, no longer able to cover all his shortcomings with a dress-coat, and to obtain absolution for every offence by the simple penance of paying for it, unable really to do much that was wrong for lack of the old opportunity and the old incentive, constantly helped and inspired by the friendly presence of honest and earnest womanhood, would have all the force of natural law to lift him up instead of pulling him heavily downward, as it does now.

===Chinese===
这青年男子，再不能凭一件燕尾服遮盖他所有的短处，再不能靠「为之付账」这一简单的苦行赎免每一桩过犯；因失去了旧日的机会与旧日的诱因，也真做不成多少错事；又因诚笃而恳挚的女性风范那友善的在场，而时时受到扶助与感发——那么，自然律的全部力量将把他向上托举，而不是像现在这样把他沉沉地往下拖拽。

===Original===
With the pressure of our over-developed sex-instinct lifted off the world, born clean and strong, of noble-hearted, noble-minded, noble-bodied mothers, trained in the large wisdom of the new motherhood, and living freely in daily association with the best womanhood, a new kind of man can and will grow on earth. What this will mean to the race in power and peace and happiness no eye can foresee. But this much we can see:—that our once useful sexuo-economic relation is being outgrown, that it now produces many evil phenomena, and that its displacement by the economic freedom of woman will of itself set free new forces, to develop in us, by their natural working, the very virtues for which we have striven and agonized so long.

===Chinese===
当我们那过度发展的性本能的重压从世界上移开，一种新式的人便能够、也必将生长于大地上——他由心胸高尚、心智高尚、体魄高尚的母亲们生得洁净而强壮，受教于新母职的宏大智慧，又在日常交往中与最美好的女性风范自由共处。这对于种族在力量、和平与幸福上将意味着什么，没有一只眼睛能够预见。但这一点我们看得见：——我们那曾一度有用的性-经济关系正在被长过去；它如今孳生着许多恶象；而它一旦为女人的经济自由所取代，其本身就将释放出新的力量，凭其自然的作用，在我们身上发展出我们为之苦苦力争、苦苦煎熬了如此之久的那种种德性本身。

===Original===
This change is not a thing to prophesy and plead for. It is a change already instituted, and gaining ground among us these many years with marvellous rapidity. Neither men nor women wish the change. Neither men nor women have sought it. But the same great force of social evolution which brought us into the old relation—to our great sorrow and pain—is bringing us out, with equal difficulty and distress. The time has come when it is better for the world that women be economically independent, and therefore they are becoming so.

===Chinese===
这一变化不是一件需要预言与恳求的事。它是一场业已开启的变化，多年来正以惊人的速度在我们中间节节进占。男人们不希望这变化，女人们也不希望。男人们没有寻求它，女人们也没有。但正是那一股把我们带进旧关系里——带给我们巨大的悲伤与痛苦——的伟大的社会演化之力，如今又带着同样的艰难与困苦，把我们带了出来。女人经济独立于世界更为有益的时代已经到来，因此她们正在变得如此。

===Original===
It is worth while for us to consider the case fully and fairly, that we may see what it is that is happening to us, and welcome with open arms the happiest change in human condition that ever came into the world. To free an entire half of humanity from an artificial position; to release vast natural forces from a strained and clumsy combination, and set them free to work smoothly and easily as they were intended to work; to introduce conditions that will change humanity from within, making for better motherhood and fatherhood, better babyhood and childhood, better food, better homes, better society—this is to work for human improvement along natural lines. It means enormous racial advance, and that with great swiftness; for this change does not wait to create new forces, but sets free those already potentially strong, so that humanity will fly up like a released spring. And it is already happening. All we need do is to understand and help.

===Chinese===
我们值得把这件事完整而公允地考虑一番，好教我们看清正在我们身上发生的是什么，并张开双臂欢迎这人间境况中曾有过的最幸福的变化。把整整一半的人类从一个人为的位置上解放出来；把浩大的自然之力从一组绷紧而笨拙的组合里释放出来，任它们顺遂轻易地运转，如它们本来被安排的那样运转；引入从内部改变人类的种种条件，促成更好的母职与父职、更好的婴儿期与童年、更好的食物、更好的家宅、更好的社会——这便是循着自然的路线为人类的改良而工作。它意味着巨大的种族进步，而且是以极大的迅疾；因为这一变化无须等待创造新的力量，它只是把那些潜在地已经强大的力量释放出来，于是人类将像一根松开的弹簧一般腾跃而起。而它已经在发生了。我们唯一需要做的，就是理解它、帮助它。

## XV / 十五

===Original===
As we learn to see how close is the connection of that which we call the soul with our external conditions, how the moral sense and the behavior of man are modified by the environment, we must of course look for marked results in psychic development arising from so important a condition as our sexuo-economic relation.

===Chinese===
当我们学会看出，我们所称的灵魂与我们的外部条件之间的联系何等紧密，人的道德感与举止如何为环境所改变，那么，对于像我们的性-经济关系（sexuo-economic relation）这般重要的一个条件，我们自然必须在心灵的发展中寻找显著的后果。

===Original===
The relation of the sexes, in whatever form, has always been observed to affect strongly the moral nature of mankind; and this is one reason why we have placed such disproportionate stress upon the special virtues of that relation. The word “moral” in common use means “chaste”; and, in the case of women, the word “virtue” itself simply implies the one virtue of chastity. Large, popular conceptions are never baseless. They are rooted in deep truths, felt rather than seen, and, however false and silly in external interpretation, may be trusted in their general trend. It is not that the virtue of chastity is so much more important to the race than the virtue of honesty, the virtue of courage, the virtues of cheerfulness, of courtesy, of kindness, but that upon the sex-relation in which we live depends so much of the further development and arrangement of our whole moral nature.

===Chinese===
两性之间的关系，无论取何种形态，向来都被观察到强烈地影响着人类的道德本性；这也正是我们何以对那一关系的特殊德性施加了如此不成比例的强调的缘故之一。通行用法里的「道德」一词意指「贞洁」；而在妇女的情形里，「德性」一词本身便只意味着贞洁这一种德性。宏大的、流行的观念从不全无根据。它们植根于深刻的真理之中——被感受到，而非被看到——无论其外在阐释何等虚假愚蠢，其大体走向却可资信赖。这并不是说贞洁之德对种族而言，远比诚实之德、勇敢之德、快活、礼让、仁慈诸德更为重要，而是说：我们置身其中的性关系（sex-relation），实在系着我们整个道德本性此后成长与安排的一大部分。

===Original===
What we call the moral sense is an intellectual recognition of the relative importance of certain acts and their consequences. This appears vaguely and weakly among early savages, and was for long mainly applied to a few clearly defined and arbitrary rites and ceremonies, set rules in a game of priest-and-people. But the habit of associating a sense of worthiness with certain acts by which came praise and profit grew in the childish soul, and the range of moral deeds widened. It has been widening ever since, growing deeper and higher and far more subtle, developing with the other social qualities.

===Chinese===
我们所称的道德感，是对某些行为及其后果之相对重要性的一种理智认识。它在早期野蛮人中间显得模糊而微弱，长久以来主要施用于少数界定分明而又出于专断的仪式与典礼——那是祭司与民众之戏中设定下的规则。可是，把一种可称许之感同某些能带来称赞与实惠的行为联系起来的习惯，在孩童般的灵魂中生长起来，道德行为的范围也随之展宽。此后它一直展宽着，长得更深、更高，也远为精微，与其他种种社会品质一同发展。

===Original===
No human distinction is more absolutely and exclusively social than the moral sense. Ethics is a social science. There is no ethics for the individual. Taken by himself, man is but an animal; and his conduct bears relation only to the needs of the animal﻿—self-preservation and race-preservation. Every virtue, and the power to see and strive for it, is a social quality. The highest virtues are those wherein we best serve the most people, and their development in us keeps pace with the development of society. It is the social relation which calls for our virtues, and which maintains them.

===Chinese===
在人类的一切特质之中，没有比道德感更绝对、更纯粹地属于社会的了。伦理学是一门社会科学。对孤立的个人而言，没有伦理可言。孤身而论，人不过是一头动物；他的行为只关系到动物的需要——自我保存（self-preservation）与种族保存（race-preservation）。每一种德性，连同看出它并为它奋争的能力，都是一种社会品质。最高的德性，是我们借以最好地服务最多数人的那些德性；它们在我们身上的发展，与社会的发展并驾齐驱。正是社会关系在召唤我们的德性，也正是社会关系在维持着它们。

===Original===
A simple instance of this is in the prompt lapse to barbarism of a man cut off from his kind, and forced to live in conditions of savagery. Even a brief and partial change in condition changes conduct at once, as is shown by the behavior of the most pious New Englanders when in mining camps. It is shown, also, by the different scale of virtue in the different classes and industries.

===Chinese===
一个简单的例证是：一个人若与同类隔绝、被迫在野蛮的境况中过活，便会多么迅速地退回野蛮。甚至境况上短暂而局部的改变也会立刻改变行为，最虔诚的新英格兰人在采金营地里的举止便是明证。不同阶层与不同行业中德性标尺的参差，也同样表明了这一点。

===Original===
Every social relation has its ethics; and the general needs of society, as a whole, are the basis of ethics. In every age and race this may be studied, and a clear connection established always between the virtues and vices of a given people and their local conditions. The principal governing condition in the development of ethics is the economic environment. This may seem strange to one accustomed to consider moral laws as not of this world, and to see how often virtue costs its possessor dear. The relative behavior of a given number of people depends, first, upon the existence of those people. Such conduct as should tend to exterminate them would exterminate their ethics. Such conduct as should tend to preserve and increase them is the only conduct of which ethical value can be predicated. Ethics is, therefore, absolutely conditioned upon life and the maintenance thereof. From the lowest and narrowest view which calls an act right or wrong, according to its immediate effects upon one’s present life, to the clear vision of ultimate results which calls a course of conduct right or wrong, according to its final effects upon one’s eternal life, our ethics, small and great, is the science of human conduct measured by its results.

===Chinese===
每一种社会关系都有它的伦理；而社会作为整体的一般需要，便是伦理的基础。在每一个时代、每一个种族中都可以研究这一点，而且总能在一个特定民族的诸德与诸恶同他们的地方境况之间，建立起清晰的联系。伦理发展中起支配作用的主要条件，是经济环境。这对于惯于把道德律看作超乎尘世、又惯见德性如何常常令其拥有者付出高昂代价的人来说，也许显得离奇。一定数目的人，其行为如何，首先取决于这些人的存在。凡趋于使他们灭绝的行为，也会灭绝他们的伦理。唯有趋于保存并增殖他们的行为，才是能够被赋予伦理价值的唯一行为。因此，伦理绝对地以生命及其维持为前提。从最低最窄的观点——依一行为对今生今世的直接效果而断其正误——到对终极结果的清明洞见——依一行止对永生的最终效果而断其正误——我们的伦理，无论大小，都是以结果来衡量人类行为的科学。

===Original===
It is inevitable, then, that in all races we should find those acts whereby men live considered right, and should see a high degree of approval awarded to him who best performs them. In the hunting and fighting period the best hunter and fighter was the best man, praised and honored by his tribe. The virtues cultivated were such as enabled the possessor to hunt and kill most successfully, to maintain himself and be a credit and a help to his friends. Savage virtues are the simple reflection of savage conditions. To be patient and self-controlled was an economic necessity to the hunter: to bear pain and arduous exertion easily was a necessity to the fighter. Therefore, the savage, by precept and example, cultivated these virtues.

===Chinese===
于是，在一切种族中，我们都不可避免地发现：那些人们赖以谋生的行为被视为正当，而履行它们最出色的人则被授予高度的赞许。在狩猎与战斗的时期，最好的猎手与战士便是最好的人，受他的部落称颂敬重。所培育的德性，是那些能使拥有者最成功地猎杀、自立生计、并为亲友增光出力的品质。野蛮人的德性不过是野蛮境况的简单映照。对猎手来说，忍耐与自制是一种经济上的必要；对战士来说，轻易承受疼痛与艰苦的劳作是一种必要。因此，野蛮人以言传身教培育着这些德性。

===Original===
In the long agricultural and military periods we see the same thing. In the peasant the virtues of industry and patience were extolled: it takes industry and patience to raise corn. In the soldier the virtues of courage and obedience were extolled, and in everyone the virtue of faith was the prime requisite of the existing religion. It took a great deal of faith to accept the religions of those times. The importance of faith as a virtue declines as religion grows more intelligible and applicable to life. It requires no effort to believe what you can understand and do. Slowly the industrial era dawned and grew, from the weak, sporadic efforts of the cringing packman and craftsman, the common prey of the dominant fighting class, to our colossal industrial organization, in which the soldier is ruthlessly exploited to some financial interests. With this change in economic conditions has changed the scale of virtues.

===Chinese===
在漫长的农业与军事时期，我们看到的是同一回事。在农民身上，勤勉与忍耐之德受到颂扬：种庄稼原就需要勤勉与忍耐。在士兵身上，勇敢与服从之德受到颂扬；而在每一个人身上，信仰之德都是当时通行宗教的头号要求。要接受那些时代的种种宗教，原需要大量的信仰。信仰作为一种德性，其重要性随着宗教变得越来越可理解、越来越切于人生而递减。凡是你能理解、能践行的东西，相信它无需费力。工业时代缓缓破晓并成长起来——从卑躬屈膝的货郎与工匠（他们是有势力的战斗阶级共同的猎物）那零星微弱的努力，直到我们今日庞大的工业组织；在其中，士兵被无情地榨取，去喂养某些金融利益。随着经济状况的这一变迁，德性的标尺也变了。

===Original===
Physical courage has sunk: obedience, patience, faith, and the rest do not stand as they did. We praise and value today, as always, the virtues whereby we live. Every animal develops the virtues of his conditions: our human distinction is that we add the power of conscious perception and personal volition to the action of natural force. Not only in our own race, but in others, do we call “good” and “bad” those qualities which profit us; and the beasts that we train and use develop, of necessity, the qualities that profit them﻿—as, for instance, in our well-known friend, the dog.

===Chinese===
体力之勇已经跌落：服从、忍耐、信仰以及其余诸德，不再像从前那样受人推重。今天我们称颂并看重的，一如既往，是我们赖以为生的那些德性。每一种动物都发展出它的境况所需要的德性：我们人类的独特之处，在于我们给自然力量的作用加上了自觉认识与个人意志的力量。不但对于我们自己的种类，而且对于别的种类，我们都把那些于我们有益的品质唤作「善」，把无益的唤作「恶」；而我们驯养役使的兽类，也就必然发展出那些于它们有益的品质——例如我们熟识的朋友，狗。

===Original===
The dog is an animal long since cut off from his natural means of support, and depending absolutely on man for food. As a free, wild dog, he was profited by a daring initiative, courage, ferocity. As a tame, slave dog, he is profited by abject submission, by a crawling will-lessness that grovels at a blow, and licks the foot that kicks it. We have quite made over the original dog; and his moral nature, his spirit, shows the change even more than his body. The force which has accomplished this is economic﻿—a change of base in the source of supplies and the processes of obtaining them.

===Chinese===
狗是一种早已同它天然的谋生之资隔绝、全然仰赖人赐食的动物。作为一条自由的野狗，大胆的进取、勇气与凶猛于它有益。作为一条驯顺的奴隶狗，卑屈的臣服于它有益——那种一挨打便匍匐在地、去舔那只踢它的脚的、爬行般的无意志。我们已经把原本的狗彻底改造了；而它的道德本性、它的精神，显示出这场变化的程度，甚于它的身体。完成这场改造的力量是经济性的——供给的来源与获取供给的过程，都换了根基。

===Original===
Let us briefly examine the distinctive virtues of humanity, their order of introduction and development, and see how this one peculiar relation has affected them.

===Chinese===
且让我们简略地考察一下人类特有的诸般德性，考察它们被引入与发展的次第，并看看这一重奇特的关系是怎样影响了它们。

===Original===
The main distinction of human virtue is in what we roughly describe as altruism﻿—“otherness.” To love and serve one another, to care for one another, to feel for and with one another﻿—our racial adjective, “humane,” implies these qualities. The very existence of humanity implies these qualities in some degree, and the development of humanity is commensurate with their development.

===Chinese===
人类德性的主要特异之处，在于我们粗略地描述为利他主义（altruism）的那种东西——「他者性」（otherness）。彼此相爱、彼此服务，彼此照料，彼此感同身受——我们人类这个种族的形容词「仁慈」（humane），便蕴含着这些品质。人类的存在本身，便在某种程度上蕴含着这些品质；而人类的发展，与它们的发展相称。

===Original===
Our one great blunder in studying these things lies in our failure to appreciate the organic necessity of such moral qualities in human life. We have assumed that the practice of these social virtues involved a personal effort and sacrifice, and that there is an irreconcilable contest between the cosmic process of development and the ethical process, as Huxley puts it. Social evolution brings with it the essential qualities of social relation, and these are our much boasted virtues. The natural processes of human intercourse and interrelation develop the qualities without which such intercourse would be impossible; and this development is as orderly, as natural, as “cosmic,” as the processes of organic activity within the individual body. It is as natural for an industrial society to live in peace as for a hunting society to live in war; and this peace is not the result of heroic and self-sacrificing effort on the part of the industrial society; it is the necessity of their condition.

===Chinese===
我们在研究这些事情上的一个大错，在于未能领会这类道德品质在人类生活中的有机必然性。我们一向假定，践行这些社会德性须付出个人的努力与牺牲，并假定——如赫胥黎（Huxley）所说——在发展的宇宙过程（cosmic process）与伦理过程（ethical process）之间，存在着一场不可调和的争斗。社会演化随身带来社会关系的本质品质，而这些正是我们大加夸耀的德性。人类交往与相互联系的自然过程，发展出缺了它们这种交往便无从进行的品质；这一发展之有序、之自然、之「宇宙性」，正与个体身体之内有机活动的过程相同。一个工业社会过和平的日子，同一个狩猎社会过战争的日子一样自然；这和平并非工业社会方面英雄式的、自我牺牲式的努力所致；它是他们境况的必然。

===Original===
The course of evolution in human ethics is marked by a gradual extension of our perception of common good and evil as distinct from our initial perception of individual good and evil. This becomes very keen in the more socialized natures among us, as in the farseeing devotion of statesmanship, patriotism, and philanthropy. Each of these words shows in its construction that the quality described is social﻿—the statesman, one who thinks and works for the State; the patriot, one who loves and labors for his country; the philanthropist, one who loves mankind. All these qualities, in their extreme and in their first beginnings, are a mere recognition of the equal right of the next man, common “fair play” and courtesy; they are but the natural product of social conditions acting on the individual through primal laws of economic necessity. The individual, in the absolute economic isolation of the beast, is profited by pure egoism, and he develops it. The individual, in the increasing economic interdependence of social relation, is profited by altruism; and he develops it.

===Chinese===
人类伦理的演化进程，其标志在于我们对共同善恶的知觉逐渐扩展，有别于我们最初对个人善恶的知觉。这种知觉在我们中间较为社会化的天性里变得极为敏锐，如治国之才、爱国之心与博爱精神那高瞻远瞩的奉献。这几个词的构造本身就表明，它们所描述的品质是社会性的——政治家（statesman），为邦国而思而作的人；爱国者（patriot），爱他的祖国并为祖国劳作的人；博爱者（philanthropist），爱人类的人。所有这些品质，无论在其极致还是在其发端，都不过是对邻人平等权利的承认，不过是寻常的「公平之道」（fair play）与礼数；它们只是社会条件经由经济必然性的原始法则作用于个人的自然产物。处在野兽那种绝对的经济孤立中的个人，因纯粹的利己主义而得利，他便发展出利己主义。处在社会关系日益加深的经济相互依存中的个人，因利他主义而得利，他便发展出利他主义。

===Original===
All our virtues can be so traced and accounted for. The great main stem of them all, what we call “love,” is merely the first condition of social existence. It is cohesion, working among us as the constituent particles of society. Without some attraction to hold us together, we should not be able to hold together; and this attraction, as perceived by our consciousness, we call love. The virtue of obedience consists in the surrender of the individual will, so often necessary to the common good; and it stands highest in military organization, wherein great numbers of men must act together against their personal interests, even to the sacrifice of life, in the service of the community.

===Chinese===
我们的一切德性都可以这样追溯，这样解释。它们全体那根伟大的主茎——我们称作「爱」的东西——不过是社会存在的第一个条件。它是内聚力，在我们这些社会的组成粒子之间发挥作用。没有某种把我们聚在一起的吸引，我们便无从聚在一起；而这种吸引，当它被我们的意识所知觉时，我们便称之为爱。服从之德在于交出个人的意志——这于共同之善往往是必要的；它在军事组织中地位最高，因为在那里面，大批的人必须违逆个人利益而共同行动，直至为共同体献出生命。

===Original===
As we have grown into fuller social life, we have slowly and experimentally, painfully and expensively, discovered what kind of man was the best social factor. The type of a satisfactory member of society today is a man self-controlled, kind, gentle, strong, wise, brave, courteous, cheerful, true. In the Middle Ages, strong, brave, and true would have satisfied the demands of the time. We now require for our common good a larger range of qualities, a more elaborate moral organization. All this is a simple, evolutionary process of social life, and should have involved no more confusion, effort, and pain than any other natural process.

===Chinese===
随着我们长入更充分的社会生活，我们缓慢地、以试错的方式，痛苦地、付出高昂代价地，发现了什么样的人是最好的社会因子。今天，一个令人满意的社会成员的典型，是这样一个人：自制、仁厚、温和、坚强、明智、勇敢、谦恭有礼、快活、真诚。在中世纪，坚强、勇敢、真诚便足以满足那个时代的要求了。如今为了我们的共同之善，我们要求范围更广的品质，一种更精密的道德组织。这一切本是社会生活的一个简单的演化过程，本不该比任何别的自然过程牵涉更多的混乱、劳苦与疼痛。

===Original===
But the moral development of humanity is a most tempestuous and contradictory field of study. Some virtues we have developed in orderly fashion, hardly recognizing that they were virtues, because they came so easily into use. Accuracy and punctuality are qualities which were unknown to the savage, because they were not needed in his business. They have been developed in us, because they were required, and so have been gradually assumed under pressure of economic necessity. Obedience, even in its extreme form of self-sacrifice, has been produced in the soldier; and no quality is more altruistic, more unnatural, or more difficult of adoption by the sturdy individual will. The common, law-abiding citizen does not consider himself a hero; yet he is manifesting a high degree of social virtue, often at great personal sacrifice.

===Chinese===
然而，人类的道德发展却是一块风浪最险、矛盾最深的研究领域。有些德性，我们发展得井然有序，几乎不觉得它们是德性，因为它们来得太顺当。精确与守时，是野蛮人所不知道的品质，因为他的营生里用不着它们。它们在我们身上得到了发展，因为它们被需要，于是便在经济必然性的压力下被逐渐养成。服从，乃至它那自我牺牲的极端形态，已在士兵身上造就出来；再没有哪种品质比它更利他、更违逆自然、更难被顽强的个人意志接受的了。寻常的、守法的公民并不自认为是英雄；然而他正表现出高度的社会德性，而且往往付出巨大的个人牺牲。

===Original===
But in other virtues we have not progressed so smoothly. In the ordinary economic relations of life, and in our sex-relations, we are distinguished by peculiar and injurious qualities. Our condition may be described as consisting of a tenacious survival of qualities which we ought, on every ground of social good, to have long since outgrown; and an incessant struggle between these rudimentary survivals and the normal growth. This it is which has so forcibly assailed our consciousness since its awakening, and which we call the contest between good and evil.
